## ON THE ORIGIN OF COMMON SALT.

...let us speak a little and explain this further, for much depends on this proof and understanding; let mechanics serve us here as an illustration. First, we see in all the trades that weave linen, silk, wool, and hair-cloth, that the longer and more delicate these fibers or threads are, the more capable and strong their work becomes; whereas wool or silk that is too short-haired is useful for nothing but coarse felt. Secondly, we see with the papermakers that once they have sorted their rags and worked each sort under the stamps so that they become delicate and fluffy like down feathers, and then give them the proper "glue-water" <term>Water</term> <note>original: 🜄</note>, they obtain a good and useful paper from each sort. However, if they work the rags too heavily, so that the fibers are shattered and torn apart under the stamps and work themselves into small <term>globules</term> <note>small spherical particles</note>—just like <term>Mercury</term> <note>original: ☿</note> driven up by <term>Fire</term> <note>original: 🜂</note>—then it becomes a brittle and useless paper.

<margin>
Letter T.
</margin>
The same situation applies to metals and minerals. For just as with the papermakers and felt-workers the rags and wool are worked with <term>Water</term> [🜄] and brought to firmness with the gluey <term>Water</term> [🜄], so too the red and white <term>Sulfur</term> <note>original: 🜍</note> of the metals are worked into almost identical metallic threads. This is done by the more delicate <term>Salt</term> <note>original: 🜔; likely referring to a vitriolic salt or metallic essence</note>, which for the first time is physically wrought here below

<margin>
Note Well.
</margin>
(and which serves them in their texture in place of water). These are wrought into their own kind and species, where one is always coarser than the other, as mechanics clearly places before our eyes—especially in the crafts of the blacksmith and the wire-drawer. For the better the two <term>Sulfurs</term> <note>original: "🜍ra," referring to the internal and external sulfurs of alchemy</note> in the metals have been wrought, the better and more durably they can be worked and stretched. We see this especially and undeniably in gold and silver, both in their very thin and delicate leaves, as well as in gilded and silver wire. For, my God, what a small quantity of these can be extended into an almost immeasurable and inconceivable thinness, even to the point of transparency, which proves the aforementioned texture more than sufficiently. Daily experience and manual labor also give us an unshakeable recognition that copper and iron are better wrought by <term>Salt</term> [🜔] (as their necessary weaving-<term>Water</term> [🜄]) and are more durable for work and all necessities than lead and tin. Although tin and lead are soft and gentle, and can also be drawn into wire and beaten into thin sheets, they nevertheless break very quickly in such work. Therefore, one may conclude that their texture or weaving—like a spoiled or coarse paper—was provoked either too strongly or too little by the subterranean <term>Fire</term> [🜂] (as briefly mentioned above). Thus their physical body remained close to the unrefined body or <term>Metallic Mercury</term> [☿]—specifically <term>mercury in its immediate state</term> <note>original: "☿ in proximo"</note>. And just as this mercury consists of an inconceivable number of the <term>smallest globules</term> <note>original: "minutissimorum globulorum"</note> and thus cannot withstand the <term>Fire</term> [🜂] in the least, nor allow itself to be hammered and stretched because these innumerable globules (like a <term>Water</term> [🜄]) do not hold together for lack of <term>living Sulfur</term> <note>original: "🜍 vivi"</note>, so they cannot be extended or hammered like the four "woven" metals: <term>Gold</term> <note>original: ☉</note>, <term>Silver</term> <note>original: ☽</note>, <term>Venus/Copper</term> <note>original: ♀</note>, and <term>Mars/Iron</term> <note>original: ♂</note>. Thus, and in exactly this manner, these two bodies consist of such coarse globules, whereby the <term>reduction</term> <note>the process of returning a substance to its original form</note> of these two bodies to a <term>living Mercury</term> [☿ vivo] may be demonstrated and easily proven—a fact well known to all true chemists. For once they are separated from their <term>foreign element</term> <note>original: "heterogeneo"</note>, namely <term>burning Sulfur</term> <note>original: "🜍 comburens"</note>, they are nothing but <term>Mercury</term> [☿]. And that there must be little of the <term>living Salt</term> <note>original: "🜔ris vivi"</note> in them is taught by undeniable experience: we ourselves, by means of certain <term>Alkalies</term> <note>original: "Alcalinorum"</note>, have separated almost 11 ounces of good <term>Mercury</term> [☿] from one pound of lead. We also previously knew an artist who, by this same path (though with a little more time and other manual techniques), separated almost 14 ounces from one pound. This clearly demonstrates what was just said: namely, that they consist mostly <term>of Salt</term> <note>original: "ex 🜔"</note> and have little <term>living Mercury</term> [☿ vivi] within them.

<margin>
Letter U.
</margin>
But here again is an opportunity to digress a little and to place a stumbling block in the way of the over-learned, asking: how can this possibly be reconciled? That one can transform almost the entire body of <term>Jupiter/Tin</term> <note>original: ♃</note> and <term>Saturn/Lead</term> <note>original: ♄</note> (not to mention <term>Silver</term> [☽] for now) into a natural <term>Mercury</term> [☿], such that hardly a fourth part is lost (which is nothing but a natural burning <term>Sulfur</term> [🜍] and a little earth)? And furthermore, through this same but reversed <term>Solvent</term> <note>original: "Menstruum"</note>, one can transform these very bodies of lead and tin entirely into a <term>Salt</term> [🜔], such that almost little or nothing at all is lost? Namely, that we do not mean here such a <term>Salt</term> [🜔] of <term>Lead</term> [♄] and <term>Tin</term> [♃] as all the laboratory assistants, <term>dabblers</term> <note>original: "Sudelköche," literally "messy cooks," a derogatory term for amateur or fraudulent alchemists.</note>, and apothecary boys can make with <term>Acid</term> <note>original: + / Acetum</note> or other sour agents, and which can be reduced back into its first body using fats or other advantages. No, we do not mean such a thing; and if one knows no other, we say openly that such a person has not yet come to the right well of the "Sweet Sea," nor bathed in its water. We also do not mean a high philosophical <term>Salt</term> [🜔] prepared by the <term>Water of Nature</term> <note>original: "🜄 Naturæ"</note>, where such a body has been reduced again into its first oily-saline being, or <term>to its first principle</term> <note>original: "ad primum principium suum"</note>. Rather, our speech is of a true metallic salt or spiritual salt, to distinguish it from common salt, from which no metallic body has ever yet been separated. And such a salt is easy to make for those who well understand the right, simple, and inexpensive solvent, and the correct <term>Calcination</term> <note>the reduction of a metal to a powder through heat</note> of the body; it is well known to all true chemists. We therefore ask, not unfairly: since these bodies of <term>Lead</term> [♄] and <term>Tin</term> [♃] (to say nothing of the other metals for now) are almost entirely nothing but <term>Salt</term> [🜔] and yet at the same time are almost entirely nothing but <term>Mercury</term> [☿], how can this be reconciled with what the <term>Physicists</term> <note>original: "Physici," meaning natural philosophers.</note> otherwise teach? Namely, that metals consist of three beginnings or <term>Principles</term> <note>original: "Principiis"</note>, which are <term>Salt, Sulfur, and Mercury</term> [🜔, 🜍 & ☿]. And how do they intend to defend their Principles in the face of such true and clear proof? And who can now...

<margin>Letter W.</margin> ...to prove that in the metals there are three essential principles, or that they consist of the same. §. 23. Let us therefore continue in the description of the texture or "weaving" of the metals, as this discourse must be completed before we go further. It was proven above that <term>Gold</term> and <term>Silver</term> are perfectly wrought. Following this, however, <margin>Letter Y.</margin> <term>Copper</term> <note>original: ♀ (Venus)</note> and <term>Iron</term> <note>original: ♂ (Mars)</note>, as those that also allow themselves to be stretched and extended quite well and hold firmly together, become all the better and more capable for all stretching, plating, and expansion the more they are separated from their combustible <term>Sulfur</term> (of which these two metals have an abundance). This then proves once more what we said above regarding their texture. The weaving of <term>Tin</term> [♃] and <term>Lead</term> [♄] is also sufficiently proven to the intelligent. We come then to the seventh metal, or rather the "metallic water," namely <term>Mercury</term> <note>original: "Mercurio"</note>, which has made more fools than wine and love ever have. We have cause to fear this "bird" not a little, because he lets his feathers fly so very easily. But because we never had the intention of catching this bird again, nor of keeping it, but only to contemplate its wondrous birth, we shall not be so very terrified of it. Much less will we quarrel with anyone over the opinions held until now regarding its essence and origin, but will gladly leave everyone to their own fancies, on the <term>condition</term> that we are likewise left undisturbed in ours. Yet, let no one imagine that he will be dealt with entirely and exhaustively in this discourse on the weaving and texture of metals; rather, we only repeat and <margin>Letter α.</margin> say once more that its texture or weaving was left quite incomplete by the weaving-waters of the metals, namely the <term>Salt</term> [🜔] and the sliminess or <term>Viscosity</term> (but not the material <margin>Letter β.</margin> <term>Cooking Salt</term> <note>Common table salt</note>) and the subterranean <term>Fire</term> <note>original: "i.e. 🜂"</note>. Thus, the <term>living Mercury</term> <note>original: "☿ vivus"</note> is in truth nothing other than the still-raw Solar and Lunar white <term>Fire</term> [🜂] or <term>universal Mercury</term> in <term>metallic form</term>, which, due to a lack of metallic weaving-water, was left lying in its <term>ore</term> <note>original: "Minera"</note> unrefined by the subterranean fire, rather than being specified into this or that metal. And so that we do not have to repeat a thing too often, yet may be sufficiently understood, it is necessary to read and contemplate with all diligence what was reported above in §. 17, Letter F, <margin>Letter γ.</margin> for then our meaning will be easily grasped; namely, that the

> "spiritual heavenly Gold and Silver—the red and white Sulfur mentioned so often before (otherwise <margin>Letter δ.</margin> called <term>universal Mercury</term> [☿])—is cooked into this or that metal in or with the weaving-water of the metals by the subterranean <term>Fire</term> [🜂]. Now, the purer the birth of this Earth has sprung from the Virgin Earth, and been endowed with the heavenly seed (*see* with all diligence §. 16 and the places cited there), the purer this <term>Matrix</term> and the birth of the metals conceived and generated within it will be, according to the nature of its own specified essence."

§. 24. We have thus briefly, yet abundantly, laid before those who love the truth the true birth of the metals and their <term>Matrices</term>, as was promised in §. 11. <header>ON THE ORIGIN OF COMMON SALT.</header>

...grains of sand, both in quicksand and all other types, contain within themselves this heavenly seed. Because the <term>rays</term> <note>original: "Radii"</note> of the stars cannot pass through the Moon’s region or sphere in equal proportion, and because the <term>motion of the stars</term> <note>original: "Motus Astrorum"</note>, as well as the alternating of light and darkness, causes the light or <term>solar rays</term> <note>original: "Radii Solares"</note> to be unable to act upon this terrestrial globe with constant equality <note>original: "æquabili proportione"</note>; it must therefore necessarily follow that the activity in the great springs of the great deep in the great <margin>Gen. 7 v. 11</margin> <term>Water</term> <note>original: $\nabla$</note> region E. F. (see Figure 1) cannot be equal. The same applies to the seas, as we demonstrated above in §. 18, Letter H. From this, it must necessarily follow that the quicksand produced from the <term>Water</term> [$\nabla$] by the subterranean <term>Fire</term> <note>original: $\triangle$</note>, and its offspring—common sandstone and rock—must likewise be of very unequal quality due to the outflows of the subterranean <term>Fire</term> [$\triangle$]. This inequality causes the diversity of the <term>matrices</term> <note>The "matrix" is the host rock or "womb" in which minerals were thought to grow like embryos.</note> of metals and minerals. The purer and more perfect the saline, sand-making <term>Water</term> [$\nabla$] and the mud or <term>Earth</term> <note>original: "Terra"</note> were as the basis of the stone and rock, the purer and more perfect the concentration of the outflows of the subterranean fire became within them. In this manner, the perfection <margin>Letter O.</margin> or imperfection of the matrix of this or that perfect or imperfect metal was generated. Furthermore, within this matrix, through the nourishment of the lunar universal <term>Water</term> [$\nabla$] (acting as the <term>menstruum</term> <note>A solvent or nutritive fluid used to extract or "feed" an alchemical substance.</note> of the matrix), the <term>digestion</term> <note>A slow heating process meant to mature a substance.</note> or boiling-out of the heavenly seed (the red and white <term>Fire/Sulfur</term> [$\triangle$] of all nature) takes place in this or that metal or mineral, depending on how pure the mother and her menstrual nourishment were. That this is irrefutably true is witnessed by all ores. The matrix, along with the birth of the metal or mineral—even the most perfect, such as gold and silver ores—bears witness to this. For the purer and more perfect they are in content, the purer and more perfect is their stone or matrix, removed from all coarse, slimy (that is, <term>Fiery/Sulfurous</term> [$\triangle$]) and <term>menstrual</term> (that is, <term>Vitriolic</term> <note>original symbol: $\oplus$</note>) impurity. This is perfectly shown by vein-ore, silver-glance <note>original: "Glaß-Erz"</note>, light and dark ruby silver ore, and many others. Their "mountain" (to speak in miners' terms) is much nobler and purer than that of other low-grade ores, and they do not have so much predatory, arsenical, or <term>Vitriolic</term> [$\oplus$] essence about them. The opposite, however, is found in poor and low-grade gold and silver ores. If one considers <term>Iron</term> <note>original: $\mars$ (Mars)</note> and <term>Copper</term> <note>original: $\venus$ (Venus)</note> ores, we would not even need to ask the learned, but only the common miners; they would immediately tell us complainingly how full these are of impure <term>Fire/Sulfur</term> [$\triangle$] and <term>Vitriol</term> [$\oplus$], and for this reason, they can be refined only with great difficulty. However, the more the experienced smelter knows how to separate these <term>heterogeneous</term> <note>Foreign or impurities.</note> elements, the purer, more perfect, and more capable their <term>Body</term> [original: "Corpus"] or metal—having reached its predestined end—will appear. True chemists can clearly demonstrate this through iron; for if they frequently moisten its pure ore-dust with <term>Fire/Sulfur</term> [$\triangle$] and <term>Water</term> [$\nabla$], it is finally reduced through corruption and rust into a yellow <term>Vitriol/Earth</term> [$\oplus$] (which is also found abundantly in iron veins and crevices). If they then add this yellow earth, called <term>ocher</term>, back to the iron, the iron becomes much tougher, softer, and more capable than it ever was before. The reason is that its coarse, combustible <term>Fire/Sulfur</term> [$\triangle$] was corrected by its own <term>Vitriolic Gold</term> <note>original: $\oplus$lisches $\odot$</note> during the roasting or corruption. Conversely, it becomes harder to melt because its coarse, easily-melted <term>Fire/Sulfur</term> [$\triangle$] has been corrected and brought to perfect form and shape by its own menstrual weaving-<term>Water</term> [$\nabla$]. In truth, we have set this down not for fools, but for the lovers of truth for their more mature reflection. It is the same with copper; it is surrounded in its matrix quite abundantly with this impure sulfur and vitriol. The more it is separated from this decay by the hand of the artist, the more stable and perfect, yet harder to melt, it becomes. But if the artist can work his destructive <term>Fire/Sulfur</term> [$\triangle$] through his own metallic weaving-<term>Water</term> [$\nabla$] toward stability, as it were, and <margin>Letter N.</margin> add its metal back to it, then he has demonstrated a masterpiece in smelting known to none but those experienced in nature. And if he understands this purification for all four lesser metals, namely <term>Copper</term> [$\venus$], <term>Iron</term> [$\mars$], <term>Tin</term> <note>original: $\jupiter$ (Jupiter)</note>, and <term>Lead</term> <note>original: $\saturn$ (Saturn)</note>, he can boldly hope that he will also find the way to even greater things. Yet, let him first of all learn to know that non-corrosive <term>Vitriol</term> [$\oplus$] which we have called here and there the <margin>Letter A.</margin> weaving water of the metals.

§. 25. Concerning <term>Lead</term> [$\saturn$] and <term>Tin</term> [$\jupiter$], one can judge similarly from their minerals; namely, the rays of the outflows of the subterranean fire, as the <term>Fire/Sulfur</term> [$\triangle$] with the <term>Solar</term> [$\odot$] menstrual <term>Water</term> [$\nabla$] and the muddy <term>Earth</term> [$\oplus$], have not concentrated and united well. Therefore, the production by the subterranean <term>Fire/Sulfur</term> [$\triangle$] could reach no other than this predestined degree. And this impurity or porosity of the matrix is also the reason why more spiritual <term>Gold</term> [$\odot$] and <term>Silver</term> <note>original: $\leftmoon$ (Moon/Luna)</note> is found in one metal than in another (even within one species, as in this lead more than in that). Namely, the subterranean fire [$\triangle$], instead of digesting this heavenly red and white <term>Fire/Sulfur</term> [$\triangle$] or seed into a perfect birth, withers it due to a lack of healthy, pure menstrual, saline nourishing juice, which is <margin>Letter M.</margin> not to be found in such an impure matrix. With the seventh metal, or rather metal-water, namely <term>Mercury</term> <note>original: $\mercurius$ (Quicksilver)</note> [$\mercury$], it has a similar relationship regarding its mineral. For its red colors give us clearly to understand that this matrix consists almost entirely of <term>Fire/Sulfur</term> [$\triangle$] (that is, volatile rays of fire) and almost less <term>Water</term> [$\nabla$], and has lacked the menstrual, saline weaving-<term>Water</term> [$\nabla$] (which binds the <term>Fire/Sulfur</term> [$\triangle$]). Therefore, they had to leave this heavenly sulfurous seed in its first coagulation in this watery form, without further refinement. If, however, the mineral has a different color, such as gray, etc., then a different judgment is required by one experienced in nature regarding such a form, and it shows

He details how impurities like sulfur and vitriol affect metals like iron and copper, and notes that Mercury remains liquid because it lacks sufficient saline "weaving water" to bind its fiery spirit.</summary>

# CHAPTER I.

<margin>Letter μ.</margin>Thus, **living Mercury** <note>original: "☿ vivus"</note> represents to us the first degree of natural manifestation or the **first predestined end** <note>original: "finem prædestinatum primum"</note>. Let the Mercurial Alchemists observe, then, what kind of bird they are dealing with, and what kind of **Fire** [△] they require to burn off its flight-feathers and—since it is still homogeneous—to make it fixed and stable into Gold. Namely, they must provide that which was lacking in its **ore-vein** <note>original: "Minera"</note>, as is easily understood from the preceding. For since the coarse, earthly **Fire** [△] could do nothing to it, <margin>Letter ν.</margin> especially as it lacked the **menstruous Salt** <note>In alchemy, a "menstruum" is a solvent that dissolves a solid body; "menstruous salt" refers to a specific catalytic salt used in the "Great Work."</note>, it must have **Fire** [△] and **Salt** [⊖] **in due proportion** <note>original: "in debita proportione"</note>, or such a fixed, **fiery** [🜂] **Salt** [⊖] that remains constantly fixed and fluid in all **Fire** [△] and is able to penetrate all fixed **bodies** <note>original: "corpora"</note>.

§. 26. What we have spoken of here regarding metals should also be understood of minerals in general, each according to its kind and species; and whoever has understood us above will require no further explanation in this matter. The man of understanding will also easily grasp it from all that has been said thus far. <margin>Letter ζ.</margin> As for what **Gur** <note>In early mining lore, "Gur" (or Guhr) was a white or yellowish "metallic milk" found in rock crevices, believed to be the "unripe" liquid state of metals before they hardened.</note> may be—namely a premature birth, an unripe metal—it is where the **running Mercury** <note>original: "☿ currens"</note> has undergone the second degree of "cooking" and has had its superfluous **Earthiness** [🜃] removed, and has thus come **into the proximity** <note>original: "in proximum"</note> of becoming a metal, but was prevented by a premature eruption, so that it could not reach full **coagulation** through the subterranean **Fire** [△]. Furthermore, we say for certain that **Gur** is as diverse as the **Earths** [🜃] of the metals and minerals, as the man of understanding will easily grant us from all that has been recounted. Thus, those who hold this **Gur** to be the **First Matter** <note>original: "pro prima materia"</note> and seek to perfect the **Philosopher's Stone** <note>original: "Lapidem Philosophorum"</note> from it err greatly; for it is as little the **First Matter** as an **embryo** in the mother's womb.

§. 27. Thus, the birth of all **Realgar** <note>Arsenic sulfide minerals</note> is also easily understood—namely Antimony and all Arsenicals, Bismuth, Marcasites, Zinc, etc.—that they are nothing other than a mere **Mercury** [☿] **in dry form** <note>original: "in forma sicca"</note>. This occurs as the Mercury is dried out by the subterranean **Fire** [△] merely with **saline Water** [🜄 🜄lina], lacking the necessary pure **Sulfur** [🜍], into this or that predestined **Body** <note>original: "Corpore"</note>, such as Cobalt, Antimony, Bismuth, Zinc, etc. For all of these can be driven off entirely into an Arsenic with a small amount of **Fire** [△]. One has had too much of the red **Sulfurous** [🜍] nature, and another too much of the white **Lunar** [☽] **Sulfur** [🜍]; therefore, neither one nor the other could be ripened into stable Gold or Silver. For all these **Realgars** are almost homogeneous offspring of the **Sun and Moon** <note>original: "Solis & Lunæ"; here meaning Gold and Silver</note>, and due to a lack of either the right **Gold-seed** [☉] or the right **Sulfur** [🜍], they remained stuck in the second degree of **coagulation**. Nevertheless, they have also reached their **predestined end** <note>original: "prædestinatum finem"</note>, for their **degrees** necessarily belong to this **Universal Harmony** <note>original: "Harmonia Universali"</note>; since nothing may happen without God's will, and whatever happens by His holy will has also reached its purpose.

§. 28. Now, before we go further, we must also speak somewhat of that wondrous mineral water, namely **Coal**. There are generally three kinds found: **surface coal**, **roof coal**, and **pitch coal**. The **fat coal** in the **Luckerland** <note>Likely referring to the region of Liège (Lüttich), famous for its coal deposits.</note> is yet another kind entirely, for it burns like heated pitch; and when a seam there has been almost entirely broken out (though perhaps 2 or 3 inches are left standing), the same shaft nevertheless grows completely full again after a few years. The **reason** <note>original: "Ratio"</note> is that its own magnet, in the remnant left standing, completely replaces everything from the **astral effluvia** <note>The perceived influence or "outflow" of the stars into the terrestrial realm.</note> or the upper air. For its birth is nothing other than a precipitation of **Solar rays** <note>original: "Rad. Solar."</note> in a purer **Lunar Earth** [🜃], along with the sand- and stone-making waters constantly exhaling in those places in Region E. F. (see Figure 1), which causes the **consistency** of the body. These are the best known, of which the first kind is nothing other than a **Sulfur**-containing **Earth** [🜃], or a **Sulfur** [🜍] coagulated by a coarse vitriolic **Earth** [🜃] (see Letters α. β. γ. δ. §. 23), more subtle than that of the roof and pitch coals, mixed with a coarse loamy earth. Therefore, they are not stable, but easily weather in the **Fire** [△] and fall apart as a powder, and thus cannot well be used for smithing and other strong **Fire**-work, unless one assists them with a clayey, **saline Earth** [🜃]; for since the **Earth** [🜃] is the bond of **Sulfur** [🜍], they are then quite good to use, as the following shall prove. The second **species**, roof coals, have a coarser **Sulfur**, better coagulated by the **Earth** [🜃], mixed with a purer clay <note>original: "Letten"</note>, wherefore their **body** is also more durable and holds together more firmly. They vary according to what the clay was like: if it was yellowish, the coal is brown, and this is quite like wood that has lain in the earth, and is therefore called **wood-coal**; bluish and white-shimmering clay also yields much brown coal, though tending toward black. If the clay is black and iron-colored, the coal will also be black; and these roof coals are already good for smithy work and all other needs, although when ground small, they can likewise be improved with a clay impregnated with the right **Salt** [⊖], so that they burn more efficiently and durably. Their floor <note>original: "Sohle"</note> is mostly a **seam** <note>original: "Flöz"</note> or clayey, also firm, sometimes even sandy stone, according to how the mountain was formed and weathered. These roof coals very often ennoble themselves in the mountains into a right good **fat** or **pitch coal**. The third kind is **pitch coal**, consisting of a coarser, almost metallic **Sulfur** [🜍] and finer clay, like quicksand; these pitch coals are the very best, being closest to this quicksand, for their **Earth** [🜃] is delicate and pure, and most precisely mixed with the **Sulfur** [🜍], and bound and fixed by the **saline Water** [🜄 🜄lina]; therefore they are also harder and can resist longer in the **Fire** [△]. Whoever denies what is said here must be little traveled in the Earth, and it is certain that where coal has the best deposits, one will find both at its base and where it settles...

# ON THE ORIGIN OF COMMON SALT.

...encounter such a delicate, greasy clay <note>original: "Letten"</note> mixed with drift sand, and often find that they pass over such a layer, which serves as the unshakeable basis for the hardness and stability of the coal. Now, **Sulfur** <term>🜍</term> <note>Sulfur here refers to the active, combustible principle in alchemy, rather than just the chemical element.</note>—namely this greasy, burning substance—is an outflow of the subterranean **Fire** <term>🜁</term>. How it is generated there may be seen below. Where this fire gains a breakthrough through the rifts and passages of Region E. F. in Figure 1 into Region D. E., and there encounters and permeates such a clayey substance, it is seized within it by the **Salt** <term>🜒</term> and **Water** <term>🜄</term>. Thus, by the subterranean **Fire** <term>🜁</term>, with the help of the aforementioned **Water** <term>🜄</term>, it is coagulated into this hard, stony, burning substance. However, if there is found here and there within this clayey earth (in "nests" or "kidneys," as the miners say) a matrix already worked out—one that is not as porous as the clayey earth and can receive nothing but the very purest and more fixed part of the aforementioned **Sulfur** <term>🜍</term>—then within that matrix, **Sulfur-Pyrite** <term>🜍 Kieß</term> <note>Pyrite, or "fool's gold," was seen by alchemists as an immature form of gold or a concentrated sulfurous mineral.</note> is generated. This is found so frequently scattered within stone-coal, and such a sulfurous pyrite is also found in most places within the clay, which completely confirms our discourse.

Since this clay is the basis of all stone-coal, wherever this **Sulfur-Pyrite** <term>🜍 Kieß</term> is frequently found in coal, it is an indication of singular quality. For the more it is found, the more it indicates that the **Sulfur** <term>🜍</term> which generated the coal is more material and fixed than where it is absent. These sulfurous pyrites all contain **Gold** <note>Sol</note> and **Silver** <note>Luna</note>, for they are already a fairly ripe matrix in which the outflows of the heavens—namely of the Sun, Moon, and stars—have been cooked to almost <margin>Lit. a.</margin> complete ripeness. For the Sun is in truth nothing other than an essential, thoroughly refined, spiritual Gold, of which Gold is spoken in Revelation 3:18. And the outflows of this Gold or Sun are nothing other than **Salt** <term>🜒</term>. Truly, here is a depth of confusion for all those who are enamored with nothing but their own speculations and have never endeavored to recognize the ground of Nature.

§. 29. It has been superabundantly proven above that the Sun, Moon, and stars are an outflow of the **Heavens** <note>original Hebrew: "Schamajim"</note> first created by God the Lord, or flowed out from His Majesty. For we say that these **Heavens** <note>Schamajim</note> were generated from the unfathomable and bottomless gentle, still waters of the immeasurable Divine Majesty, of which the wise man should think more than speak. It has also been superabundantly proven and demonstrated above that the outflows of all these bodies (which bodies are the dispensers of the **Heavens** <note>Schamajim</note>) are nothing but **Salt** <term>🜒</term>, and that this **Salt** <term>🜒</term> is an essential **Fire** <term>🜁</term>. Furthermore, this **Fire** <term>🜁</term> or **Salt** <term>🜒</term> is the red and white **Sulfur** <term>🜍</term> of Nature—namely, the spiritual Gold and Silver from which all other beings live and arise, and which is found in all bodies, down to the very most delicate grain of sand, like sun-dust. Thus, it must certainly and indisputably follow for those practiced in Nature that **Fire** <term>🜁</term>, **Salt** <term>🜒</term>, and the **Sun** <term>☉</term> are entirely one and the same in their root. Also, the red Solar and white Lunar **Sulfur** <term>🜍</term> are at their core and origin only one and the same: namely, spiritual **Gold** <term>☉</term> and **Silver**, or **Salt** <term>🜒</term> and **Fire** <term>🜁</term>. He who knows these two **Sulfurs** <term>🜍ra</term>, knows how to find them, and knows how to put them together in right proportion, to unite and to shape them, must necessarily come to see the magnificent, shining, indestructible, and immortal body of the man Adam (Aphar, Adamah) <note>Hebrew terms for the "dust" and "ground" from which Adam was formed.</note>. For man (the **Microcosm** <term>Microcosmus</term>) is the comprehensible end of all creatures, in whom all the seeds of the Universe are contained and have been made perfect, which henceforth cannot possibly be brought to naught, as is to be understood from the above. Yes, whoever <note>NB.</note> well weighs and understands all this will also grasp what we said above on page 3, margin note a.a.: namely, how fallen man, gone into corruption and putrefaction through sin, must be held fixed and stable against the total corruption and destruction by the sweet, fixed **Salt** <term>🜒</term> of the still, gentle eternity—Jesus Christ—generated in the still, gentle sea of the unfathomable mercy of God the eternal Father, according to the **Fire** <term>🜁</term> of the glorious Majesty of God, unto His eternal glory. He will also have to understand what we aimed at in the <note>NB.</note> on page 10, in §. 20, where we spoke of the red and white **Sulfur** <term>🜍</term> of Nature or of the Sages. He will understand why Christ our Savior, for His most holy Supper—namely, for the partaking of His blessed Body and Blood, the fixed, indestructible Salt of Love of the infinite mercy of God (which is the holy spiritual gold and silver of Revelation 3:18)—took the essential pieces of bread and wine as the Solar and Lunar elements, which in essence are also only one and the same, as all the preceding proves. But here it is commanded: chatter no more of the mysteries of God than is required to answer, and it is better to feast with a still spirit at these springs of the unfathomable wisdom of God and to reflect therein, than to wish to chatter and speak much before the cursed, malicious, and lie-loving world. Therefore, the lover of wisdom and truth will demand nothing further from our weakness. We say only this: that in the City of God (Revelation 21) no earthly sun or moon shines, nor is gold to be found there, for the Sun, Salt, and Gold of the eternal Divine Essence is present there. And our bodies, tinctured by the living **Antimony/Earth** <term>⊕</term> <note>The symbol ⊕ here likely refers to the "Tincture" or the "Earth of the Sages," identified here with Christ as the transformative agent.</note> Jesus Christ, and turned back into the still, gentle sea of the Divine Waters (Revelation 21:6 and 22:1), will there be eternally refreshed, so that they may bring forth their monthly ripe fruits by these Divine streams and brooks to the praise of His eternal glory. With this, we shall now break off and remain silent, lest we wander entirely astray from this astonishing depth.

§. 30. And we take up again the thread from which we have somewhat deviated, namely...

<meta>This page continues the discussion of mineral formation, specifically focusing on the relationship between subterranean fire, the production of sulfurous coal, and the spiritual-chemical nature of Salt as the "Beginning and End" of all material existence.</meta>

### CHAPTER I.

...to the **pyrites** <term>△ Kießen</term> <note>Pyrites or "fire-stones," often associated with sulfur and the "maturation" of metals.</note> and **stone-coal**. Regarding the former, we have said that they contain **Gold** <term>☉</term> and **Silver** <term>☾</term>; yet this gold and silver of theirs is still mostly volatile, because the matrix is still too porous, and the **vital moisture** <note>original: "Olinische Webungs ▽"</note> was lacking in due proportion. Therefore, the skillful refiner <note>original: "Scheider"</note> must know how to assist them here; namely, he must know how to give them the right **Fire** <term>△</term> and **Water** <term>▽</term> for cooking, if he wishes to fix the volatile part and arrange the separation usefully. He must not be ignorant of the **Fire** <term>△</term>. We certainly believe regarding the **vital moisture** <term>▽</term> that it is not the **sour kitchen salt** <term>⊖</term> that is necessary for this fixation. Rather, before the artist undertakes this work, he must well know the **sweet alkaline of nature** <note>original: "Alcalinum dulce naturæ"</note> and how to increase it from the **rays of the Sun** <term>☉ radiis Solaribus</term>. For certainly, whoever does not know this lovely **Salt** <term>⊖</term> has not yet experienced the distinction between salts; for the **saline moisture** <note>original: "Salinische Webungs ▽"</note> of metallic, animal, and vegetable bodies is a completely different **Salt** <term>⊖</term> than the **sour kitchen salt** <term>⊖</term> found in seas, wells, mountains, and the earth. 

However, no one here should imagine that we are acting against our first thesis in § 10, letter k, where we said that common **kitchen salt** <term>⊖</term> is the beginning and the end of all things. On the contrary, we confirm this once more. Because the recently mentioned **Salt of Nature** <term>⊖ naturæ</term>—as the "stone-root" of the sour salt found everywhere—is not visible to our eyes as such, but rather as it has become corporeal in this great kitchen (the globe of the earth) by the **subterranean fire** <term>△ Subterraneo</term>, and has taken on acidity from the **rays of fire** or **fiery vapors** (which are also nothing but **Salt** <term>⊖</term>), enough has been said for the lovers of truth and wisdom. The fools, however, may understand it as they wish. Suffice it to say, when we speak of the **vital moisture** or the **moist saline solvent of all bodies** <note>original: "Salina menstruosa omnium Corporum"</note>, we speak of **Salt** <term>⊖</term> as we see, touch, and taste it, and which the man of understanding can at any moment transform into the **Salt of Nature**. For it is the very same **Salt** <term>⊖</term> generated by the Sun, Moon, and Stars, of which enough and more than enough has been said previously, and thus no objection will remain among the wise.

§ 31. Shortly before, we considered that the acidity of our universal salt in this great Earth-Kitchen was caused by the fiery vapors of the **subterranean fire** <term>△</term>, even in the sea itself, which is likewise not at all free from the exhalations of this subterranean fire. That this is true, the **stone-coals** shall especially prove to us. For in truth, anyone who denies that where stone-coals are found, no salt-springs are to be found—or where **Salt** <term>⊖</term> springs are found, there are no stone-coals—is truly foolish and little practiced or experienced in Nature. This requires no proof at all. Therefore, because the salt-veins mostly run deep, the **surface-coal** <note>original: "Tage- und Dach-Kohlen"</note> has less fixity and usefulness than the **stone-coal**, **fat-coal**, or **pitch-coal**, where the breaking-forth **Fire** <term>△</term> is better fixed and bound by the **Salt** <term>⊖</term> streams. On the other hand, the salt-veins also take more and more of the acidity of the fire into themselves and become sharper. This is in truth the only reason <margin>Lit. τ.</margin> why springs are weaker in one place and sharper in another, as shall be proven following in Chapter II, *On the Nature of Salts*. For where abundant and good stone-coals are found in the subterranean mountains, there the springs are much more magnificent and sharper than in those places where there are few and poor coals, such as surface or "roof" coals. It is also well enough known to those practiced in **true chemistry** <note>original: "vera Chymia"</note> how to separate the **sour salt** <term>⊖</term> from this accidental acidity and restore to it its innate natural pleasantness and sweetness, which it has substantially in and of itself when one knows how to draw it from its pure, unadulterated sea—though this is not everyone’s task. This proves sufficiently that its acidity is a being caused by the **Fire** <term>△</term> or the subterranean fiery rays, because it can be separated from them again and reduced to its **primeval state** <note>original: "ad primum suum esse"</note>. For all acidity is the **offspring of fire** <note>original: "des △ Ausgeburth"</note>, just as **fire** <term>△</term> itself can easily be reduced into a sharp, sour **water** <term>▽</term>, as well as into a **Salt** <term>⊖</term>, and finally back to its first lovely original state. Now, whoever understands this well will truly also easily perceive how the **Alkalies** (see § 5, letter b) can easily be transformed into a pleasant, sour **cooking salt** <term>⊖</term>. 

It is <margin>Lit. υ.</margin> thus sufficiently proven that acidity is a thing accidental to the **fire** <term>△</term>, and the man of understanding can easily see how far those err who consider **Salt** <term>⊖</term> and **Mercury** <term>☿</term> to be two very closely related things, <margin>see Chap. II. § 10.</margin> indeed almost one thing. (Of which, however, more will be said in detail in the following chapter, § 10.) We have thus dealt with stone-coal in enough detail, and sufficiently demonstrated and proven that **Salt** <term>⊖</term> is a true **Fire** <term>△</term> and **Mercury** <term>☿</term>, and thus it is true that all things consist <margin>Lit. φ.</margin> **of three in one** <note>original: "ex tribus in uno"</note> from **Salt** <term>⊖</term>, **Fire** <term>△</term>, and **Mercury** <term>☿</term>, which in the beginning are namely only **Fire** <term>△</term>. It remains then to be proven that **Fire** <term>△</term> is also **Water** <term>▽</term>, which is not the least point. Furthermore, that **Salt** <term>⊖</term> is the beginning and the end of all things—namely the **Water** <term>▽</term> that is **Fire** <term>△</term>, the outflow of Divine Majesty, namely **Schamajim** <note>Hebrew for "Heavens." Welling interprets this kabbalistically as a compound of *Esh* (Fire) and *Mayim* (Water).</note>.

§ 32. First, we must prove that **Salt** <term>⊖</term> is the beginning and the end of all things; indeed, that the end of all perfection is nothing but **Salt** <term>⊖</term>. To the understanding and the wise, this requires no great demonstration; it is also sufficiently known and demonstrated in everything treated so far that all things have sprouted and been born from **Schamajim**, and further generated and brought forth by the **Sun** as Father, and the **Moon** and **Stars** as the Mother of all sublunary bodies. These bodies or stars altogether are nothing other than the **Schamajim** driven together into spiritual bodies (in regard to sublunary bodies). Their outflow can then be nothing other than what they themselves are: namely **Salt** <term>⊖</term> or **Fire** <term>△</term> or **Water** <term>▽</term> and **Mercury** <term>☿</term>, or whatever else the wise man wishes to call it, such as **Universal Salt** <term>⊖ Universalis</term>. It has also been sufficiently proven that all these outflows gather in this sublunary mass, through the subterranean fire...

# CONCERNING THE ORIGIN OF COMMON SALT.

...or congeal and continue to grow in that Body, in all three kingdoms. Furthermore, **Fire-work** <note>The practical application of alchemy and chemistry through the use of heat.</note> teaches us tangibly that no Body may be found in nature which cannot be entirely **reduced into Solar Salt** <term>☉</term> <note>Welling uses the symbol for the Sun/Gold (☉) to represent the "Essential" or "Central Salt," the perfected state of matter.</note>, even the most perfect ones, such as Gold and Silver. But alas! Here I see a hailstorm and cruel weather of **Sophistical scholarship** rising against these words; yet I remain undaunted, for Truth is our shield, and the Most High will hide us in His tabernacle in such evil times. Yes, He will secretly hide us in His chamber, and set us under His holy hand upon the rock of safety, etc. Psalm 27. Therefore, be of good cheer, and pay no heed to such windy, raging, roaring weather, but rather let the **still, gentle whistling of the lovely waters of still eternity** be our contentment and refreshment. We must nevertheless provide some proof, should the fools wish to become wiser, and so we make a beginning in the **Vegetable Kingdom**, since the Animal Kingdom will then prove and protect itself; yet we pray that the lazy rascals and stubborn fools do not approach, for this shall be no judgment for them. One should take several 1000 pounds [℔] of
<margin>Letter ℵ</margin>
wood and burn it, so that all smoke and moisture may be captured without any loss; when it is now burned, the smallest part will be **Ash**, in which resides a **fixed, fresh Salt** <term>🜔</term>. Leach this well, place it in a suitable vessel, and pour so much of the captured liquid upon it that it is sufficiently moistened; close the vessel and let it stand in proper **Digestion** <note>A process of gentle, continuous heat, similar to incubation.</note>; thus this moisture will truly congeal within the **Salt** <term>🜔</term> and become **fixed**, that is, it will become one with the **Solar Salt** <term>☉</term>. Continue this with patience, and you will, beyond all dispute, have all your captured moisture figured and congealed again into a true **Solar Salt** <term>☉</term>; and this is the first part. However, here I must necessarily look about and resist a whole troop of **Sophists**, and filthy murderers and destroyers of the works of God, who all unanimously know how to talk, prattle, and scream so much about the deaf, dead, leached-out ashes, called by them **condemned earth** <term>Terra damnata</term> <note>The useless residue remaining after all "virtue" or salt has been extracted.</note>, asking where this then remains? Whether this too becomes **Solar Salt** <term>☉</term>? Claiming this would strive entirely against Nature. But the answer is: **Yes, you fools**; for if you knew Nature and her ways, you would know what your **condemned earth** <note>original: *Terra damnata*</note> was **in the beginning** <note>original: *in principio*</note>, and that all things are to be reduced back **to their first being** <note>original: *ad suum primum esse*</note>; you would not then rage so cruelly against Nature and destroy her, but would instead learn to recognize that all Bodies, from their beginning and the progress of their growth unto their perfection, may be brought back by that very same path to their beginning and first original state; for in truth, this is the **Jewel** for which the Sages strive. For **Gold** may not become **Lead**, for that would be to forget God and act against Nature, to transform a perfect Body, outside of its **specific type** <note>original: *Specifico*</note>, into a lesser one. However, Gold as well as Lead may each, through their own paths, be brought back again to their beginning and final original state, which may be understood by way of comparison from this **Reduction** of the so-called **condemned Salt** <term>🜔</term> or dead ashes. One should take these leached ashes and put them into a durable vessel, and let them be well **calcined** <note>Reduced to powder through high heat.</note> with a strong **Fire** <term>🜂</term>, yet sealed; after a certain time, open your vessel, and you will find your ash salty once more. Leach it well, and you will again obtain a little Salt; weigh your ash, and it will already be lighter than when you put it in to be calcined; thus continue with patience, and you will in truth reduce it perfectly to **Solar Salt** <term>☉</term>. This **Reduction** will show the lover of Divine Mysteries, by way of comparison, how one day after the determined times of the eternities, through the **Fire** <term>🜂</term> of the righteous vengeance of the living **GOD**, all damnation and hellish dark dross, and the dark **Fire** <term>🜂</term> of Hell itself, must be calcined and consumed back into a living, shining **Solar Salt** <term>☉</term>, appearing once more in its first luster and glory. Whether a shorter path in Nature to accomplish such is known to the Sages than the one recounted above, we do not doubt in the least; just as we could have provided a clearer demonstration here through **charcoal**, since the same is through and through a pure **Fire** <term>🜂</term>, and further a pure **Solar Salt** <term>☉</term>; but we have found this unnecessary, for whoever knows the **fiery Fire** <term>🜂</term> or **watery Water** <term>🜄</term> in which all things melt back into their very own first essence, also knows that all of this is perfectly true. What we now prove here in the **Vegetable Kingdom** is also to be perfectly understood of the **Animal Kingdom**. And so we come further to the very hard knot, in the loosening of which one's teeth far too easily become blunt, namely to the **Mineral Kingdom** <note>original: *Regno Minerali*</note>. To avoid all prolixity, we shall set ourselves to the firmest and most perfect Body of **Gold**; yet know, you who have never made or seen anything right, that you shall not foolishly imitate this, even though it is described clearly and truthfully enough.
Take [℟] fine **Gold** <term>☉</term>, amalgamate it with well-purified **Mercury** <term>☿</term>; draw the **Mercury** <term>☿</term> away from it again very gently; then calcine the **Gold** <term>☉</term> gently in a **hidden Fire** <term>🜂</term>; then amalgamate it with fresh **Mercury** <term>☿</term>; draw it off again; calcine it again as before; then amalgamate it again with the distilled **Mercury** <term>☿</term>; and rub it every time as well as possible in a mortar, and let it digest for 24 hours every time before distillation; draw the **Mercury** <term>☿</term> off again gently, and thus you must proceed with the work, and complete the hidden **Calcination** well, until your **Gold** <term>☉</term> is not only brought to an incomprehensible powder, but also to the point where it has become entirely **irreducible** <note>In this context, meaning it cannot be easily melted back into metallic gold, having been transformed into its salt-form.</note>; then it is in the **proximate form of Salt** <note>original: *proxima forma Salis*</note>. If you then further know the right **Water** <term>🜄</term>, sharpened with its own **Salt**, the wonderful heart-strengthener and **Drink of Life** <note>A reference to the *Aurum Potabile* or Drinkable Gold, a universal medicine.</note>, then you can in truth very soon place this Gold-powder or Salt before the eyes of all the world in an actual and essential **Solar** <term>☉</term> form. The work is truthful...

### CHAPTER I.

...as all those who have ever recognized the Truth will acknowledge it as such. Now, as far as **Minerals** are concerned, 
<margin>Letter $\alpha$.</margin>
the work is quite easily completed through a simple **Calcination** <note>The process of heating a substance to high temperatures in air to achieve decomposition or oxidation.</note>, though one not achievable by just anyone. He who has perfectly understood us in the preceding discourse 
<margin>Letter A.</margin>
will have no further doubt in this matter. One must know, however: if one can maintain **Common Salt** $\ominus$ in the smelting-fire for its proper time, and then knows how to make it volatile *per viam naturae* <note>original: "by the way of nature"</note> through the **waters of the seas** in which it was primordially generated—and which are indeed the Salt $\ominus$ itself—then he has certainly achieved something worthy of praise. He shall have a **Water and no Water** $\nabla$, a **Salt and no Salt** $\ominus$, both a Water $\nabla$ and a Salt $\ominus$ without taste. It remains, therefore, to speak a little concerning **Stones**. These are likewise treated through **calcination** and leached, as taught on page 17. He to whom the aforementioned **sweet Salt-Water** is known will attain this more easily. Whoever has understood the first part will also more easily grasp our meaning here, especially if one has well understood us in the entire discourse regarding the **weaving and texture of metals**. 

The seeker and lover of Divine Truth will also be guided to understand (of which we spoke a little on page 15 at the "NB") how and in what manner, one day through the ages of eternities, the **hellish Fire-slag** $\triangle$ and the separated, glowing but not shining subterranean realm—namely the **hellish Fire** $\triangle$ and all its offspring and spirits, together with their habitation of the barren, dead, tasteless earths, as the true bonds of Hell, Death, and Damnation—shall be again **solved, purified, and overturned** through the **Salt** $\ominus$ of Hell and the clear waters of the lovely sea of still, quiet eternities. This is so that they may once again be and shine as **spiritual Gold and Silver** (of which we have already spoken extensively above), namely the lovely, indestructible, shining **Body of the bright Dawn of the Beginning.** <note>Welling here references the "Morgenröthe" or Aurora, a concept popularized by the mystic Jacob Boehme, referring to the spiritual rebirth of the cosmos.</note>

But stay! Here we wander from our purpose into a matter that does not satisfy or make sense to every spirit and mind; therefore we return to where we deviated. We remind ourselves, before some **busybody** <note>original: "Naseweiser," a know-it-all or one who sticks their nose into things.</note> plucks us by the sleeve: since all Bodies can be reduced into a **Salt** $\ominus$, can the beautiful **precious stones**, held in such hard bonds (which have not been mentioned until now), also be transformed into Salt $\ominus$ in this way? To them we answer: even if it were unknown to you and us, it is nevertheless an eternal and certain truth. Moreover, one need only consider their **glassiness**; and he who knows what glass is and what it consists of will easily be able to acquiesce in this point. 

However, it would not be strictly necessary to set down here how to manage their **reduction into Salt** $\ominus$, even if one knew it. He who knows the powers, ability, and strength of the **Sun**, **Fire**, and **Salt**, as well as **Water** $\nabla$, will, we believe, easily accomplish this. For they are composed exactly like all other bodies, only they are coagulated, as it were, unmixed in their first purity; and of these, the bright ones are **Lunar** $\unicode{x263D}$ offspring, while the colored ones are **Solar** <note>original: "Orische," from "Aurum" or "Orient," referring to Gold/Sun.</note> offspring, all of which has been clearly and extensively proven above. 

It should also be remembered that the **Solution and Reduction** of the most constant and solid bodies, such as **Gold and Diamonds**, into Salt $\ominus$ could be taught more clearly through a special path of dissolved **Solar and Lunar Sulfur** <note>original: "Orischen und Crischen Schweffels." Solar (Gold) and Lunar (Silver/Crystalline).</note>. We do not wish to gloss over this withholding with the common excuses of all the **Sophists**, as if one wanted to hide it from the unworthy; no, in truth, that is not our reason. Rather, our cause is this: we know through certain experiments that something would be taught by such experiments through which **singular evil** could occur. With this, then, this point shall be concluded.

§. 33. We come now to the final point, namely that **Fire** $\triangle$ **is also truly Water** $\nabla$, and vice-versa. Whoever has well understood the previously demonstrated principles will be able to grasp this completely without any further proof. For first, it is sufficiently known to even the most simple-minded that all **Salts** [Salia] can easily be brought to the point where they dissolve into a water in the open air <note>original: "im Tufft," referring to the moisture in the atmosphere.</note>. But this is not enough for the wise, for this **Salt-Water** $\ominus \nabla$ is entirely reducible; therefore a completely different proof is necessary. Namely, that **Salt** $\ominus$ in all its species must be brought to the point that it becomes its own **primordial Water** $\nabla$, from which—through common cooking-fire $\triangle$, according to the method of the **kitchen-scullions** <note>original: "Sudelköche," a derogatory term for unphilosophical chemists who only follow recipes without understanding.</note>— 
<margin>Letter $\Gamma$, No Art</margin>
no Salt $\ominus$ is again to be separated. This is easy for a tireless Chemist to do, if he knows how to **calcine** his Salt $\ominus$ (of whatever species it may be) to the right degree, and how to make it volatile in its own **Lunar Water** $\nabla$. All of this is not possible for everyone to reduce back into a Salt $\ominus$ so easily. 

Yet we have brought it to that point more than once, and these simple (though somewhat laborious) works yield a **Menstruum** <note>A solvent, often considered the "universal solvent" in alchemy.</note> which is in truth 
<margin>Letter $\delta$.</margin>
worthy of more consideration than the fools can believe; this will be treated more extensively in the following chapter. Who can now deny us that **Fire** $\triangle$ **is a Water** $\nabla$, or that Water $\nabla$ is a Fire $\triangle$? The same applies to Gold and Silver, as well as the **Universal Mercury** $\unicode{x263F}$. Thus, the points on page 4, letter k, and page 5, letter p, are sufficiently satisfied. 

The wise man will therefore have to fully admit that the **Beginning and the End** are nothing other than **Salt** $\ominus$, which is **Fire** $\triangle$; and that the mother of this Salt is **Water** $\nabla$. Thus it follows irrefragably that the essence first created by **GOD the Lord** was a **Water** $\nabla$, which has a **Fire** $\triangle$ in its innermost part, namely **Salt** $\ominus$. Concerning these three principles—**Salt** $\ominus$, **Sulfur** $\unicode{x1F70D}$, and **Mercury** $\unicode{x263F}$—many have filled the world with great chatter...

# CHAPTER II.
## ON THE NATURE OF SALTS.

### §. I.

<margin>Letter H.</margin>
**T**his chapter might not have been strictly necessary, as everything was fully explained in the preceding one; however, so that we may not be accused of promising something we could not uphold with Divine Light, we wish to show that Eternal Wisdom has not abandoned us in this place either. It was stated above in §. 3 that the figure of common Salt (⊖) is cubic, which is the <term>figure of earthly bodies</term> <note>original: "figura corporum terrestrium"</note>, and it acquired this quality while passing through the earth.
<margin>Letter Θ.</margin>
In that same paragraph, it is said that its form is <term>diaphanous</term> <note>meaning transparent or translucent</note> or see-through, just like glass.
<margin>Letter I.</margin>
Also, that it is fusible and fluid, and passes through all bodies with great ease.
<margin>Letter K.</margin>
In the same place: its taste is sour and slightly astringent.
<margin>Letter Λ.</margin>
In the same place: it is of a drying nature and property.
<margin>Letter M.</margin>
Cooling.
<margin>Letter N.</margin>
And in that same place: that in its innermost core, it is an essential Fire (△).

Regarding the first point (H), all common physicians, pharmacists, and distillers know this to be true beyond all dispute: namely, that common Salt (⊖) has a cubic figure, and the more it is purified from <term>heterogeneous substances</term> <note>foreign or impure materials not belonging to the primary substance</note>, the more beautiful and perfect this figure becomes. However, this cannot be given to it by common Water (🜄), but it must be bathed, washed, and purified in its own Water (🜄). This is not a task for everyone, but where one knows how to accomplish it, one obtains a very lovely and sweetish Salt (⊖) which will possess the aforementioned figure <term>most perfectly</term> <note>original: "perfectissime"</note>. <margin>Letter Ξ.</margin> He who further knows how to handle Mercury (☿) well, and how to dissolve the salt into itself and make it entirely volatile, and then bring it to crystallize again, can boast that he has seen the miracle Mercury of nature, or the mystery of <term>Feathered Alum</term> <note>original: "Alumen Plumosum," a term often used for asbestos or a specific crystalline mineral salt</note>, the Universal in dry form. But how few there are who walk this simple path according to nature and strive for this jewel! For the work seems too simple and lowly to them, yet it remains hidden and too laborious; it requires great patience and a fundamental knowledge of true dissolution, for without these parts it cannot be accomplished. It was said above, and remains held as an <term>axiom</term> <note>a self-evident truth</note>, that all bodies may be brought back to their first beginning through the very same path they took in their manifestation, growth, and multiplication. He who has understood us well therein will be able to complete this here. Thus it remains certain that the true figure of this earthly Salt (⊖) is cubic, which the unbeliever can learn through the right trial. For just as the globe is the fastest and most mobile body, so the cube—which is the <term>figure of gravity</term> <note>original: "Figura gravitatis"</note>—is the heaviest and most immobile. (a) In this cubic figure, yet another and far greater mystery is hidden, which will be dealt with in Chapter 3, "On the Use of Salt (⊖)," where we speak of the Magical and Cabalistic characters of Nature. This cubic figure now indicates that the Sun (☉) has undergone a new birth in the Earth as an alien mother; for if we know how to separate this Sun (☉) well from its heterogeneous parts, we shall find that this corporeality arose from the <term>Virgin Earth</term> <note>original: "Terra virginea," referring to primordial, uncorrupted matter</note> (the drifting sand). (b) If one dissolves Salt (⊖) through the path of nature and makes it volatile, and then fixes it again, its cubic figure is separated from it; for the delicate drifting sand that causes this figure is dissolved in this operation back to its first principle—into which, as mentioned above, all other pebbles and stones may be resolved. Then it is the most secret <term>Salt of Feathered Alum</term> <note>original: "Sal Alumen Plumosum secretissimum"</note>, as it is otherwise called, about which much can be read and attached above. For the drifting sand adheres to all spiritual bodies as a basis for their tangibility. What the drifting sand is and from whence its birth comes has already been demonstrated in detail above, and could be explained even more clearly at this point. Also, that Water (🜄) is an offspring of the Night, or of the Stars and the Moon (☾), and the Earth is a birth of the Sun (☉) in the Waters; this is clear in itself, and the wise man will demand nothing further from us on this. Only the inexperienced, yet truth-seeking, will stand in need of further clarification. Such a person should know that all births of every corporeal and spiritual thing, until its perfection or <term>predestined end</term> <note>original: "fine prædestinato"</note>, are twofold: first, a spiritual conception from the upper lights, and second, the bodily birthing from the earthly Water (🜄) and Fire (△), which were initially an offspring or separation of the first-created spirits toward the waters. <margin>Letter Π.</margin> Thus it is clear and understandable that the drifting sand does not fall from <term>Heaven</term> <note>original Hebrew: "Schamajim"</note> in such a tangible and material way, but is perfected and wrought down here. For the first [the blessed] are purified through Baptism and the Lord’s Supper of Jesus Christ and His holy death from all bitterness and earthly <term>heterogeneities</term> <note>foreign or impure substances</note>, and henceforth can live in no other way than in their Principle—that is, in holy, sweet, still contentment. But the others, lacking this holy purification and separation, remain in gravity, bitterness, and thus in material form; therefore, they are entirely unfit for the aforementioned state and must remain subject to all the suffering of the <term>secondary qualities</term> <note>sensory properties like weight or texture</note> and their sensitivity, according to the **Gospel of Jesus Christ**, until the appointed times of purification, through the Fire (🜂) of God’s righteous vengeance. However, how this separation, inversion, and locking away occurred, and how this very nature, with all its first-born offspring, was thus turned inward upon the fallen spirits—remaining unchanged in figure <note>original: "secundum figuram"</note> but altered in form <note>original: "secundum formam"</note> only through harsh bitterness or contraction—and how it remains the dwelling place of the fallen spirits, these are unfathomable mysteries of Him who is without beginning of days or end of times, and remains from eternity to eternity.

<margin>Letter P.</margin>

This single created being nevertheless consists of three distinct things, yet in their substance all three are only of one kind, and each is for itself exactly what they otherwise are all three together—each for itself in particular. We have thus proven from whence the cubic figure of common cooking Salt (🜔) arose. 

<margin>Letter Σ.</margin>

§. 3. Letter I. page 20. **Let the Salt <note>symbol: 🜔</note> be flowing and liquid, and let it penetrate all bodies:** First, everyone knows that it is flowing and liquid, and it requires no proof. The second point, however—that it penetrates all bodies—is known to all who are practiced in chemistry. For either it penetrates all metals, minerals, and stones, by whatever names they may be called, in the melting fire <note>symbol: 🜂</note>, as it also does to all animals and vegetables; or its Water <note>symbol: 🜄</note> or Spirit performs this. In short, no body is so dense or compact that Salt would not dissolve, divide, or penetrate it. This is because, as proven before, it is the origin and beginning of all bodies; therefore, this is possible for Salt <note>symbol: 🜔</note> alone and no other being. Salt <note>symbol: 🜔</note> also divides or penetrates metals, even the most fixed and perfect—namely, Gold <note>symbol: ☉</note> itself. <margin>Letter γ.</margin>

This <term>Enixum</term> <note>A Latin term for a "neutral salt"; here used to describe a substance that has reached a state of perfect balance</note>, however, is known to so very few that this is the cause of so many wants and infirmities among men. He will see how the stinking, dark, glowing—yet not illuminating—hellish coals or Fire <note>The symbol here is a sulfur variant, a triangle with a cross below.</note>, with all their spiritual offspring and damnable works, shall be overturned. Through the wondrous Salt <note>symbol: 🜔</note>, Jesus Christ, the Savior of all the world—who earned eternal redemption and who was begotten from eternity in the unfathomable sea of all the mercy of God the Father—they shall be purified again and brought back to their first pure, shining essence and glory. (these come immediately from the sand-forming waters E. (These matrices are baked or drawn together by the subterranean fire-rays, the quicksand, and their necessary Salt-water in harshness and severity, serving this or that metal, as proven in the first chapter.) # CHAPTER II.

...consist of Salt <note>symbol: 🜔</note> and Fire <note>symbol: 🜂</note>, so that all of these are but one, namely Gold <note>symbol: ☉</note>, which is Fire <note>symbol: 🜂</note> and Water <note>symbol: 🜄</note>; we do not wish to repeat this here, as it has been more than sufficiently demonstrated and proven in the first part and the beginning of this chapter. Its drying nature, therefore, can be denied by none but the fools who do not know the grades of nature. We also see this drying nature in embalmed bodies, salted meat, etc., how it draws everything together and hardens it. Furthermore, it is an unshakeable and undoubted principle, as was discussed on page 17, Letter x, concerning the concentration or shaping of the "liquor of wood" through and upon its own salt. Can there be any further doubt, since one can dry a spiritual Water <note>symbol: 🜄</note>, draw it together, and make it bodily? And all this is done merely through Salt <note>symbol: 🜔</note> and Fire <note>symbol: 🜂</note>, which is also Salt <note>symbol: 🜔</note>, so that this point also has its correctness.

§. 6. Letter M. page 20. **Salt <note>symbol: 🜔</note> is Cooling.** Regarding this, there are almost as many opinions as there are scholars in the world; yet it would be necessary to prove it with such reasons as could be understood even by the simple-minded. He who can achieve this through Fire <note>symbol: 🜂</note> and a refined Water <note>symbol: 🜄</note> <note>original: "enixum"</note> comes closer to the goal. Yet both ways are correct, though known not to all sloppy-cooks and coal-murderers <note term="coal-murderers">A derogatory term for amateur or "puffing" alchemists who wasted time and fuel without understanding the true spiritual or chemical principles.</note>, but only to the true Alchemists. When it is thus prepared, its power can be felt, and its effect is nothing other than a lifting of the disorder of the harmony of the Microcosm <note>The human body as a reflection of the universe.</note> and a pacifying of the *Archaeus* <note term="Archaeus">In Paracelsian medicine, the vital force or "internal chemist" that governs the health of the body.</note>. Thus, this Water <note>symbol: 🜄</note> is the true Water from the Paradisiacal stream, Pison. # ON THE NATURE OF SALTS.

...as if torn away, and we shall reserve this matter for the lovers of the secrets of God until a further opportunity, which the LORD GOD may further grant us abundantly for His wonderful knowledge and the praise of His glory. It was said at the beginning of this section that the Salt <note>symbol: 🜔</note> must be entirely purified from its heterogeneous material <note>original: heterogeneo; referring to foreign or impure substances mixed within a compound</note>; then it becomes cooling <margin>Mercury <note>symbol: ☿</note> B</margin>, for thus it became the cool Moon <note>symbol: ☽; original: Lunae</note>, concerning which see Chapter I, page 3 and following, especially page 6 under the letter W, where water concentrated through a mirror is discussed. This Salt <note>symbol: 🜔</note> is then also an excellent cooling agent in all hot diseases; it heals all dropsy and Tartaric diseases <note>A term in Paracelsian medicine referring to ailments caused by "tartar" or stony precipitates in the body, such as gout or stones</note> to the root <note>original: radicitus</note>, but it is sought by few and found by even fewer; and this is proof enough of its true cooling, which yet in its center is nothing else.

§. 7. Letter N. page 20. As an essential fire, which has already been demonstrated more than enough in the first chapter, it is not necessary to mention even a single syllable further; rather, those who desire the truth must seek further there. We only wish to speak a little more here with those who have invented the different degrees of heat and cold, and other such groundless follies, and ask how this can possibly be reconciled: that a thing can be at once and at the same time essential cold and also at once and at the same time essential heat in the simplest yet strongest degree <note>original: in ſimpliciſſimo doch fortiſſimo gradu</note>?

The intelligent person, who has well noted and understood all our previous discourses—especially where we proved that the Almighty GOD created only one very simple Being after His most holy likeness, which is yet at once and at the same time three kinds of things, namely Salt <note>symbol: 🜔</note>, Fire <note>symbol: 🜂</note>, and Water <note>symbol: 🜄</note>—will perfectly understand this distinction of heat and cold. For he will then certainly know that the Salt <note>symbol: 🜔</note> is an essential Fire <note>symbol: 🜂</note>, which Fire <note>symbol: 🜂</note> or Salt <note>symbol: 🜔</note> can be made spiritual and volatile by the way of nature <note>original: per viam naturae</note> and transformed into its primaterial cooling water, whose innermost heat and Fire <note>symbol: 🜂</note> is namely Light; thus the 6th section of Chapter 2 is proven once again. It will now be clear enough to the intelligent what the situation is with the essential heat and cold of Salt <note>symbol: 🜔</note>; thus Salt <note>symbol: 🜔</note> would be described recognizably enough according to its nature <note>original: ſecundum naturam ſuam</note>, so that we believe nothing more can be required concerning this.

§. 8. We will therefore proceed to speak of the mineral and other Salts <note>symbol: 🜔</note> as much as the Divine light has granted us, to see whether we might succeed through this blessed light <margin>Mercury <note>symbol: ☿</note> C</margin> in satisfying the intelligent and truth-seeking person in this as well. We will therefore have to make a <margin>a</margin> beginning again from common Salt <note>symbol: 🜔</note>, how it is the basis of all known and unknown salts, and how from it all salts, whatever their names may be, can be <margin>b</margin> made and produced.

§. 9. That common salt is the ground and basis of all known and unknown salts is certainly not doubted by experienced chemists and miners; yet for certain reasons, we must demonstrate this somewhat clearly and distinctly. We will first take <margin>#.P</margin> Saltpeter <note>symbol: ⊕; original: Nitrum</note>, as it is not the least among those in which this can be proven. To accomplish this (though let the lazy person, who might come upon this and read it, have no hope that we will chew the food for him and also put it in his mouth), take from the niter all its volatile acidic particles <note>original: particulas acidas Volatiles</note> through fire, as these are the sole cause why common Salt <note>symbol: 🜔</note> has become an Earth <note>symbol: ♁; likely referring to the fixed, mineral state of antimony or earth</note>. This latter you can perform through a precipitation of the fixed universal volatile acid <note>original: acidi volatilis Univerſ.</note>, if you know it correctly, and a certain pure mineral Sal Ammoniac <note>symbol: ✳</note>, for common Sal Ammoniac <note>symbol: ✳</note> is too impure and stinking for this. This work is indeed true, though we well believe that not everyone will understand us so easily and simply—especially in the last operation of taking away its alkaline quality and giving back its earthly acidity—but those who are a little experienced with fire will understand us all the more easily. Particularly those who have thoroughly understood our discourse on page 14, section 28. Concerning Alum, it can likewise be helped through the <margin>EX</margin> above-mentioned precipitation to make a perfect kitchen-salt from it. Yet this can also happen through another mastery, namely by taking away its accidental acidity and making it entirely sweet, which is indeed known to everyone; afterwards, it is easily transformed into a common Salt <note>symbol: 🜔</note>, though this latter is not known to every distiller. But so that we may better satisfy the lover of Divine truth and the secrets placed by Him into nature, we will set down a fundamental rule which should be enough for those who are practiced even a little in nature through fire. Namely: all salts, whatever names they may have, when they have been turned back from their last degree and alkalized, one should dissolve them in common water <note>symbol: 🜄</note> and then let this lye run through Gold <note>symbol: ☉; though often the sun, here likely the metal or a solar vessel</note>; then boil this down as is customary, and you will obtain a Salt <note>symbol: 🜔</note> perfectly equal to common salt in everything, except that it still tastes a little urinous or like lye. To take this away from it as well, one only needs to dissolve it once more in common Salt <note>symbol: 🜔</note> and add a little ferment of common Salt <note>symbol: 🜔</note>, and filter and thicken it three times, and it is brought to perfection. This description is somewhat too simple for the overly clever and wise, but he who loves the truth will recognize it with all the more thanks, as something great is hidden within it. How the conversion from its last degree back to alkalization proceeds, we will likewise make known through a proof, namely through Vitriol—take whichever one you wish—and dissolve it in common water and let it boil up quickly over the fire <note>symbol: 🜂</note>, and in the...
