### CHAPTER II.

While it is boiling, one should throw in as much <term>Vitriol</term> <note>symbol: 🜖; a sulfate mineral, often green or blue</note> as will be enough. One will find, after it has been given a few more boils and then removed from the fire and left to stand quietly, that all the sludge and impurity have separated from it. This otherwise cannot happen, even if it were dissolved, filtered, and coagulated many thousands of times. If one repeats this operation a second and third time, it is brought to the highest perfection and is uncommonly magnificent for the work of <term>Fire</term> <note>symbol: 🜂</note>. Whether this vitriol be "veneric" <note>copper-based</note>, "martial" <note>iron-based</note>, or of another metal, it nonetheless possesses in its innermost being its <term>heterogeneous part</term> <note>original: partem heterogeneam; foreign or impure substances</note>, the <term>essential nature of its metal</term> <note>original: Essentiale metalli sui</note>, from which the <term>Salt</term> <note>symbol: 🜔</note> has become such a vitriol. To take this away from it as well, we wish to further make the way known.

<margin>§ 3 H</margin>
Take as much <term>Common Salt</term> <note>symbol: 🜔 commune</note> as you wish; let it flow well in a good and sturdy vessel. Into this, carry as much Sal Ammoniac <note>original: Sal Armoniac</note> as it can take while still remaining fluid. Now, if the vitriol was copper-based, take that which is the opposite of Copper <note>original: Veneri</note> and is otherwise well known, and add as much of it as the mass can absorb, with a little more <term>Sal Ammoniac</term> <note>symbol: 🜹</note>. Let it all flow into a thick mass. Then dissolve the vitriol in an iron kettle, and for every 1 pound of it, take about <term>2 ounces</term> <note>symbol: ℥ ij</note> of this mass, pulverized. Throw it in while it is boiling and let it boil for about 7 or 8 minutes. Then let it cool and settle, and further filter and thicken it. Thus the vitriol is perfectly purified of its metal-based impurities and is <term>very nearly</term> <note>original: in proximo</note> common salt, and can be perfectly transformed into it by the ways previously indicated. Had it been a vitriol of Iron <note>original: Martis</note>, the operation would have had to take place in a copper vessel, and instead of the aforementioned substance, the opposite of Iron would have to be melted into the mass. This precipitation, as simple as it may appear, nonetheless holds a great secret behind it, and the understanding person will see from this how the delicate metal must be precipitated from the rich vitriols containing gold and silver, and how one must prepare the precipitates for it. This is certainly not the least of the secrets and will provide perfect guidance, both in the wet and dry ways, on how the essential separation of metals must proceed.

<margin>Z. J.</margin>
And while we are dealing here with precipitates, it will be no less useful to speak a few words regarding the improvement of low-quality <term>Brine</term> <note>original: Sohle; salt-saturated water from a spring</note>. This can likewise be utilized with great advantage through a precipitate. However, one must first take great care to have thoroughly investigated the properties of the brine. For even the common man knows that no brine is found on earth that is entirely simple or <term>homogeneous</term> <note>uniform in composition</note>. Rather, however it is found, it has taken on this or that quality in its passage through subterranean passages, from this or that metal or mineral which it most strongly encountered. This must first be properly examined. For the most part, this is a mixture of all <term>Gold</term> <note>symbol: ☉; the Sun</note> or its brine: namely, vitriolic, aluminous, nitrous, and also mixed

<margin>z. k.</margin>
with acid particles from the <term>volatile fixed thing</term> <note>original: ex volatili fixato</note>. If the brine now has only one of these qualities, it is easy to help; but if two or three properties surround it at once, one must adjust the precipitate accordingly. We once encountered a brine in a well-known place

<margin>§ L</margin>
which consisted of the <term>prime saline essence</term> (which we call <term>Common Salt</term> <note>symbol: 🜔 commune</note>), as well as a delicate vitriol. These qualities were taken from the very rich yet volatile copper-sand which covers the "roof-coals" of that place and also runs beneath them, as well as from the veins themselves in which the salt-springs are contained, which consist of a stone full of volatile silver-containing copper. Thirdly, it consisted of a volatile acid or niter, which latter quality it possesses <term>immediately</term> from the influences of heaven through the power of its own internal magnet (from its <term>primary saline quality</term>). To help this brine, we

After the clarification, the precipitation was made from 3 parts <term>Mercury</term> <note>symbol: ☿</note>, 2 parts <term>Tin</term> <note>symbol: ♃</note>, and 1 ½ or 2 parts <term>Lead</term> <note>symbol: ♄</note> or <term>Antimony</term> <note>symbol: ♁</note>. Then a little <term>Sal Ammoniac</term> <note>symbol: 🜹</note> must also be added. We let all this flow into a mass and scattered 1 pound, or even ½ pound, of it into a clarified pan full of brine that was boiling. We let it boil thoroughly with it, and thus there precipitated from the raw brine (which had been obtained with bucket and rope and was full of "wild water") an exceedingly magnificent, white, and durable salt with good profit. No moisture could harm it, but it became like a stone and surpassed all other known cooking salts in pleasantness and sharpness. In later times, however, when the workers of that same mine, to whom this technique had not been revealed, endeavored to maintain their profit through <term>graduation</term> <note>the process of concentrating salt brine by evaporation, often using large brushwood towers</note>, their brine was indeed found to be "graduated" and strong enough. But because the lovely <term>volatile acid</term>—namely the <term>solar male seed</term>—had been released and taken away from it in the <term>Fire</term> <note>symbol: 🜂</note> through the graduation, it could remain as nothing other than a moist <term>Lunar</term> <note>silver-related or moisture-prone</note> <term>Salt</term> <note>symbol: 🜔</note>. On the other hand, through our precipitation, both were so inseparably linked and united with one another through their own fixed **MAGNET** that it attained the highest perfection. This is now the way to the precipitation of those brines mixed with foreign qualities, as well as poor brines. However, the artist must know how to direct himself herein according to the qualities or <term>mixed heterogeneous particles</term> <note>original: particulis heterogeneis immixtis</note>, as this entire section will teach him; otherwise his effort is in vain. ### ON THE NATURE OF SALTS.

...are unhealthy, unless their quality were completely tested and not overcome by the <term>common salt</term> <note>original: Sale communi</note>, or if the vitriolic and aluminous qualities arose from weathered and dead metals and minerals; in such cases, they are highly harmful and unhealthy in the body when they are boiled down in the common way. Furthermore, for the sake of the scrupulous, we must offer a reminder of how and in what manner we wish the word "precipitation" to be understood here. Namely, we do not mean such a <margin>Reason for precipitation</margin> settling where the <term>salt</term> <note>symbol: 🜔</note> immediately falls completely to the bottom and leaves the water in the pan standing entirely freed above it, so that one could immediately strike it from the bottom of the <margin>SP</margin> pan into the baskets without further effort. Rather, the lover of truth should know that the <term>lunar salt</term> <note>salt associated with the moon or silver</note> is so inwardly united with the water that they cannot be separated by boiling except with great difficulty, as can be seen more perfectly at the end of Chapter I under letter z and in other places. Indeed, during the boiling itself, the exhaling <term>water</term> <note>symbol: 🜄</note> acts as a <term>matrix</term> <note>a womb or life-giving source</note> and takes the noblest parts—namely the male <term>Ouric seed</term> <note>original: Orischen Saamen; likely referring to the solar or gold-like essence</note>—away with it. Therefore, our precipitation is nothing other than a complete separation of these heavenly influences (the salts) from the matrix, namely from the water with which it is so intimately united; for as all our <margin>QY</margin> previous discourses have proven, they are both of one origin and are one in their innermost being. Thus, the actual effect of this precipitation is the complete separation of the salt from the water, so that the water steams away more quickly and easily, leaving the salt perfectly beautiful, hard, and well-qualified behind. This prevents even the slightest bit from escaping in the delicate water-vapor, as if in a <term>sulfur</term> <note>symbol: 🜍</note>, whereby one requires much less wood and time for true boiling and obtains a much more magnificent and far greater quantity of salt than could otherwise be obtained in any other way or manner. We have had to report this so clearly and in such detail for the pleasure of the enthusiasts and for the sake of many useful reflections. Furthermore, we do not want to forget to mention <margin>Regarding salt, see page 23, Letter ψ.</margin> that the salt springs which consist of <term>primitive salt</term> and the <term>fixed volatile acid</term> are the noblest and best of all. These are found in many places in Germany, especially in Austria, Bavaria, and Switzerland, and other mountainous places. This should now be sufficient regarding the reduction of all salts to a common salt, yet we wish to repeat and confirm this again with these few words for the sake of abundance: namely, one should <term>alkalize</term> <note>convert into an alkaline or basic substance</note> all salts, whatever their names may be, and solve this alkali in common water through <term>vitriol</term> <note>symbol: 🜊</note>, then thicken it, solve it again with common water, and <term>ferment</term> it with <term>saline common water</term>; then thicken it again according to common practice, and one will grasp the truth with one's own hands. Or, if the artist can <term>volatilize</term> this alkali <margin>Page 8</margin> by the way of nature and ferment it with the acid of a certain sulfur, he has practiced the art even better; however, we believe this will probably not be understood by everyone. Thus, this point on page 2, letter a, as well as the end of section 5 on page 2, Chapter I, is sufficiently explained, so that the intelligent man will have nothing more to desire.

**§. 10.** That from the same [common salt], all salts, by whatever names they may be called, can be made and produced (page 2, letter b), requires little proof for the experienced; it is also well known enough to all ruined laboratory workers that one can prepare a perfect vitriol with common salt and copper or iron, or another metal or mineral. (Which clearly proves what was said above on page 16, letter v, <margin>Sulfur T</margin> namely that <term>sulfur</term> and <term>vitriol</term> are two entirely different things; for what sulfur is, we have already proven above with irrefutable reasons). In this process, the metallic particles unite themselves as if inseparably with the salt, and thus from the sour salt, by reason of the metallic particles, a salt is formed and nothing else, which differs widely enough from sulfur. This discrepancy is also shown by the heavy <term>oil of salt</term> <note>original: Oleum; likely hydrochloric acid</note>, which at its foundation is nothing other than a <term>spirit of salt</term> concentrated upon metallic particles, just as a similar very heavy and very hot oil can be prepared from common salt if the spirit of common salt is concentrated upon <term>calamine stone</term> <note>original: Lapide Calaminari; zinc ore</note>. Furthermore, let the lover of truth purify the salt as taught in the previous section of this chapter and then distill an oil from it; he will experience and realize even more clearly what we have proven here. He will then obtain not a thick, red, corrosive oil, but a clear, lovely oil. Thus, what was promised on page 14, letter n, is sufficiently proven: that common salt, melted in due proportion with <term>antimony</term> <note>symbol: 🜨</note> and fermented with nitre-like substances (but better with a little <term>spirit of nitre</term> <note>original: Spiritu Nitri; nitric acid</note>), can be transformed into a perfect salt in a very short and quick time; almost everyone knows this. However, we are aware of another manual trick in this matter by which such a thing can be performed even faster and better. ### CHAPTER II.

...Alkali, and from these in turn a <term>common salt</term> <note>original symbol: $\ominus$</note> can be prepared; in short, <note>original: mutatis mutandis & additamentis necessariis</note> with the necessary changes and additions, one can make from common salt whatever genus of salts one desires. An intelligent person will have perfectly perceived this from what was recounted above; however, he who cannot understand us from this will not be helped even if everything were set down here in the clearest possible way. We shall therefore let it rest with what has been said so far, and believe that we have fully satisfied the second point, Letter b, page 2. For although much more could be said of this matter and many more experiments could be brought forward, such would only be a tedious expansion, as we have dealt with it quite redundantly from the beginning until now.

<margin>11/ε</margin>
Indeed, it is enough that we have proven from the beginning until now, with irrefutable reasons, that everything has proceeded from or was created by one—namely, from GOD—in a completely simple being according to His likeness. From this simple being, or the outflow of Divine Majesty, all creatures and created things, however they may be found or named, were again created and procreated through His Almighty Will and Word. This includes the most holy angels as well as the most abominable devils (namely, in their first pure and holy radiance, before their presumption, rebelliousness, and pride), as well as all other creatures and beings, however they are found—whether in our opinion they are good or evil, lovely or loathsome. It matters not; let it be understood or taken however it will, it is once and for all true—indeed, an eternal Divine Truth—that outside the living GOD there is nothing but His eternal Omnipotence and the depth of His eternal, unsearchable glory. From this, everything has proceeded that is in heaven and on earth or in the waters below, even in the very center of Hell. Namely, all was good and glorious in its beginning, but became abominable, corrupted, and contrary through the fall of the powerful evil spirit, Lucifer, as well as the evil will of fallen man. Thus, this too is and remains an eternal truth, in defiance of the devils and all their creatures and evil men: namely, that all things may be brought back again to their first perfection. This is what true Philosophy perfectly teaches. For if one considers what kind of medicine can be prepared from the oft-mentioned red and white <term>Fire</term> <note>original symbol: $\triangle$</note> of nature (by those who rightly know this Principle), which has nothing higher above it than the eternal medicine of the soul—the most holy flesh and blood of JESUS Christ—which brings everything imperfect and corrupted in the physical human body to perfect health, so that in tangible nature there is no disease or weakness that this glorious and powerful medicine should not have the power to heal perfectly.

Now, if such a small part (which is nonetheless nothing other than a <term>fixed Gold</term> <note>original symbol: $\odot$</note>, an essential, imperishable <term>Fire</term> <note>original symbol: $\triangle$</note>) can perfectly heal such cruel diseases in so swift a time and turn them back to health, how much more will the <term>Salt</term> <note>original symbol: $\ominus$</note> of the eternal Divine Mercy of JESUS Christ—the Sustainer of life and Savior of all creatures—transmute all that is corrupted in the whole of nature, both of men and fallen spirits without any distinction? Yet each part, according to the eternities appointed to them, shall be transmuted back to original perfection in the righteous vengeance-<term>Fire</term> <note>original symbol: $\triangle$</note> of the living God. For what has gone out from GOD must return to GOD and cannot remain separated; otherwise, it would be a partitioned part of GOD, and thus the Divine Being would not remain in His eternal, unsearchable, and incomprehensible whole Circle. Since in GOD there is nothing but a simple being in the highest perfection, and not two contrary qualities (<note>original: qualitates mixtæ secundariæ</note> mixed secondary qualities), and if nothing is outside of GOD, but rather everything imaginable is contained within GOD, then everything must necessarily be brought back into Him to its first primordial perfection. For in His essential goodness, an arisen evil cannot exist eternally with Him; otherwise, according to common theology or teaching, two completely contrary qualities would have to be believed to exist in the simple, eternal Divine Being. To say such a thing is devilish, let alone to believe it. But since there can be no whole Circle—that is, an eternity of destruction running parallel with GOD—He has devised an eternal redemption: namely Christ, His only-begotten Son, procreated from eternity, the <term>Salt</term> <note>original symbol: $\ominus$</note> of His eternal fatherly love. Through the enjoyment of this, every creature in time and eternity will be rescued from total destruction. Namely, when the gross elementary <term>Fire</term> <note>original symbol: $-\triangle$</note> of Hell and all its offspring swallow themselves up again, and all its proud, dark, devilish dross is dissolved and destroyed by this, its own raging <term>Fire</term> <note>original symbol: $\triangle$</note> (which consists in the gross elementary elements—see Figure Numbers 2, 3, 4, 5—that is, in heat and cold, or in the dark <term>Fire</term> <note>original symbol: $\triangle$</note> and <term>Salt</term> <note>original symbol: $\ominus$</note>). Thus it is turned back from all outward elementary quality or dark and damned being, so that not even a trace of a sun-speck remains. This is done so that in them, as in a completely pure <term>Water</term> <note>original symbol: $\nabla$</note>, no other passive state or desire and will remains except that which the eternal Divine Light, Christ Jesus, will shine into them, awaken, and make them desire.

This river (well known to the <term>Magi</term>, <term>Theosophists</term>, and <term>Cabalists</term>) allows them to flow in and out again into the glassy sea of Revelation 15. Where then was this evil hidden, which made the creature so contrary to its Creator, since nothing existed or could be outside of God? Furthermore, if the living God had not revealed His most holy counsel, the work of redemption in Christ Jesus, could the kingdom of the devil, of damnation, and of essential evil have existed eternally alongside the Almighty God? From common teaching, one might conclude that not only would an adverse division have occurred in God—existing eternally against His good will or, to speak plainly in German, as if in defiance of Him—but that it would also have essentially been in Him and with Him simultaneously, only revealing itself in the time of Christ's manifestation. §. 12. Now concerning the first point, Letter c. of this section: whether the Almighty God could have completely warded off and hindered this departure of the creatures is without any doubt, otherwise His holy omnipotence would be slighted. But why He did not do so is hidden from us poor, darkened humans in the eternal depths of His unsearchable wisdom. Thus, it is fitting for us to stand still before this astonishing abyss with the deepest humility of soul, so that we do not step too close and plunge our souls into eternal danger through a <term>spiritual vertigo</term> <note>A state of spiritual confusion or madness caused by attempting to understand mysteries beyond human capacity.</note>. For let one only consider how it could be possible for a human—who is but such a small and comprehensible tiny part of His so immense Majesty—to simultaneously and at once contemplate this number, which sprang <note>original: "ab unitate," from unity.</note> from unity: 100,000,000,000,000,000,000,000,000,000,000,000,000 in its <note>original: "individuitate," individuality or indivisibility.</note>—a number which is still as nothing compared to the <note>original: "numero divino perfectissimo innumerabili," the most perfect, innumerable divine number.</note>. And if one thought to grasp this set number and wanted to count and comprehend it through millions, these again through thousands, these again gradually through hundreds, and finally through tens, down to the origin of unity, it is yet nothing and far from our meaning. Our meaning consists in this: that one might contemplate this number in an indivisibility <note>original: "in suo toto individuo secundum omnes partes suas uno & ipsissimo momento in einer Idea," in its total individual whole according to all its parts in one and the very same moment in a single Idea.</note> all at once. But this is truly and entirely an impossibility; how then will wretched man search out and judge the unmentionable, unsearchable number of the most holy Divine Will? And how does it rhyme that the end should thoroughly comprehend its beginning—that beginning which was begotten from the front, which never had a beginning (but runs forth from the <note>original: "unitate Substantiali," Substantial Unity</note> in a never-ending number)? Oh, the terrifying folly and blindness even to think of it! Let us therefore turn away from such an abyss, so as not to plunge ourselves into eternal ruin, and wait in a quiet spirit for what the Almighty will give us to know of it in His holy rest after our dissolution. Evil is also not original in God, but merely grounded in the will of the creature. He thus set himself against the Divine power and glory, wherefore the Almighty God had to undertake the separation—of which more shall be spoken in the following, as much as God's Spirit shall grant us. # CHAPTERS II. & III.

The creation and the fall of Lucifer is not the least of these mysteries; to attempt to fully grasp it is an entirely vain undertaking. Instead, these matters will remain sealed until the complete revelation of the Divine Majesty. Thus, it remains entirely incomprehensible to us why He did not prevent, but rather permitted, pride and disobedience—the true, essential evil. On one hand, the Almighty wished to demonstrate to the entire creation that nothing can exist or be maintained outside of His Divine Being; indeed, even the hellish kingdom itself could neither be nor subsist outside of His Majesty and His holy will, and it must ultimately serve to praise His glory. However, the Almighty God gave the radiance of His glory—the heavenly man Christ, His beloved and only-begotten Son from eternity—as the means of reconciliation and union for all creatures. Without this means, nothing can return to its original glory and reunification with the living God. What might have happened in the course of the eternities if this means had not been provided by the omniscient God, and whether damnation and the kingdom of hell would have remained eternal and parallel to God without this means, are mysteries sealed within the inscrutable counsel of God. We ought to think of these things only with the deepest humility of soul and wait with a quiet spirit and patience until, through the complete revelation of Divine Majesty in the temple of the fullness of His Godhead—which is Christ Jesus—we may recognize the depths of the Divinity, each according to his measure, and then be instructed in these mysteries. This is what we have been able to answer, through the mercy of God, regarding the very terrifying objection noted at Letter c, page 29. We do not doubt that every soul hoping in God will be satisfied and content with this until, in the following discourse, the most holy God shall show us more through His Holy Spirit in Christ Jesus.

§. 13. Letter d. page 29. Concerning the <term>constituting fixed spirit</term> <note>original: "Arichten fixen Geist." In this context, "arichten" likely refers to the "primary" or "constituting" principle that gives structure to matter.</note> $\ominus$ <note>The alchemical symbol for Salt.</note> or *Aphar min Haadamah* <note>original: Hebrew for "dust from the ground," referring to the substance from which Adam was formed in Genesis 2:7.</note>, the Paradisiacal Earth: since enough has been said regarding the beginning of all things, or the essence first created by God the Lord (from which all others were procreated, and that this same essence was a heavenly $\ominus$ [Salt], or Fire, or even Water, and that all creatures, by whatever name they may be called, are nothing other than a $\ominus$ [Salt], which is $\nabla$ <note>The alchemical symbol for Water.</note> or a $\triangle$ <note>The alchemical symbol for Fire.</note>), it would indeed be proper to treat this extensively as the final goal of all creation. However, as said before, the man of understanding will already be fully satisfied in this; as for the perverse, malicious, and those minded against the truth, nothing is written here for them at all, and we gladly let everyone walk their aforementioned paths.

§. 14. Letter e. page 29. However, it is not our intention to satisfy everyone's desire; otherwise, instead of this treatise, we would have to assemble a work of many volumes. Furthermore, we have compiled this more for our own delight than with the intention that it should ever be made known to the world.

# CHAPTER III.
## On the Utility and Use of Salt.

§. 1.

This chapter might seem entirely unnecessary to many clever and over-wise people, as everyone knows how to preserve their beef and pork with it, and it is well enough known how to use it in cooking and enjoy it in food. However, this is its least utility; for it is so universally necessary to the entire creation that without this eternal influx, they could not subsist for even a moment.

§. 2. It has also been constantly used in all holy acts in the first true Magical church, as well as in the orthodox Jewish and early Christian churches. Mark says that all men shall be salted with $\triangle$ [Fire] and every sacrifice salted with $\ominus$ [Salt]. <note>A reference to Mark 9:49: "For every one shall be salted with fire, and every sacrifice shall be salted with salt."</note> Now, we have already said enough to show that $\triangle$ [Fire] and $\ominus$ [Salt] are fundamentally one and the same thing. Christ says: "No one is good but God alone." <note>Luke 18:19.</note> Yet He says elsewhere: "The $\ominus$ [Salt] is good; a good thing." <note>Luke 14:34.</note> Therefore, the first Christian church learned from the true Jewish church to hold no divine service without $\triangle$ [Fire] or burning candles, which is still customary today among many sectarians; however, it is now nothing more than a well-preserved habit or misuse derived from Anti-Christianity, and for the old people it was almost a piece of their fai— <meta>catchword: faith (Glauben)</meta>

### Of the Use and Utility of Salt.

...became [the practice] of the faith. The first true believers (as is still practiced to this day by the true Magi in their prayer chambers) held that no service to God should be conducted without Salt $\ominus$ and Fire $\triangle$, because nothing in the created world is more like the Divine Essence than these two creatures, Fire $\triangle$ and Salt $\ominus$. Thus, they constantly reminded themselves of the omnipresence of the Divine Majesty through them, for all appearances and revelations of Divine Glory always occur in Fire $\triangle$. He Himself calls Himself a consuming fire, a light that no one can approach; and even today in the Oriental Christian churches, it is the custom to baptize with Fire $\triangle$ and Water $\nabla$. <note>Welling refers here to the "Baptism of the Spirit and Fire" mentioned in the Gospels, often interpreted by alchemists as a literal use of elemental energies.</note> In the early days of the Christian Church, Salt $\ominus$ was constantly used during baptism; specifically, when the person was baptized, the baptizer took a little Salt $\ominus$ and placed it in their mouth with these words: "Take the Salt of Wisdom," <note>original Latin: "Accipe salem sapientiae"</note> and this served as a witness that, just as surely as the baptized received the Salt from the hand of the servant (which can strengthen and preserve the outer body), so surely and truly did they receive at the same time, in baptism, the Salt of Eternity—Jesus Christ—for their eternal preservation. 

Among all nations and religions at all times, Salt $\ominus$ has been held in very high esteem. Indeed, the ancient Germans, at their meals, placed the Salt $\ominus$ on the table before all other things, a custom still common in many places. This was done, firstly, as a reminder of the Divine Presence and His most holy blessing; and secondly, because all creatures were born from this noble creature (as we have proven extensively above) and inwardly consist of nothing else. Thus, before all things, before enjoying food, one should offer to the Divine Majesty for such an outflow the "sacrifice of the lips," seasoned by the burning Fire $\triangle$ of the spirit. And just as food seasoned with Salt $\ominus$ serves for strength and preservation, so too should the Divine Mercy preserve and work upon the inner, eternally remaining body, soul, and spirit with the Salt $\ominus$ of eternal salvation, Jesus Christ, for the preservation and participation in His eternal glory. Therefore, Jesus Christ also taught us to pray in His summary of all prayers in Matthew 6:11, which the Greek text gives as: "Give us today our bread for existence;" <note>original Greek: Τὸν ἄρτον ἡμῶν τὸν ἐπιούσιον δὸς ἡμῖν σήμερον; usually translated as "daily bread."</note> and is commonly rendered in German as: "Give us this day our daily bread." However, according to the Syrian text, it might sound like <term>Bread of necessity</term>, <note>original Latin: "Panem indigentiae"</note> and according to the Greek: <term>Sufficient bread</term>, or <term>Bread of sufficiency</term>, <note>original Latin: "Panem sufficientem" or "panem sufficientiae"</note> in which words are included not only daily physical lack, but also—and primarily—the lack and poverty of the inner man, the hunger and thirst of the soul and the spirit. And so they also left the Salt $\ominus$ on the table last, to remind themselves of the same duty of the sacrifice of thanks in burning prayer toward the kind and wonderful Creator.

The Persians held their <term>Orimasda</term> <note>A reference to Ahura Mazda, the Zoroastrian deity associated with light and fire.</note> so holy that, upon pain of death, it could be used for nothing other than holy use; and their kings always had this (their holy Fire $\triangle$) carried before them to maintain all the people in devotion and reverence toward the gods.

### CHAPTER III.

§ 2. We have a mystery to consider: namely, why must earthly Salt $\ominus$ <note>The alchemical symbol for Salt.</note> always be present in offerings? It was mentioned shortly before that the Fire $\triangle$ <note>The alchemical symbol for Fire.</note> of the Lord, at the dedication of the Tabernacle and Solomon’s Temple, consumed the offerings and ignited all the wood on the altar—a Fire $\triangle$ that had to be kept burning perpetually without going out. The earthly Salt $\ominus$ represents earthly, corrupted man, who must be consumed in the Divine Fire $\triangle$ (which is Salt $\ominus$ in its essence, namely His Mercy), if he is to be reunited with the Holy Fire $\triangle$ of his origin. Therefore, Mark says that all people shall be seasoned with Fire $\triangle$, and all offerings (that is, the offerings of the lips) with Salt $\ominus$ (that is, with a contrite and broken heart, in a fiery and longing spirit). <note>Reference to Mark 9:49: "For every one shall be salted with fire, and every sacrifice shall be salted with salt."</note> That this holy Fire $\triangle$ had to be maintained eternally shows us, on one hand, that His mercy toward us is inextinguishable, and on the other, that such mercy will endure eternally through the constant offerings of our fiery prayer. And this is spoken somewhat regarding its holy use—a glorious subject from the Magic and Cabalistic arts that could be extended much further—but we will save that for now and see what the <term>Ruach Chokmah <note>Hebrew: רוּחַ חָכְמָה</note></term> (that is, the Spirit of Wisdom) will teach us in what follows.

§ 4. Beyond its use in household economy, Salt $\ominus$ has countless other uses in earthly application. The common miner may not wish to praise it, for in the smelting process, its acidic form causes him more harm than good and even makes the most fixed bodies <note>In alchemy, "fixed" substances are those that resist the heat of fire without evaporating.</note> volatile in the Fire $\triangle$; for this reason, he cannot use it at all in common smelting. However, the smelter who is a little more experienced in the Fire $\triangle$ and understands the "turning" <note>Transmutation or inversion of properties.</note> of things will

be able to use it in his work with the Fire $\triangle$ to much greater benefit. ### CHAPTER III.

also called **<term>Leafy Earth <note>original: 🜃 foliata; a term for the purified alchemical earth that has reached a state of readiness for transformation</note></term>**, or **<term>the Philosophers' Dryness <note>original: ♁ Philosophorum siccus</note></term>**, and other such names. §. 2. Furthermore, we wish to demonstrate mathematically that the **Cube** is the **Sign of gravity**, just as the **Globe** (which is the figure of fire and water) is the **Sign of speed**. It is known to all mathematicians and physicists that no inanimate body, by its own force or by reason of gravity, can or may move otherwise than according to descending lines, or those inclining toward the perpendicular; and this motion is not even pure or absolute, but is only to be called a movement according to the force of their gravity and the lines already mentioned. For pure and absolute motion consists in the free will of animate creatures, which we wish to clearly prove to those unlearned in mathematics and physics.

<margin>K.</margin>
Given a surface (*superficies*) marked **s. q.** (see **Figure No. 12**), which is entirely horizontal and level, so that it does not incline in any place, a perfectly round globe placed upon it, wherever it may be, will lie entirely still and immovable according to its diameter or perpendicular **c. a.** upon the point of rest **a.**, which is also the point of gravity (*punctum gravitatis*). This may be quite well understood by any reasonable person. Thus, it is also crystal clear and undeniable from this that if one imagines a perpendicular **a. z.** passing through this horizon **s. q.**, and that it rises with perfect equality and unity with the diameter of the globe **a. c.**, then the globe **b. c. d. a.** must rest just as still, stiff, and immovable upon the perpendicular **a. z.** at the point of rest **a.** as it does upon the horizon **s. q.** The same condition of rest applies to the Cube **h. g. i. k.** on that same horizon **s. q.** and the perpendicular **Jupiter <note>original symbol: ♃</note>** at the point of rest **a.**, which is the point of gravity. Now, no demonstration is needed—for it is known even to children—how easily a globe weighing many hundreds of pounds may be moved, and how laboriously, by contrast, an equally heavy cube may be moved upon such a level horizon common to them both. Secondly, if the surface **W. X.** were given, intersecting the first **s. q.** at **f. a.**, then the globe's (**a. b. c. d.**) point of rest and point of gravity according to this surface **W. X.** is the point **f.** From this point **f.**, the perpendicular **f. Sun <note>original symbol: ☉</note>** is dropped according to the horizon **s. q.** Because this runs parallel with **a. z.**, it is crystal clear that due to the accumulated weight of **f. e. c. a.**, the globe cannot rest at **f.** upon the perpendicular **f. c.**, but will run forward and hasten toward point **X.** However, the case is not the same with the equally heavy cube **l. m. n. o.**; for it rests 
<margin>M. Q.</margin>
<margin>N. Mercury <note>original symbol: ☿</note></margin> 
upon the horizon **W. X.** not upon a single point like the globe, but upon one of its surfaces **l. n.** Beyond the piece **n. b. o.**, this surface is still pressed and weighed down perpendicularly by the entire load of its weight **l. m. b. n.** and the added air **l. 4. m.** (which replaces the piece **n. b. o.** that has deviated from the particularity and is inclined toward falling or motion). Thus, it cannot push itself forward out of its own heaviness or weight without an external driving force, unlike the globe, which is pressed upon only a single point by the entire load of its weight. Because of the added load **f. e. c. a.**, the other half **a. c. d.** cannot be pressed quickly enough onto a fresh point bordering the first without it—for those very reasons of weight—unceasingly rolling onto a fresh point and running forward until it is stopped by an object.

<margin>§ O. Earth <note>original symbol: 🜃</note></margin>
Mechanics shows us daily that the cube **l. m. n. o.** can now be moved more easily on this surface **W. X.** (which is a descending line, or one inclining toward the perpendicular **a. z.**) than on the water-level surface **s. q.** We see how a large and heavy load can be moved and pushed forward much more easily downhill than on a level surface. This movement of the cube becomes easier and easier the more the surface **W. X.** (upon which it is pressed by its load) inclines toward the perpendicular **a. h.**, as can be seen in the following **Figure No. 13**, until it finally rests on nothing more, but—because all rest has been taken from it—it hastens violently downward along the perpendicular **a. h.** And although this motion is caused by the load of its weight, it is nevertheless, as we said above under **Letter N. I.**, not pure or absolute, but mixed. For since nature 
<margin>P. Note: Jupiter <note>original symbol: ☞ ♃</note></margin> 
abhors a vacuum—and this can neither be proven nor grasped—the place left vacant by the body **a. E.** (see **Figure No. 13**) is filled again with violent and incomprehensible speed. This fulfillment continues in violent haste with the fall of the body, as the diagram **No. 14** demonstrates. There, **b. d. e. f.** is the globe of the earth, and **c.** is its center, toward which everything heavy and corporeal composed of the tangible elements hastens. **a.** is the body that hastens from the height **g. h.** downward toward the center **c.** or the surface **d. e. f.** Now, because all the air that surrounds the earth radiates in a cone toward the center of the earth, as seen in the inverted cone **g. c. h.**, it is easy to understand that the deeper the **Fire <note>original symbol: 🜂</note>** follows the falling body **a.** in the cone to replace the void, the more this **Fire <note>original symbol: 🜂</note>** in the cone is pressed toward the apex **c.** Consequently, it must also promote the fall of the body more and more violently. Therefore, the motion of the body, although caused by its own heaviness, is not pure or absolute, but mixed; and it is thus irrefutable that in the entire universe no inanimate body...

§. 9. Now we have yet to speak of its **Signature**. The ancient **Magi** <note>Wise men or practitioners of high natural magic</note> and **Cabalists** <note>Followers of the Jewish mystical tradition known as Kabbalah, often adapted by Christian hermeticists</note> have at all times designated both the Elements and all their offspring with certain signs or characters to indicate the essential power and nature of things. In this, our modern <term>pretend-philosophers <note>original: Philosophastri</note></term> and <term>pretend-chemists <note>original: Chymistastri</note></term> have never followed them; but just as their brains have been darkened and clouded by the many poisonous vapors of their messy works, so they have also obscured and made the truth unrecognizable with their foolish signs and characters.

<margin>R <note>A symbol resembling a capital R with a crossed tail, often used in alchemy to represent a recipe or specifically "Regulus."</note></margin>
The ancient Mages and Cabalists depicted **Salt** <note>original: 🜔</note> as seen here, namely by a **Cube**, and they were not wrong to do so; for with this cubic figure, they explained and represented, as it were, the secret of the entire Creation. The Cube has 6 faces (<term>surfaces <note>original: superficies</note></term>); the <term>time of creation <note>original: tempus creationis</note></term> lasted 6 days, and L. is the <term>point of rest or the Sabbath <note>original: punctum quietis f. Sabbathum</note></term>. Furthermore, whichever face one looks at, one always sees the character of the four elements generated out of Fire <note>original: 🜂</note>; by this they clearly intended to show that Salt is the <term>first thing created, or Fire/Light—that is, the first outflow from God, the Best and Greatest <note>original: primum creatum f. Δ. i. e. effluvium primum, à Deo optimo maximo</note></term>, which the holy text calls **Shamayim** (שמים) <note>Hebrew for "Heavens"</note>, from which further all creatures (by whatever name they might be called or imagined) were generated or came into being. And thus, with this character, they clearly intended to show us that in Salt <note>original: 🜔</note>, everything created is concentrated; indeed, that Salt <note>original: 🜔</note> is the beginning and the end of all being, which we have proven above at great length and irrefutably.

<margin>S</margin>
Furthermore, the Cube is composed of 12 bodies, each of which in turn has 6 faces. These 12 bodies are the 12 pillars of the true invisible Church; and when these 12 bodies are multiplied by their 6 faces, the result is the holy number **72**, which is the <term>Mystic number <note>original: numerus Mysticus</note></term>, and the 24 Elders of the first Church multiplied 3 times. The true Sages also intended to show and prove through this described <term>cubic figure <note>original: figuram cubicam</note></term> that nothing can or may be perfect unless it be perfectly dissolved, separated, and put back together again, so that it becomes one body—like the Cube, which is composed of 12 bodies, each of which consists purely of triangles. These triangles can be described or modeled in no other way than <term>from the center, i. e., from unity <note>original: à centro, i. e. ab unitate</note></term>. Thus, 3 consists out of 1, and 4 consists out of 3 and 1; these 4 multiplied by 3 produce 12, and these 12 in turn multiplied by 6 (the 6 <term>surfaces <note>original: superficiebus multiplic.</note></term>) give birth to the <term>Mystic number</term> 72.

<margin>TY <note>A ligature combining the letters T and Y, likely an editorial or symbolic marker.</note></margin>

### CHAPTER III.

...is found so often within it: from eternity to eternities, and the smoke of their torment rises from eternity to eternities, or from ages to ages; <note>original: "von Ewigkeit in die Ewigkeiten"</note> that is, in an incomprehensible sequence. That this must be so can be sufficiently concluded from the frequent repetition of these terrifying phrases. Although these aforementioned "times" are only comprehensible and expressible in numbers (because the signs that operate there—the distinction of day and night, as well as all years and seasons—will then no longer exist), we cannot have any concept of our specific times and eternities through our own transitory time (which is produced and distinguished by the sun, moon, and stars). Before the entire, unrestricted Divine Majesty, all such durations (called "times") are as nothing, as the text of Psalm 90, verse 4 says: *For a thousand years in thy sight are but as yesterday when it is past, and as a watch in the night.* Here we find something which, after the transformation of this present world, cannot be grasped, much less named—unless, according to our principles, the constant duration of the sun, moon, and stars is established (though after their transformation); in which case all doubt is resolved, and we remain with the previous thesis.

<margin>Letter β.</margin>
By this, however, we do not wish in the least to disturb the good and holy thoughts of others who believe that this circle of purification will resemble a great <term>Sabbath-Year <note>A cycle of 7,000 years, echoing the seven days of creation</note></term> of 7,000 years occurring seven times—which makes 49,000 years—until the great <term>Jubilee Year <note>original: "Hall-Jahr," referring to the biblical Year of Jubilee</note></term>, namely the 50,000th year, when everyone shall return to their own property and inheritance (Leviticus 25:8). <margin>Letter γ.</margin>

Otherwise, they could not possibly close together or unite in <term>most perfect union <note>original: "perfectissima Unione"</note></term> at their center point L. This clearly shows us that everything, before it can or may return to God, must again be perfectly purified, so that it may again receive its first perfect figure and form and be completed.

<margin>Letter δ.</margin>

$\psi$ <note>Sulphur/Air</note>
</margin>

And just so they also designated the Water-triangle: namely, with the other half-circle (see Figure 31) as **a. q. c.** and the narrowed ends **g. a.** and **g. c.**, the diameter **a. c.**, and the radius **g. d.** parallel with **a. c.** as **h. i.** shows. But when the radius **d. g.** (that is **h. i.**) is pulled away, it is the Earth, as the mentioned Figure 31 shows. From this one now sees why the ancient sages designated **Salt** thus [$\oplus$]: namely, that in it alone all perfection...

### Chapter III.

<margin>r T.</margin>
...to find perfection, and also to find the entirety of Nature concentrated within them; this is clearly understood from the fact that Fire <note>symbol: $\triangle$</note> and Water <note>symbol: $\nabla$</note> are but one and the same, as their three characters, when viewed implicitly, also demonstrate. We have now explained more than sufficiently why Salt <note>symbol: $\ominus$</note> was represented by the true Magi and Cabalists through a Cube and a Circle with its Diameter <note>original: "Circulum cum Diametro suo"</note>, or also by a Sphere; namely, that from the Circle **a. b. c. g.** (see Figure No. 32) and the Square of that same Circle, both signify one and the same thing. All other characters of metals, minerals, and salts spring from this, as the following sections will explain. We believe, therefore, that we have spoken enough—perhaps even more than enough—regarding the signature of Salt <note>symbol: $\ominus$</note>, for which we might well be criticized; yet God, from whom all wisdom flows, has Himself been the leader and guide in this matter.

§. 12. In the following sections, we shall speak further of the Signature of metals, minerals, and salts, beginning with Gold, which was drawn by the ancients in this manner (see Figure No. 33). They also used this character to designate the Sun, intending to show that both are, in their innermost essence, entirely one and the same: namely, the Essential <margin>Lit. A.</margin> Fire <note>symbol: $\triangle$</note> from the Heavens <note>original: "Schamajim," a Hebrew term for the heavens which alchemists often interpreted as a combination of 'Esh' (fire) and 'Majim' (water)</note>. The character itself demonstrates that nothing can be more perfect than these two bodies; for just as no more perfect figure can be drawn from a Point than the Circle, so too is there no greater perfection than these bodies. Both are nothing other than a completely essential Fire <note>symbol: $\triangle$</note>, the blood-red Heavenly Fire <note>original: "Sahamajim"</note>, which no earthly fire can ever overcome or affect; rather, they must be mastered by the heavenly Water <note>symbol: $\nabla$</note>. That the Circle, Globe, or Sphere is the figure of Fire and Water <note>original: "figura Ignis & Aquæ"</note> was already stated at the <margin>Lit. B.</margin> beginning of the 8th section of this Chapter, and thus requires no repetition. Through Fire <note>symbol: $\triangle$</note> and Water <note>symbol: $\nabla$ </note>, the constant and eternal movement is best observed; this is all that remains to be remembered. <margin>Lit. Γ.</margin> In the true, ancient Cabala, one also finds the Macrocosm <note>the universe</note> and Microcosm <note>man as a miniature universe</note> designated as in Figure No. 34. This indicates that just as the Point is surrounded by the Circumference, so too is this world surrounded by the Heavens <note>original: "Schamajim," meaning the wondrous Fire-Water we have just discussed</note>. As for why they also depicted the Microcosm in this way, we believe they intended <margin>Lit. Δ.</margin> to suggest that man’s innermost essence is nothing other than an essential Gold (Dust from the earth <note>original: "Aphir min Haadamah," a reference to Genesis 2:7</note>). Indeed, this very essence within him is and remains the eternal, indestructible body. That this indestructible Gold is also found in other forms is certain and true; without this Gold or Fire <note>symbol: $\triangle$</note> and the pure Salt-Water, no creature could exist or remain for a single moment. <margin>Lit. E.</margin> Gold from the mountains is congealed into a pure body within its pure Matrix (see Chapter I, sections 20 and 25 through to the end). Therefore, according to its outward form <note>original: "secundum formam"</note>, it is not to be recognized as the Sun <note>symbol: $\odot$</note> of which we speak, unless it is dissolved by its own Salt-Water according to the true rules of the Philosophical Art, and entirely separated from the foreign substances <note>original: "heterogeneis"</note> that cling to it here on earth. <margin>Lit. Z.</margin> This happens quite easily, provided one does not go astray in the solvent <note>original: "Menstruo"</note> and knows the gold well according to its form. For this reason, we have said it must be done with Gold’s own Salt-Water <note>symbol: $\nabla$</note>, which consists of the Fire of Heaven and Earth <note>original: "$\triangle$ Cæ & Olis," referring to the celestial and terrestrial principles</note>, that is, of Gold and Silver—a liquid made of two Suns which, at their core, are but one and the same.

***

§. 13. Now we come to the other metal, Silver, which was drawn by the ancients thus (see Figure No. 35): that is, with a half-circle and a point or center, with the other half-circle turned or placed inward. This was also the character they used for the Moon. This character clearly shows us that Silver must be a completely perfect <margin>Lit. H.</margin> metal, equal to Gold and of an entirely simple essence with it, yet in such a way that the "red" is turned inward—namely, the Sun <note>symbol: $\odot$</note> in its outward form. The <margin>Lit. Θ.</margin> ancient Cabalists likewise characterized Water with the symbol of the Moon <note>symbol: $\unicode{x263D}$</note>, as they did everything else that was fluid, even Mercury <note>symbol: $\unicode{x263F}$</note> itself. By this, they intended to sufficiently show that Silver consists of nothing other than the white Fire <note>symbol: $\triangle$</note> of the Heavens, the watery, glass-like Salt <note>symbol: $\ominus$</note>, the Lunar Seed <margin>Lit. I.</margin> (regarding which, read the first Chapter). If one dries out this white, watery, glass-like form within it—that is, if one turns the outer half-circle back around—one then has the figure of the Sun <note>symbol: $\odot$</note> or Gold once more. It is not necessary to repeat here what the true Solar and white Lunar Fire <note>symbol: $\triangle$</note> or seed is, as this highly important matter was treated extensively enough in Chapter I. Whoever has not read and understood that first chapter well will find our discourse on these characters of little or no use. How and in what manner this drying and turning must occur is shown by the character itself, especially if one observes the Point correctly and has learned well what the Light-Fire <note>original Hebrew: "Or Esh" (אור אש)</note> is—the holy fire which the Philosophers clearly and distinctly point out in their writings. This holy Fire <note>symbol: $\triangle$</note> and its fountain-springs were very well known to the true prophets and priests of the Old Testament, as may be read in the second book of Maccabees. The Persians called this Fire *Orim-as-da* <note>a reference to Ahura Mazda, the Zoroastrian deity of light</note>, as we have mentioned briefly before. Therefore, whoever knows and recognizes that Fire <note>symbol: $\triangle$</note>, Water <note>symbol: $\nabla$</note>, and Salt <note>symbol: $\ominus$</note> are truly and certainly one and the same, will all the better grasp the close relationship between Gold and Silver. They will also perfectly understand, when true physicians speak of curing otherwise incurable diseases, why Gold <note>symbol: $\odot$</note> must be used for some and Silver <note>symbol: $\unicode{x263D}$</note> for others, what they mean by Gold and Silver, and how they must become Potable Gold and Potable Moon <note>original: "Aurum & Luna potabilis," or liquid alchemical elixirs</note>. However, people willfully refuse to know that the heavens EXISTED OF OLD, and the earth, which [consisted] out of the water...

## On the Benefit and Use of Salt.

...and stood in the water, etc. 2 Peter 3:5. With this, we wish to conclude the description of the character of the Moon <note>original: "Character Lunæ"</note>, believing it has been explained more than clearly and distinctly.

§. 14. We now bring forth the destructible metals one after another, and first the most beautiful among them: namely Copper, which is marked as shown in Figure No. 36. It is drawn with a complete circle without a center, and a cross—or its radii—attached below (see page 41, plate L. T.), which signifies Salt <note>symbol: 🜔</note>. <margin>Letter A.</margin> This character explains itself well to anyone who has understood us in all the preceding parts. Its complete circle indicates its very Orish <term>Orish: likely a derivation of 'Aureic' or solar-celestial earth</term> Earth <note>symbol: 🜔</note> or Solar Earth <note>original: "Terram Solarem"</note>, which is present within it in great quantity. However, because the center—that is, the point of rest <note>original: "punctum quietis"</note> or the constant Light-Fire <note>symbol: △</note>—is lacking in this circle, it indicates that this Terrestrial Earth <note>symbol: 🜔</note> is attached to it only superficially; that is, it is entirely volatile. This circle without a center or point of rest also signifies that its Fire-Wheel <note>symbol: △ Radt</note> consists only of the wild, earthly Fire <note>symbol: △</note> or Earth <note>symbol: 🜔</note>, and still lacks the fixed, essential, constant Fire <note>symbol: △</note> of rest. Furthermore, the cross attached below (which in the circle is nothing other than its radii or diameters, that is, Salt <note>symbol: 🜔</note>) shows that it lacks the internal, essential Fire <note>symbol: △</note>—that Fire of which we have just spoken—and that only a coarse, earthly, destructible, Vitriolic Salt <note>symbol: 🜔</note> is attached to it. <margin>Letter M.</margin> Now, whoever knows how to separate this coarse earthly Salt <note>symbol: 🜔</note>, or how to turn it back inward—that is, how to set the Saline Fire-Wheel <note>symbol: 🜔liche △ Radt</note> back upon its axis or point of rest through its spokes or radii, giving it the constant, life-giving, essential Fire <note>symbol: △</note>—that person has grasped the Art.

§. 15. Now Mars, the warlike, invincible General, steps forward. For many reasons, he should rightly have been given precedence, as he is so necessary—indeed, almost the most necessary and useful thing on the entire earth. No art, science, or anything else can be devised where Mars could be dispensed with. <margin>Letter N.</margin> Indeed, even most of our food and nourishment consists of him, as is clearly evident from all the loamy and clayey earths from which bricks are fired: their redness indicates nothing other than the presence of Iron, which may be separated from it in a strong Fire <note>symbol: △</note> by means of a good flux, however small the amount may be. It is an unshakeable truth that this loamy earth and yellow clay were formerly nothing but Iron, which is daily torn further and further from its mineral source and root by the plowshare, brought to decay, and brings forth grass, herbs, roots, and all kinds of grain and fruit for our nourishment. If Iron is constantly moistened with water in the air so that it initially rusts, and if this rust is further constantly moistened with Water <note>symbol: ▽</note> in the air, it finally becomes, through the length of time, a natural yellow clay or loam, which is as good and fit for sowing as any other loam or soil. <margin>Letter Ξ.</margin> The fact that almost the entire surface of the earth was once covered with iron ore, which weathered in this way over time, is clearly proven by the fact that one still finds such clods of iron ore here and there, especially in places where the soil does not stand high above the quicksand. There, the sun's rays (the red Orish Earth <note>symbol: 🜔</note>) and the quicksand's water (which is a sand- and stone-making Water <note>symbol: ▽</note>, regarding which see Chapter I, §. 12, 13, 14, 15) can easily take hold; there, these radii are cooked into this Iron Mineral through the medium of this Water <note>symbol: ▽</note> and the help of subterranean heat, as was also discussed in the first chapter. <margin>Letter O.</margin> Indeed, it is also clearly proven by the fact that almost no fertile field is found where such a yellowish, rusty, iron-rich Water <note>symbol: ▽</note> does not spring forth. Thus we see briefly what a fellow Mars is, what his origin is, and how highly necessary—even indispensable—he is. On the other hand, he is also highly harmful when the wickedness of men provokes the vengeance of the Most High, and the Lord holds back the fertile rains, the dissolving Waters <note>symbol: ▽</note>, and that which is written in Deuteronomy 28:23 is fulfilled: namely, "further, your heaven that is over your head shall be brass, and the earth that is under you shall be iron," etc. But where the right Waters <note>symbol: ▽</note> are not lacking, then that which stands in Job Chapter 37:14 is fulfilled for our benefit: "That it (the earth) changes like clay into which a seal is pressed." We have not placed these sayings here in vain. <margin>Letter Π.</margin> Otherwise, the Sun <note>symbol: ☉</note> is the physical, immediate father of Mars, but the Moon <note>symbol: ☽</note> is his stepmother; for his father here below dallied with a foreign woman (see under Letter Z of this section) and begat him outside the orderly marriage bed. Therefore he has become so ill-mannered and wayward, yet he has still retained much of his father's blood. <margin>Letter P.</margin> We humans can also learn to recognize our own ill-manners, unruliness, malice, and bloodthirstiness from this daily nourishment of ours, which, as mentioned above, mostly springs from Mars. At the same time, however, we can judge from his pure blood, which he inherited from his father (which is held to be a perfectly pure, bright, eternally indestructible Fire <note>symbol: △</note>), from what kind of seed our spiritual, inner, indestructible body must consist: namely, as the Scripture says, from red earth taken from Paradise, *Min Aphar min Haadamah* <note>Hebrew: "From the dust of the ground," Genesis 2:7</note>. The ancient sages marked this Mars or Iron in this way (see Figure No. 37): with a complete circle and a complete diameter coming out sideways from the top, at the end of which are again two radii or semi-diameters pointing from the top down toward the circle at a right angle. This character has very many and secret meanings, of which we will mention the most necessary here, starting from point **a**: <margin>Letter Σ.</margin> From that point, two radii or semi-diameters of the Higher Light <note>original: "Luminis Superioris"</note> **a.c, a.d** run out, forming a right angle at **a**. That is: nothing is lacking but the *Aqua Schamajim* <note>Hebrew: "Water of the Heavens"</note>, the Orish white Earth <note>symbol: 🜔</note>, for it to be the perfect Fire-Globe <note>symbol: △ Globus</note>, i.e., a pure perfect being, the constant red Earth <note>symbol: 🜔</note> of Fire <note>symbol: △</note>; but as it is, the two radii of light...

### CHAPTER III.

...of Fire <note>symbol: △</note> which lacks this [specific alignment] c. d., mixed with an impure woman <margin>a. That is, the entire 1/16</margin> of the diameter of the lower sphere, i.e., with a crude earthly Earth <note>symbol: ⊖</note>, which is its Salt <note>symbol: ⊕</note> of vitriol. Thus, this crude body of his was generated. But that this Earth <note>symbol: ⊖</note> of his is much purer and holier than the <margin>Letter r.</margin> Earth <note>symbol: ⊖</note> of the Sun <note>symbol: ☉</note>, you can see clearly from this: firstly, that his Earth <note>symbol: ⊖</note> or diameter is already a sublimated or purified and ascended Earth <note>symbol: ⊖</note>, which already mixes itself in the air, as it were, with the rays of light, and is enclosed by both rays of light. Therefore, it is also so sweet and lovely, for <margin>Letter v.</margin> it has already been corrected to a large degree by these light-rays. This Mars <note>symbol: ♂; Iron</note> is of an entirely different kind than the Venus <note>symbol: ♀; Copper</note>, which is entirely earthly, for it hangs from below its spheres; but this one of Mars is nearly equal to the *Sal Mirabile* <note>original: "Sali Miraculoso"; Glauber's Salt, or sodium sulfate</note>, otherwise called Nitre, which was generated from the upper ray of light and fire; see page 39 under Letter V, and this will be spoken of further in its proper place. Thus, from this excellent Fire <note>symbol: △</note> and the aforementioned Oil, the Sphere of Mars was generated. However, there is no center there, which then shows that the rays of light are entirely swallowed by the ascending diameter, and the crude Earth <note>symbol: ⊖</note> entirely predominates, and the red Solar Fire <note>symbol: △</note> is entirely turned inward. Therefore, the Earth <note>symbol: ⊖</note> of Mars should be applied as its <margin>Letter φ.</margin> own Menstruum <term>Menstruum: A solvent or substance used to dissolve or extract essence from a metal.</term> for its own correction, and the Fire <note>symbol: △</note> turned inward again; i.e., to unite it inwardly with the Fire <note>symbol: △</note> of constant Jupiter <note>symbol: ♃</note>. He who knows how to set the wheel back onto its spokes (radii) will make it move and run again on its own center, <margin>Letter x.</margin> and thus reveal the perfect circle. And we have already said in another place above—not in vain—that all things may be brought back to their original state from their predestined end, by the very same path which they held from their conception to their final birth; also, in Chapter I, §. 24, under Letter θ, page 13, the improvement <margin>Letter ψ.</margin> of Iron was not spoken of in vain. With this, we then set the character of Iron aside, and wish only to give the lovers of truth and wisdom this much more to consider: That this sign ♂, as an arrow of the Moon’s sphere ☽, was given by the ancients yet another meaning; and what we could say of this, we will save for the following parts, for this matter is properly Cosmographical, and shall therefore be handled in its proper place.

§. 16. The turn now falls upon JUPITER, or Tin. This is a metal about which both the Philosophers and the Chemists have written little or nothing correct; the miner also reports nothing further of it than how he simply brings it out of the mountains, melts it, and prepares it for common use. But because more is hidden behind it than many might dare to dream, it would be well worth the effort for the learned, such as ourselves, to treat it more extensively. Otherwise, this metal has been properly drawn as in Figure No. 38, which character, without long-windedness, soon shows what the nature and property <margin>Letter Ω.</margin> of Tin must be: Its half-circle a. b. shows that it must have a good portion—indeed, nearly half—of the Fire <note>symbol: △</note> of the Sun ☉, as g. a. h. represents to us; the remaining part b. g. h., however, indicates the Lunar Water <note>symbol: ∇</note>, Moon ☽, Mercury ☿, or Fire <note>symbol: △</note>. The attached cross b. d. f. e. shows that a Terrestrial Earth <note>symbol: ⊖</note> is entirely <margin>☩ A</margin> incorporated into its body. This cross is nevertheless its own menstruum, with which its external form must be entirely destroyed, so that the inner may reveal itself again, and the same sphere a. h. b. can be led entirely around its axis g. The crude earthly Fire <note>symbol: △</note> and Earth <note>symbol: ⊖</note>, which are e. c. f. and b. c. d., give Jupiter its crackling <note>The "tin cry," a distinct sound made when bending tin</note> and all its bad nature; therefore, you should let this cross with the half-circle of perfection pass through the fire of purification, and you will have reached your goal and will taste the goodness of Jupiter <note>symbol: ♃</note> abundantly enough. However, it is not our intention to write the process here, but only to explain the mystical or hieroglyphic sense of these characters as much as possible for us.

§. 17. SATURN, or Lead, now also comes creeping along. It is outwardly known to all the world, much despised and of little regard; nevertheless, it is the examiner of all other masters and their inner goodness and fitness. It may be worth as much as all of them [m. n. a.], and if the world knew it rightly, more honor would be shown to it. The ancient sages, however, who knew it better than we, drew this old, strange fellow thus, as in Figure No. 39. That is: at the top a cross g. h. i. k. b. and at the bottom two half-circles against each other, and the upper one with a point or center as a. b. d. c. and c. e. f. This wonderful signature or characterization must certainly mean something special, and as far as we can conclude from the true Cabala, <margin>☩ B. ☩ C.</margin> the two half-circles [Fire △ and Water ∇] do not mean the common ones—notwithstanding that both can be separated from Saturn—but the half-circle b. d. c. with the center a. indicates that almost its entire half consists of the Fire <note>symbol: △</note> of the Sun ☉, from the red Solar Fire <note>symbol: △</note> or male seed; the lower half-circle e. f., however, consists of the Water <note>symbol: ∇</note> of Salt, the white Lunar Fire <note>symbol: △</note> or seed. Otherwise, it would be impossible for this metal to destroy all others so easily, carry their fluidic Salt with it, and leave the perfect fixed one standing glorified. By this, let one learn to know this universal earthly menstruum well, so the knowledge of the spiritual universal menstruum will also not be lacking. He who now knows how to reunite these two circles again through his sweet wonder of the Sun ☉ [h. i. k. b.], and to sink the center g. into the circle’s center a., and thus move the sphere on a. again, will become aware <margin>☩ D.</margin> of what Saturn <note>symbol: ♄</note> is capable of to his highest wonder. But learn to know its own menstruum h. i. k. b., which stands not below, but above it; it is no earthly, but a heavenly Earth <note>symbol: ⊖</note>, with which the Earth <note>symbol: ⊖</note> [of Mars ♂] has great kinship...

-># Of the Use and Application of Salt.<-

...has; if you now give him the Earth <term>symbol: ♄; Saturn/Lead</term> or Matrix <note>original: "Terram oder Matricem"</note> which he loves, and which is entirely equal in form to the Water <term>symbol: ▽</term>, you will then set the union in motion. His inner Fire <term>symbol: △</term> a. will then become alive and cause the entire Sphere to be animated, to your greatest wonder; and thus Saturn has limped past. <note>In alchemy, Saturn is often described as "limping" due to its slow planetary motion and its association with heavy, "melancholy" lead.</note>

§. 18. For the inconsistent and fleeting **MERCURY**, who is nonetheless the most constant of all, the time is becoming almost too long before he too can step forward. His Character and designation <margin>E <note>Alchemical symbol: A circle containing a small cross, topped with a crescent moon.</note></margin> from the Magi and Cabalists is as shown in Figure Number 40: namely, at the top is the Circle of the Moon; beneath it a whole Circle without a Center that would be visible; and under the whole Circle a Cross <term>symbol: +</term>. This designation is almost wondrous: the upper half-Moon <term>symbol: ☽</term> indicates his fluidity or spiritual volatility, and <margin>F <note>Alchemical symbol: A vertical line crossed by two diagonal lines, forming a stylized X with a vertical axis.</note></margin> the lower Cross <term>symbol: +</term> indicates the earthly Watery <term>symbol: ▽</term> or Saltish <term>symbol: 🜔</term> dampness or Metallic form and volatility. At the same time, it shows that the fixed form of Salt <term>symbol: 🜔</term> is lacking in his Circle. The whole Circle indicates that although the noblest and most fixed part—the red Solar <term>symbol: ☉</term> Fire <term>symbol: △</term>—is hidden centrally <note>original: "centraliter"</note> within him, its life or Action <note>original: "Actio"</note> (namely the Point) is entirely locked within him and scattered in his volatile Lunar <term>symbol: ☽</term> form. One should bring these together within him and reveal them, turning the Saltish <margin>G <note>Alchemical symbol: A vertical line with a fork at the top and a small horizontal crossbar below the fork.</note></margin> nature along with his Lunar volatile form or Humidity into his interior, and dry these out through his own Fire or Life—namely, his hidden Point—making them fixed and constant. Then it will be found that the living Mercury <note>original: "☿ vivus"</note> is in truth nothing other than the red Solar and white Lunar Fire <term>symbol: △</term> in metallic form; regarding this, look with all diligence at Letter Z, Chapter I, §. 23, page 12, and §. 25, Letter µ, page 13. That the Magi and Cabalists also intended to indicate something far higher <margin>H <note>Alchemical symbol: A stylized cross with decorative loops or circles at the terminals.</note></margin> and more secret with this Character than common running Quicksilver is surely beyond all dispute. For the half-Moon lying on top is indeed the Figure of the Moon <note>original: "Figura Lunæ"</note>, and the whole circle is the Sun <term>symbol: ☉</term>; but the Cross <term>symbol: +</term>, when it is brought into the Circle, is the Character of the entire universe <note>original: "Character totius univerſi"</note> (see §. 11 under Letter z of this Chapter, page 39). Thus, they intended to indicate and note the Universal Earth <term>symbol: ♁</term>, that is, the Principle of all principles <note>original: "Principiium Principiorum omnium"</note>, which is the so often-mentioned *Shamayim* <note>A Hebrew term (שמים) for the Heavens, which the author interprets alchemically as a combination of Fire (Esch) and Water (Majim).</note>. This is Fire <term>symbol: △</term> and Water <term>symbol: ▽</term> and also the Sun <term>symbol: ☉</term>, however one wishes to have and use it: the true Essence of all things <note>original: "vera Eſſentia rerum omnium"</note>, the first-created Being of all beings. However, since this has already been discussed extensively enough above, it would be a folly to continue further with it here, for it would be nothing other than a repetition; thus the Character of Mercury <term>symbol: ☿</term> is explained as much as is necessary. Since, however, the 7 metals have been discussed and <margin>I <note>Alchemical symbol: A vertical line with a diagonal line crossing through it.</note></margin> handled here according to the 7 planets, it would not be poorly done if we also dealt a little with the 7 planets themselves, as the worlds and their properties closest to us, for a better understanding of this material. But since this is a matter that does not properly belong in this chapter "On the Use of Salts" <note>original: "de Uſu Salium"</note>, it shall receive its own special place and position in the following, provided we have time remaining for it.

§. 19. Now we wish to reach the end and make the conclusion with the Minerals and Salts. We speak of the Signature, not the Nature, for their nature has been discussed enough in Chapter I, §. 26 through the end of §. 31, and Chapter II, §. 8 to 10. We remain solely with their Signature, as much as has remained to us from antiquity. First place shall be taken by **ANTIMONY**, which was designated by them as in Figure Number 41: namely, a <margin>K. <note>Alchemical symbol for Antimony: A circle with a cross on top, resembling an "orb and cross."</note></margin> Cross on top, which signifies its Universal Principle of Salts, and a whole Circle without a Point, to indicate the volatile, as yet unbound Solar Fire <term>symbol: △</term> which it possesses in its whole Sphere. Its Sun <term>symbol: ☉</term>, although noble and excellent, only clings to its Sphere externally; but he who knows how to present this Sun in the form of Universal Mercury and how to cook the pure body of Mars <term>symbol: ♂; Iron</term> into it, will find that Heaven was not unfavorable to this mineral, and it will then appear to him in its perfection.

Next comes **ARSENIC**, called *Arsenum* by the ancients, and it was <margin>L. <note>Alchemical symbol for Arsenic: Two circles vertically aligned and connected by a line with a crossbar.</note></margin> designated by them as in Figure Number 41; that is, with two whole Circles, one above and the other below, drawn together with a Ray <note>original: "Radio"</note>. This clearly shows us that it consists of the two principles of superior light and inferior fire <note>original: "superioris luminis & ignis Inferioris"</note>. It is to be understood that it is nothing but the still entirely raw, dry, and unbound (for the point stands both above and below) Universal Venus <term>symbol: ♀; Copper</term>, dried out by the subterranean Fire <term>symbol: △</term> rays. Therefore, if one gives it a proper Metallic moisture—that is, a suitable Salt <term>symbol: 🜔</term> and a proper Moon <term>symbol: ☾</term> as a Matrix—one will find it again magnificent, fixed, and transfigured.

Following this is **SULFUR**, which was designated as in Figure Number 43: namely, <margin>M <note>Alchemical symbol for Sulfur: A triangle on top of a cross.</note></margin> a Triangle on top and a Cross below. This is nothing but Fire <term>symbol: △</term> and Salt <term>symbol: 🜔</term>. But one should know that this characterization is to be understood of the earthly Fire <term>symbol: △</term> alone; for when the Magi and Cabalists wish to speak of the upper or heavenly Sulfur, they designated it as the Sun <term>symbol: ☉</term>. <margin>N <note>Alchemical symbol: A cross with a small circle or dash in the center.</note></margin> Therefore, one should understand us rightly: which Fire <term>symbol: △</term> is being spoken of here, and what it lacks in its imperfection—namely Salt <term>symbol: 🜔</term> and the Minus sign <term>symbol: –</term> (see above §. 11, Letter V, page 39). That is: destroy the earthly Fire <term>symbol: △</term> within it through its own Water <term>symbol: ▽</term> and awaken in it the heavenly Fire <term>symbol: △</term> that is so firmly and completely locked away, and cook these together again; then you have reached the end of your undertaking: namely, to see a living, fixed Sulfur.

Now **Cinnabar** shall follow this—namely the natural kind—and it is designated thus (see Figure Number 44) with 4 half-Circles, always 2 against one another, and with 2 whole Rays drawn through. By this we recognize that its birth arose from a pure Fire <term>symbol: △</term> and Salt <term>symbol: 🜔</term>. For the 2 Rays make the 2 Crosses, one for Venus <term>symbol: ♀</term> and the other for Fire <term>symbol: △</term>; but the Fire <term>symbol: △</term> is this Mercury <term>symbol: ☿</term>...
