### CHAPTER III.

...and this is the Mercury <term>symbol: ☿</term>—one must understand us correctly—the first is Salt <term>symbol: 🜔</term> Cae, the other however is Salt <term>symbol: 🜔</term> Olis; <note>original: "Cae... Olis"; these likely refer to *Sal Caeli* (Salt of Heaven) and *Sal Solis* (Salt of the Sun).</note> these are so bound and dried out by the subterranean Fire <term>symbol: △</term> that when the radical metallic moisture is given and united with them, you then have it in a fixed metallic form. These
<margin>P <note>Alchemical symbol: 🜔 (Salt)</note></margin> are now the four characters with which the ancient sages—through composition or subtraction—marked all other minerals.

<margin>Q <note>Alchemical symbol: ♁ (Antimony or the Earthly Globus)</note></margin> For unslaked lime <note>Quicklime (calcium oxide), a highly reactive substance used in many alchemical processes.</note> however, they have—out of highly important consideration—also given a unique character, which is this one, as Figure Number 45 indicates; and it stands here for special reasons between the minerals and salts. It has at the top a half-circle, that is, the Lunar Magnet <term>symbol: ☽</term>, and at the bottom a cross <term>symbol: +</term>, that is, the Purest Terrestrial Earth <term>symbol: 🜨</term>, acting as the field in which the previously mentioned heavenly Sun <term>symbol: ☉</term> has been sown. The upper half-circle also signifies pure Fire <term>symbol: △</term> and the cross signifies the fixed Terrestrial Salt <term>symbol: 🜔</term>, acting as an attractive medium. The ancients also designated the fixed universal volatile acid <note>original: "acidum volatile fixatum Universale"</note> with this character; this character holds much that is hidden, therefore one must learn to use it well.

<margin>R <note>Alchemical symbol: 🜔 (Salt)</note></margin> Now we come to the **COMPOUND SALTS** <note>original: "SALIA COMPOSITA"</note>, and Saltpeter <note>Nitrum</note> in Figure Number 46 is the first. Its sphere is from the entire universe; it possesses the ray of the superior light and the inferior Fire <term>symbol: △</term>. It consists therefore of volatile particles and fixed alkaline parts; it is a wonder-salt <term>symbol: 🜔</term> of nature.

<margin>S <note>Alchemical symbol: 🜔 (Salt)</note></margin> Figure Number 47 is **VITRIOL**. That this is composed of universal salt, common salt, and metallic particles is shown by its rays pointing from above downward, from the sides, and from below upward. If it also possessed the fourth ray (that is, the perfect Glish essence <note>original: "Glische Wesen"; likely a reference to a specific alchemical quality of brilliance or glass-like transparency.</note>), it would be the character of the entire universe; see above, Section 11, Letter z, page 39. Thus its admirers see what a glorious subject Vitriol is; and yet, it is not yet the Universal Subject Sun <term>symbol: ☉</term>,
<margin>T <note>Alchemical symbol: 🜔 (Salt)</note></margin> which is to be sought in another mountain.

<margin>V <note>Alchemical symbol: 🜩 (Sal ammoniac)</note></margin> Figure Number 48 is the character of **SAL AMMONIAC**. It is not contained within a sphere, which indicates that it is produced not by nature, but by art or accidentally. It otherwise consists of six rays <term>symbol: 🜇</term>; these are rays of the common Mercury <term>symbol: ☿</term>, of the Fire <term>symbol: △</term> of the universal volatile acid, and the V of the Salt <term>symbol: 🜔</term> matter. But that these have no internal union at all is shown by their lack of a sphere. It received its first name from the Temple of Jupiter Ammon; for when many pilgrimages were made to this temple, which lay in a large, dry Egyptian desert, this Sun <term>symbol: ☉</term> was perfected in the sand from the urine of humans and animals, through the enclosed rays and the subterranean Fire <term>symbol: △</term>. Later, when this was no longer obtainable in that way, this accidental birth was imitated by art, and it was made just as well through the salt <term>symbol: 🜔</term> of urine, furnace soot, and cattle blood. Its use and application, however, are so great and indispensable that we could write a large book about it here, but this does not belong to our present purpose.

<margin>U <note>Alchemical symbol: 🜖 (Alum)</note></margin> Figure Number 49 is the character of **ALUM**. Although it is equal in its outer sphere to the Sun <term>symbol: ☉</term> and Moon <term>symbol: ☽</term> as well as Mercury <term>symbol: ☿</term>, the fact that everything is locked and contracted within it clearly shows that neither the rays of the superior nor inferior light <term>symbol: 🜂 △</term>, and even less the Point, are to be found in it. Nevertheless, it has great kinship with the Oil of Salt <term>symbol: 🜔</term>. Therefore, much art and time are required to reveal the light and fire <term>symbol: △</term>—that is, its Point—which is entirely locked within it through harshness and acidity. This may nonetheless happen perfectly if it is dissolved and coagulated in the right Sea <term>symbol: 🜄</term>. He who knows how to wash it with its own Water <term>symbol: 🜄</term>, to bathe it, and to reunite it again, will obtain a wonder-salt <term>symbol: 🜔</term> of nature, a master of purifying metals as great as Saturn ever was; moreover, an excellent and quite uncommon extinguishing of all poisonous and dangerous heat in the Microcosm <note>The human body as a reflection of the universe.</note>, etc.

<margin>W <note>Alchemical symbol: 🜚 (Alkali salt)</note></margin> Figure Number 50 is **ALKALI SALT**, which is an Arabic name, and simply means "salt." This is the root-Sun <term>symbol: ☉</term> in all vegetables and animals; it is the cornerstone from which all other salts can be made, or which can be transformed into all salts; see Chapter II, Section 9 and following. If one gives it these Fire-rays <term>symbol: 🜂</term>, one has a perfect Saltpeter; if these Water-rays <term>symbol: 🜄</term>, then a perfect common salt <term>symbol: 🜔</term>. Otherwise, many great secrets are hidden within it, which are almost known to those who work in the Fire <term>symbol: △</term>; but to set down everything else regarding it here would be utter folly.

<margin>X <note>Alchemical symbol: 🜗 (Tartar)</note></margin> Figure Number 51 is **TARTAR**, namely the salt <term>symbol: 🜔</term> from wine, a true wonder-Sun <term>symbol: ☉</term> of nature. Its salt <term>symbol: 🜔</term> shows that it consists of the four secondary qualities, that is, the Elements; and the character ♁ shows that the upper light-Fire <term>symbol: △</term> and lower Water <term>symbol: 🜄</term> have attached their rays to it. Yet its sphere is locked within it with very hard bonds. But he who can reveal these, and can enclose the attached Sun <term>symbol: ☉</term> or rays within and unite them inseparably, will obtain such a high and wondrous salt <term>symbol: 🜔</term> that it will bring him no small pleasure. Among almost all salts, none is known to us from which—with good experience—an excellent medicine could be more easily brought forth than from this. With this, we wish to conclude the signatures for this time, especially since we find no trace of the others from antiquity, whether they were extinguished by the length of time, or whether the ancients judged that those just recounted would be enough for the intelligent to recognize all the remaining genera collectively. Thus we must leave it at that, not doubting that every sincere lover of truth and wisdom will find his pleasure herein. We should therefore rightfully end this long and extensive
<margin>Y <note>Alchemical symbol: 🜔 (Salt)</note></margin> discourse on the Sun <term>symbol: ☉</term>; however, we are entirely persuaded that he who...

<meta>This page concludes the third chapter on the nature and utility of Salt. ### On the Utility and Use of Salt.

<margin>Z.</margin>
We hope that whoever found pleasure in the preceding pages will not take it amiss if we conclude this inexhaustible matter as we began it.

<margin>a. <note>An alchemical symbol for Mercury ☿ with a dot above it, often signifying a specific state of the "Universal Mercury" or its spiritualized essence.</note></margin>
This beginning briefly consisted in this: that the essence first created by God the Lord was a fiery Water <term>symbol: 🜄</term> <note>original: "$\nabla$"; though usually the symbol for water, the author describes it as "fiery" to show the union of opposites.</note> or a watery Fire <term>symbol: 🜂</term> <note>original: "$\triangle$"; the symbol for fire.</note>; that is, it was a Fire <term>symbol: 🜂</term> that was also Salt <term>symbol: 🜔</term> <note>original: "$\ominus$"</note> and also a Cube <term>symbol: ⚀</term> <note>original: "$\square$"; representing the stability of Earth.</note>. Each was distinct, yet each was all three simultaneously and at once. From this, all creatures were further issued and created. Since these creatures do not extend into an infinite number, but rather ended and ceased on the sixth day of the work of Creation, we cannot reasonably conclude otherwise than that the all-wise Creator not only demonstrated His masterpiece in this final creature <note>Referring to Man (Adam).</note>, but also concentrated and enclosed within it the beginning and the end of all creatures. That is, He allowed the entire universe to run together and assemble in this single circle. Following this conclusion of Creation was the foremost point of rest—that is, the Sabbath—to indicate that everything outside of this point is in constant unrest and incessantly longs for its beginning, which is the point of its rest. Now, in all that has preceded, we have sufficiently proven that the essence or outflow of His Majesty first created by God is a salty essence, which

<margin>b. <note>The standard alchemical symbol for Mercury ☿.</note></margin>
was at the same time Fire (that is, <term>symbol: 🜂</term>) and also Water (that is, <term>symbol: 🜔</term> <note>original: "$\ominus$"</note>). This was called *Schamajim* <note>Hebrew: שמים, meaning "Heavens."</note> by Moses, but called *Mercurius Universalis* <note>Universal Mercury</note> by the ancient sages. They named it **MERCURY** because, like quicksilver, it can and may perfectly take on all shapes and forms, just like a bright mirror. *Schamajim*, however, means as much (as already proven in the first chapter) as *Esh* and *Mayim* <note>Hebrew: אש (Fire) and מים (Water).</note>, that is, Fire <term>symbol: 🜂</term> and Water <term>symbol: 🜄</term>. Now we find that on the sixth day, Creation ceased

<margin>c. <note>An alchemical symbol representing the Earth ♁ or a variation of the symbol for Salt.</note></margin>
with Man, whom Moses or the holy text calls **ADAM**. Thus, without any objection, the conclusion is firm and irrevocable: that Man must be that perfect thing of which we have treated from the beginning until now. Indeed, he is not only that perfect thing, but something far greater and more perfect, in that his eternally living spirit—which the holy text calls the "Breath of Life" <note>Hebrew: נשמת חיים (*Nishmat Chayim*)</note>—was communicated to him directly from the eternal light <note>original: "à lumine æterno"</note> (Genesis 2:7),

<margin>d. <note>A stylized character resembling the letter A topped with a cross, likely a signature for a spiritual or "Alpha" beginning.</note></margin>
so that he himself is a little spark of the living Godhead. Whoever has ears to hear, let him hear, and also read with the highest attention the first chapters of Genesis and these three chapters of our description; then he will in truth find and see more than he ever believed. However, to set everything down here word-for-word <note>original: "verbatim"</note> might be interpreted as a great and shameless folly on our part. So much has been spoken clearly here and there that it is almost beyond responsibility; yet this is our comfort: that for him to whom the light of the Most High does not shine, he must remain blind even at bright high noon. But let Man learn to know himself; then, from his entire composition <note>original: "ex totò suo Concreto"</note>, he will judge each and every creature, both visible and invisible, and nothing will remain hidden or covered from him. Even the four separated Principles or secondary qualities <note>original: "Principia seu qualitates secundariæ"</note>, commonly

<margin>e. <note>A symbol of a cross over a horizontal line, signifying the division of the elements or the stabilization of a substance.</note></margin>
called the Elements <note>original: "Elementa"</note>, will be entirely revealed and manifest to him, along with all the creatures within them that are commonly invisible to us, which we shall treat further. Indeed, Heaven and Hell, Light and Darkness, will be perfectly revealed and manifest to him. He will be able to see and fulfill with firm and irrefutable truth that the most "dead" body <note>original: "Corpus"</note> can be perfectly

<margin>f. <note>The alchemical symbol for Mercury ☿.</note></margin>
restored again and made eternally constant. These last words might well cause offense to one or another inexperienced person, but that shall not hinder us, as it nevertheless is and remains an eternal truth. From this, Man will be able to perfectly see

<margin>h. <note>The alchemical symbol for Saturn ♄, often representing lead, darkness, or the process of "death" and putrefaction in the Great Work.</note></margin>
and conclude how, after the appointed times of the eternities, every creature that is entangled in darkness—that is, in the dross or tangibility—shall be turned back again and translated into the spiritual Mercurial life, in which henceforth nothing can act but Spirit and Light. Therefore, O Man! learn above all to know yourself rightly and well; then it will not be hidden from you what that is which the Philosophers call their Mercury <term>symbol: ☿</term>, but Moses calls Fire <term>symbol: 🜂</term> (that is, Salt <term>symbol: 🜔</term> and Water <term>symbol: 🜄</term>), and which we have called **Salt** with a universally understandable word. You will understand what is said in Jeremiah 10:13: *He makes the lightnings into rain* <note>Hebrew: ברקים (*B'rakim*)</note>.

<margin>i. <note>A symbol resembling a Z with a cross-bar, a cryptic mark for a specific chemical process or a bridge between states.</note></margin>
These words certainly mean to say something more than the common interpreters imagine with their glosses. In short, it is well to consider why Man is called the "Little World" (*Microcosmus*). And since we ourselves are what we seek, it is only fair that

<margin>k. <note>An eight-pointed cross or star, signifying the completed work or the "Star of the Magi."</note></margin>
we make the beginning in and on ourselves, so that we may arrive at the universal knowledge of the whole of Creation and its origin, and consequently to the knowledge of the most holy Creator Himself: to whom be praise, honor, and glory from eternity to eternity

<margin>l. <note>The same stylized A with a cross seen in margin 'd'.</note></margin>
without any end. With this, we then fully close this long chapter, and present Figure Number 52 for further contemplation to the seekers <note>original: "Liebhabern"; literally "lovers" (of the art/wisdom).</note>, though whether many will well-comprehend our intention herein remains to be seen.

# CHAPTER IV.

## ON THE ARCHETYPAL WORLD.
<note>original: "DE MUNDO ARCHETYPO"</note>

->§. 1.<-

<margin>m ☩</margin> Now that we have once—within the preceding three chapters—concluded the description of the origin, nature, and properties, as well as the use and utility of the Sun <term>symbol: ☉; representing both the celestial body and the metal gold</term>, as far as we have been enabled by Divine Mercy; we come now to the remainder of what we promised on the title page: namely, to the description of the remaining invisible creatures within the visible elements, as well as the true location of Paradise. This cannot be done clearly and distinctly unless we begin from the Archetypal World <note>original: "à Mundo Archetypo"; the "Archetypal World" refers to the original spiritual blueprint or divine idea of the universe that existed in the mind of God before physical creation.</note> and describe everything that was created therein, including the Fall of Lucifer and the subsequent separation or creation of this entire universe <note>original: "Creatio totius universi hujus"</note>. Furthermore, we shall describe the creature called Man, who came to possess Lucifer’s place again, and how he also fell; and finally, the general return of every creature—none excepted, whatever they may be—to their first original perfection in which they issued from GOD, so that what is written in Zechariah 14:6–9 may be fully fulfilled. <margin>n ☩</margin> *At that time there will not be a light which is now precious and then dark. But it will be a unique day, known to the LORD; it will be neither day nor night, but at the time of evening there will be light. At that same time, living water will flow out from Jerusalem, half of it toward the eastern sea and the other half toward the western sea, and it shall continue in both summer and winter. And the LORD will be King over all the earth; at that time the LORD will be only one, and His name only one.* Likewise in 1 Corinthians 15:28: *That GOD may be all in all.* Also what is written in Revelation 10:6 & 7: *And swore by Him who lives for all eternity, who created heaven and what is in it, and the earth and what is in it, and the sea and what is in it, that henceforth there shall be no more time. But in the days of the voice of the seventh angel, when he shall sound his trumpet, then the mystery of GOD shall be finished, as He declared it to His servants the prophets.* And it shall be fully revealed that, *Behold, I make all things new* (Revelation 21:5), and the New Jerusalem shall be revealed, the city of the living GOD, where there will be no more night. For the clarity of GOD illuminates it, and the Lamb is its candle; and every creature shall be led to the pure river of the water of life, clear as crystal, which proceeds from the throne of GOD and of the Lamb; and there shall be no more cursed thing, but every creature in unsearchable, soft, still rest and sweetness shall behold the Most Holy Face of the living GOD, who alone is the beginning and the end of all being and all times, to whom alone belongs praise, honor, and glory without any change of times, Amen, Hallelujah. <margin>o ☩</margin> All of this we intend to treat in an orderly manner in various chapters, as much as the Almighty GOD and His most holy Light shall impart to us, and we set it down as follows: <margin>p ☩</margin> For the Diagram of the Archetypal World <note>original: "Schema de Mundo Archetypo"</note>, see Figure No. 53. However, we must remind the reader, and at the same time ask that no one take it ill, that we did not treat this matter first—as might have seemed fitting—before the three preceding chapters. Such a sequence would not have suited the Mago-Cabalistic style <note>A method of interpretation combining "Magia" (divine wisdom/natural philosophy) and "Kabbalah" (mystical tradition).</note>. For the style of the Holy Spirit, as Moses was led to record it by his most holy impulse when describing the beginning of creation which ended in Man, is one thing; but the Mago-Cabalistic style is another, which mostly tends to make its beginning from "characterization" <note>original: "à Characteriſticatione"; the study of outward signs or "characters" that reveal the hidden inner essence of things.</note>. Since the visible creatures, which are all concentrated in Man, are the "characters" of the essence hidden within them, and Man is the perfect "character" of all of these, it was not permitted for us to proceed otherwise than we have done, but we had to follow this customary style.

->§. 2.<- The following Diagram, Figure No. 54, requires no special explanation as to what it is; but as to what is indicated by it, as well as concerning the state of things before this our visible world, it certainly requires one.

<margin>q § ☩</margin> Firstly, there is likely no doubt in the mind of a rational man that in the most holy Divine Being there is neither beginning nor end. Whether the Most Holy Divine Majesty, in His immeasurable eternity or incomprehensible circle, never acted otherwise than at the time of His Majestic Will for the creation of the Spirit-World: this is a depth that we should properly pass over in silence. For this is a depth before which every creature must rightly be astonished; let us satisfy our minds with the knowledge that the Almighty GOD requires no external pleasure, but is Himself all pleasure and perfection.

He outlines a grand theological vision where all fallen creatures, including Lucifer's domain, will eventually be restored to divine perfection.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the transition into the theological and cosmological foundations of the work. Having established the "Archetypal World" on the previous page, von Welling now explores the nature of God's eternity, the creation of the spiritual realms, and the initial position of Lucifer before his fall.</meta>

<page-num>47</page-num>

<header>ON THE ARCHETYPAL WORLD.</header>
<note>original: "DE MUNDO ARCHETYPO"</note>

<margin>r ♃ <note>The symbol for Jupiter.</note></margin>
Yet this much is certain: that within the astounding Divine Depth **A. A. A. A.**, the Center **B.** with its entire circumference—even if this were many millions of times larger than this universe—could still have infinitely many times more remaining space.

<margin>S ♄ T ♃ <note>Symbols for Saturn and Jupiter.</note></margin>
It is also certain, however, that the Living God never created any other or more worlds than this spirit-world and our earthly world, which was further separated from it; we see this in Jesus Christ, the Savior of all the world, who is from all eternity the beginning and the completion of the Divine Will. Therefore, we wish to turn away from this Depth and toward the beginning that arose according to the Will of God—that is, to the origin of the Spirit-World. For why should we ask so many questions and entertain our idle curiosity <note>original: "Fürwitz"; implies an impertinent or overly-bold curiosity into divine secrets.</note> about whether the Lord God sat quiet and idle through all His unfathomable eternity before this creation took place? This is in itself a very unchristian and punishable, inquisitive question; yet to answer those who ask it nonetheless, we say: let one consider well whether He who has neither beginning of days nor end of time can be attributed a past, a present, or a future? And must one not grasp that with Him there must have been, and still is, a constant presence, which shall remain into all eternity? With Him, yesterday is as today and tomorrow. And even if one wished to grant an innumerable multitude of worlds, one after another, one must nevertheless stop somewhere; otherwise, this quite cruel absurdity would follow: that as long as the Lord God has existed, worlds would also have existed, and thus these worlds would have been co-eternal and co-existent with God. It would then follow that one world must have always "hatched" the next, from which, in the end, they would actually become gods themselves. Oh, the folly of the over-clever and yet stone-blind world! It remains the case that when it pleased Almighty God to create this world, this "boundary of the beginning" <note>original: "Terminus des Anfangs"</note> was with Him just as all His infinity was; thus, one could see no "past eternity" there—which would otherwise have been a *determination* <note>original: "Determinatio"; a fixed limit or definition</note> or a specific boundary, which cannot take place in God. Rather, this boundary was (so to speak) in the infinite, constant presence of God, without a single change of past or future.

<margin>1. 2. 3.</margin>
§. 3. Therefore, when it pleased the Divine Majesty to create something that would contemplate this His eternal glory and perfect pleasure—along with the creature’s own highest and most inward pleasure—to the praise of His most holy Name, it pleased His Infinity to create a beginning of all things in Jesus Christ, the image of His being. This was the Spirit-World, in the highest perfection. Around the throne of His glory, He set the Seven great and mighty spirits, of whom one may read throughout the entire Apocalypse <note>The Book of Revelation.</note>; see also Chapter IV, verse 5 and Chapter VIII, verse 2. Through these, the Alpha and Omega <note>original: "A und Ω"</note>—the holy and hidden number of His secrets—is completed, as may be read in the aforementioned Revelation of St. John. These are followed by the twelve worlds or circles of the holy and undefiled spirits, as seen in Figure No. 54. **B. C. D. E.**, however, is the world of the Son of the Dawn <note>Lucifer; from the Hebrew "Helel" (הילל), meaning "shining one."</note>, the most powerful, glorious, and wisest spirit, the brilliance of the Divine Light, toward whom all the rays of light of the Divine Majesty <note>original: "Radii luminis Divinæ Majestatis"</note> shone. He had the Center **B.** of this great spiritual universe for his glorious throne, and was enclosed by the Divine Glory and blessed Majesty as if by a circumference; the brilliance of the glorious Divine Light radiated incessantly within it (see **F. B.**). His contemplation and delight were constantly in the unfathomable Divine Wonder-Light **B. F.**, and all twelve worlds of the spiritual hosts influenced <note>The word "influenced" here refers to "influx," a flowing in of spiritual power or qualities.</note> their wisdom and glory toward this center, which proceeded from the Divine Ray **F. B.** For all their wisdom, power, and glory flowed steadily from one into the other, without any separation or interruption (see **G. H. I. K. L. M. N. O. P. Q. S. T.**), and surrounded this throne of the Son of the Dawn.

<margin>4. 5. 6. 7. 8. 9.</margin>
§. 4. This Son of the Dawn <note>Hebrew: הילל (Helel)</note> is the first creature of the living God, the Motion of the First Divine Majesty <note>original: "Motus Primæ Divinæ Majestatis"; a philosophical term for the first act of creation</note> (see with all diligence Chapter III, §. 11, up to Letter V). For when the glory of the living God moved in His will for creation from **F.** to **B.**, the radius or semi-diameter of the entire universe (which was the first motion) was created, ending at point **B.** (which was in the Son of the Dawn). Regarding this, one may read in Job, Chapter 38, verse 7, where it is written that "the morning stars (that is, the spirits of the Son of the Dawn) sang together," etc. It was proven in the previous chapter that no circumference or perfect round can be drawn without a Center **B.** and the Radius **B. F.** (where point **F.** is the unfathomable Divine Glory, which is the beginning and the conclusion of all things) and the "second motion" <note>original: "Motus secundus"; the completion of a circular path in geometry used as a metaphor for creation.</note> may be completed. And thus was the lower spiritual world—of which the Son of the Dawn was the master or ruler—ordained and created by the Majesty of the Most Holy. The eternal Divine Glory, however, mastered and governed this lower world with all its circles through the Seven great and mighty spirits of His most holy throne. Then, through Sachariel <note>In some traditions, an archangel associated with the planet Jupiter and the outpouring of divine light.</note>, the first influence of Divine Light went to this lower Spirit-World. This gentle and quiet movement of Divine Light would have continued in an incessant succession through these great spirits in their order (as it stood then) to this lower world, along with the infinite Divine Glory—had not the Son of the Dawn become a renegade through pride, and thereby caused Divine Justice <note>original: "Göttliche Gerechtigkeit"; here associated with the Hebrew name for God, Elohim (אלהים).</note> to cast him out and throw his glorious Spirit-World into this earthly confusion.

<summary>Welling describes God's existence as an eternal present and details the creation of the spiritual universe. He explains that Lucifer (the Son of the Dawn) was originally the central recipient of God's light, governed by seven great spirits, before his pride led to his fall and the creation of the material world.</summary>

<header>CHAPTER IV.</header>

<meta>catchword: Paradise/</meta>

§. 5. Thus **B** (namely the throne of the **Son of the Morning**) <note>original: "Sohn der Morgenröthe"; a name for the archangel who would become Lucifer, here shown in his unfallen state.</note> was the mirror in which the rays of the holy Divine Light concentrated themselves diametrically; from there, this holy Divine Light returned <note>original: "per repercussionem"; through reflection or bouncing back.</note> and radiated into the remaining circles of his <margin>10.</margin> mighty hosts **C. D. E.** down to the circle of the **Sons of God** <note>original: "בני אלהים" (Bnei Elohim).</note> see Genesis 6:2, Job 1:6, also 2:1 and 38:7. This circle was the last <margin>11.</margin> of the world of this Son of the Morning. For just as the Throne of God’s Glory consists of the Seven great and mighty Spirits who constantly hover before His most holy countenance, and the twelve circles of the spirits nearest to them end downward in the circle of the **Intelligences** <note>original: "Intelligentiarum"; in this context, these are high-ranking celestial beings who govern the movements of the spheres.</note>, so the world of the Son of the Morning ended in the <margin>12.</margin> circle of the **Sons of God**, whom the Almighty God mentions to Job in chapter 38:7. And so, in those unending times, the entire creation existed in perfectly peaceful light, peace, and joy: namely, in the Throne of Glory of the living God—which is the Seven great and mighty Spirits before His most holy countenance, through whom the outflows of His Majesty are guided to all others—and <margin>13.</margin> this is the **first world**. Furthermore, it consists of the Center and circumferences of the Son of the Morning, <margin>14.</margin> and this is the **second world**. Thirdly, it consists of the twelve circles or **Choirs** <note>original: "Choro".</note> of the innumerable <margin>15.</margin> spirits and angelic hosts, and this is the **third and middle world**. These spirits, angels, and dominions moved themselves into the Divine Light and rest solely by their own free will, to the praise of His glory; this might be compared to a constant <margin>16.</margin> gentle surging, without any compulsion. For compulsion was only later sired out of Justice and set against disobedience, which was sired out of pride. At this time, however, such things did not yet exist; instead, as mentioned, there was nothing but a silent and joyful <margin>17.</margin> movement through one another without any hindrance. This movement was like a very great light that, despite being moved from place to place according to its being <note>original: "secundum esse de loco ad locum".</note>, nevertheless casts its pleasant glow in all directions where it finds no obstacle; and as far as its lovely rays shine, the light itself may be seen, as if it <margin>18.</margin> were present there. In summary, here was nothing but a constant exchange of all joy, peace, and loveliness in the contemplation of Divine Majesty, in the constant sharing of light and power from one to another, as if in a transparency—of which we intend to think more than we <margin>19.</margin> can write. And so it was in the **first time**. These words should offend no one; for although we only now take our starting point <note>original: "Terminum à quo".</note> from **the beginning** <note>original: "בראשית" (Bereshit); the first word of Genesis.</note>—that is, from the start of the creation which Moses described to us through the Holy Spirit—this was actually the first time, when the Almighty God created the world just described. For previously, everything was in His <margin>20.</margin> own ever-present depth, without beginning or end of any time. But when the Almighty God allowed this world just described to proceed from Him, then there was also a beginning of all things and times. And because everything proceeded from God, was created glorious and good, and cannot be separated from God or exist for even a moment outside of God, this beginning of time will extend alongside Divine Eternity according to His most holy purpose. For the Good, because it is founded with its **root** <note>original: "Radice".</note> in God, will remain eternal like God. And even though it had a beginning—that is, a starting point—which was nothing other than the revealed will of the living God in these holy creatures, it shall nevertheless never have an end.

§. 6. How long this time lasted—that is, how long this perfection remained—is entirely inscrutable to us, because it happened before the beginning of our world-times; it remains sealed to us until the final revelation of Divine Majesty. Therefore, even from the foundation of the **Kabbalah** <note>original: "Cabala".</note>, we cannot know how long one of the seven great spirits would have ruled from the throne <margin>21.</margin> of God and the Lamb. This, however, is known through **tradition**, and we can also conclude from Kabbalistic principles, that **SACHARIEL** (that is, the **Spirit of Jupiter** according to the Greek name) ruled at the beginning of this creation. He also ruled at the time of the incarnation of our Savior and the Savior of all creatures. He was called by the Hebrews **Good Fortune** <note>original: "מזל טוב" (Massal Tof); literally 'Good Constellation'.</note> because of his bliss and goodness. We must conclude this much: because before the throne of the Living One there is neither day nor night, and thus no change of hours, days, months, and years, the time of their reign must be swift and unceasing, like a silent flash or glow (see Psalm 104:4, Hebrews 1:7).

§. 7. And this is now the first movement and creation of the Almighty, with which we wish to conclude this chapter for now, after first making a small mention of the <margin>22.</margin> words of St. Paul in 2 Corinthians 12:2. He was caught up to the **third heaven**, and in verse 4, he was caught up into **Paradise**. These words seem as though they might not easily be reconciled with this **Kabbalistic system**; but to speak briefly of this, the question is not to be debated in this chapter, as that which happened to St. Paul did not occur in this World of Light, but in the subsequent elementary worlds; for in this World of Light, nothing has yet been said of **Paradise**. In the meantime, we only say this: <margin>23.</margin> one should carefully consider the words of Christ in John 14:2:

> **In my Father's house are many dwellings; if it were not so, I would have told you. I go to prepare a place for you.**

Now St. Paul says he was caught up to the third heaven—he does not say "before the face of God"—and further, that he was caught up into

Paradise / here he names the place specifically, but at first generally, from which it is then quite irrefutably concluded / that he was caught up <note>original: "entzückt"; referring to St. Paul's ecstatic vision in 2 Corinthians 12.</note> into the third level of glory of this place / (called Paradise) / according to the above-cited words of **Jesus Christ** / in my Father's house are many dwellings <term>etc.</term> which were rightly called "Heaven" by Paul according to their excellence. <margin>24.</margin> Christ the Lord also promises this place to the thief in Luke 23:43 / for when the thief in verse 42 asked and said: **Lord / remember me / when you come into your kingdom /** then **Jesus** answered him in verse 43: **Truly / I say to you / today you shall be with me in Paradise.** Now, since Christ the Lord says this without any further specification, only generally of Paradise / it is to be understood as nothing other / than the first degree of the rest of the blessed / in the house of His dear heavenly Father / this is to be concluded as clear as day from the words of our Savior in John 20:17 / which even the gates of hell may not overcome: Namely / **Jesus** says to her (Mary) <margin>25.</margin> touch me not: For I am not yet ascended to my Father / but go to my brothers / and tell them: **I ascend to my Father / and your Father / and to my God and your God.** From which it is then clearly concluded / <margin>26.</margin> that He / through whom all things shall be restored that were lost in Adam / must also first take possession again / according to His human nature / of that place which Adam ruled and possessed before his fall in his creation / which was Paradise / but called <term>Eden</term> <note>Eden: a Hebrew word meaning "delight" or "pleasure."</note> by Moses. So the conclusion is also easy to make / even according to the Creed <note>original: "Symbolo"; referring to the Apostles' Creed.</note> of our Christian faith / where we pray: Crucified, died, buried, descended into hell, and on the third day rose again <term>etc.</term> and to conclude / that / while His most holy body rested in His grave during this time / He went in spirit and soul down to hell and into the prisons <note>A reference to 1 Peter 3:19, where Christ preaches to the "spirits in prison."</note> / (of what He performed there / we shall speak in the <margin>27.</margin> sequence in due time) and then ascended again to rest and into Paradise / until He took possession through a complete ascension above all the heavens of heavens. Therefore also the Royal Prophet says in Psalm 16:10: You will not leave my soul in <term>שאול</term> <note>original: "Sheol"; the Hebrew term for the underworld or the grave.</note>. That however <margin>28.</margin> the descent into hell must have been very rapid and like a flash of lightning / we learn from the words spoken to the thief / when He promised him with an oath / that he should be with Him in Paradise this very day; He does not say "in His kingdom," at the right hand of the power of God: So that <margin>29.</margin> during this time / during the departure of our Savior to hell / the thief had to pass through the judgments of the living God as if in a flash / and perform perfect penance for his misdeeds not fully atoned for in this world / through faith in Jesus Christ / in which he departed.

From this we then learn / that / whoever has not stood fully in true inward repentance in this life <margin>30.</margin> / will have to complete such in the other world / in his designated place / with far more immeasurable soul-anguish / in faith in Christ Jesus / before he will behold the place of rest / the place of man's first glory / namely Paradise / and from there be able to reach the sight of the face of Divine Majesty in Christ Jesus / of which, however, further shall be spoken hereafter.

§. 8. It would not have been necessary to cite the words of St. Paul here / for / as thought above / they actually belong in the following creation. However, we had to touch upon them a little here for quite special and important reasons / in order to lay the foundation stone for the <margin>31.</margin> following / with which we then close this chapter: Believing / that everyone / who has recognized God and the truth / will find full satisfaction herein; but this mystery shall be spoken of further in the Chapter of the Sabbath or the final chapter of this work / and explained through figures.

## CHAPTER V.

### Of the Fall of Lucifer, and the resulting separation, or Creation of this whole universe <note>original: "Creatio totius universi hujus"</note>.

#### §. I.

In the preceding chapter it has now been shown / as much as the Divine Light has communicated to us / how from the most holy Divine Will / the Spirit- or Light-World was begotten / and how at that time everything stood in the highest perfection / rest and glory and most immeasurable pleasure; how before the throne of His glory / and before the face of His most glorious Majesty / the seven great and powerful spirits / the spirits of His great power and wisdom / hovered / of which one may clearly read in the Apocalypse <note>Apocalypse: The Book of Revelation.</note>; and in this point of perfection / the outflow and brilliance of the Divine Light and glory went / through the spirit SACHARIEL (according to Cabalistic naming) see Fig. Num. 54. through all

delight / for the Almighty God does not want any compulsion at all 
<margin>35.</margin> toward any good: Therefore also the eternal most glorious Majesty / who is perfect

freedom / that is the own-will and self-lust / might not grow up into an eternal power running 
parallel to God / so that / where own-will / that is, rebelliousness 
and disobedience shows itself in any creature / justice would again be at the door to eradicate 
it / and not let it grow into a power 
<margin>36.</margin> lasting equal to God; And in this way is to be understood the justice and the judgment of the Holy 
God / and the damnation of the self-willed / that is, the wicked and unjust.

quiet life of joy / sought in its place and loved his own power / apart from the

Divine radiance within himself / whose desire and imagination immediately infected all his hosts; then, in an instant, the action of the Divine Light ceased within him, and this glorious radiance was entirely enclosed and turned inward within him, as well as in all the princes and hosts subject to his obedience, who were all essential outgrowths of his being; all these fell with him as far as the Cross of the Sons of God W. X.

<margin>See Fig. Num. 54. 37.</margin>
Since the Light, which has no other origin than in GOD, was no longer felt in him and all his followers, there was nothing in this great space but a terrifying darkness: namely the essence from which the space and the circles that this Lucifer occupied—which were his *Shamajim* <note>original: שָׁמַיִם; Hebrew for "heavens," which Welling interprets alchemically as a combination of *Esh* (fire) and *Mayim* (water)</note>, that is, a fiery light-water or watery light-fire—became, as it were, a tangible and material darkness <note>original Hebrew: חשך, *choshek*</note>, in which Lucifer, who had now become Satan <note>original Hebrew: שטן, *satan*, meaning an adversary</note>, raged and stormed with all his millions, lacking the Divine Light, in a hideous, dark, fiery form.

<margin>38.</margin>
This darkness is called "Earth" <note>original Hebrew: ארץ, *eretz*</note> by the holy text in Genesis 1:2, because those *Shamajim* in which Lucifer had dwelt in his glory had now become a thick, dark, tangible being; therefore the text says: **"And the earth was without form, and void; and darkness was upon the face of the deep. And the Spirit of God moved upon the face of the waters,"** etc. This verse is in truth so full of the mysteries of creation that it cannot be understood at all without the light of Divine grace. Above in the first chapter, sections 12 and following, we have already dealt thoroughly with this matter, though not in the manner that will be necessary here; therefore, the seeker of truth must compare this and the aforementioned passages with each other so that he may fully understand us. The text puts the Earth first, and thereafter that the Spirit of God hovered over the waters, and thus the Water <term>∇</term> <note>the alchemical symbol for the element of water</note> stands last.

<margin>39.</margin>
Now we have proven in the first chapter that Water was the first secondary quality, which we shall now perform even more clearly and distinctly through the Divine Light of grace: for we must go back a little and consider what first flowed out from the Divine Light in the creation of the fixed spirit-world from the <note>original text likely refers to the "Word" or "Son"</note>, that is, Christ, and what this was? What this was, we have fully handled in the first chapter, and we say only briefly here that the Almighty GOD is a Light <term>☿</term> <note>the symbol for Mercury, often used by Welling to represent the universal Spirit</note> and a consuming Fire <term>△</term> <note>the alchemical symbol for fire</note>, as He calls Himself; therefore, the outflow of His majesty could be nothing other than Light and Fire <term>△</term>, which the Holy Spirit calls *Shamajim* through Moses, that is, a fiery Water <term>∇</term> or a watery Fire <term>△</term>. The entire first chapter should be read for this, where this *Shamajim* is clearly and distinctly proven according to its essence. We see, then, that Lucifer's *Shamajim* or location was a fiery Water <term>∇</term> or a watery Fire <term>△</term>, a glorious wonder-light (though we must remind the reader that the *Shamajim* or Heavens differ in glory and beauty, according to the glory of the choirs and principalities that possess them). But when the Divine radiance and light were turned inward in this place or *Shamajim*, it was no longer a bright or light-water, but a dark, cold, grim-fiery, and tangible Water <term>∇</term>, a sand- and stone-making Water <term>∇</term>; and therefore Genesis 1:2 calls it Earth or Earthly, that is, Jovian <term>♃</term> <note>the symbol for Jupiter; in this context, it refers to the densification of spiritual matter into a more solid or "metallic" state</note>, and that was the Water <term>∇</term> or Chaos, which the text calls *Choshek*, that is, Darkness.
<margin>See Cap. 1. page 5. §. 14.</margin>

<margin>40.</margin>
§. 4. Now the Chaos consisted of the following figure (see Figure No. 55), namely, in the dark, earthly, grim, fiery <term>△</term> water upon which the Spirit of God moved (Genesis 1:2), whereas the *Shamajim* is a lovely light of joy and light-water.

<margin>41.</margin>
In this Chaos, the rebellious spirit was now locked and shut in with all his followers, and thus had to experience what it means to be lacking the Divine Light. This Chaos, with its enclosed foul spirits, was nevertheless still that same thing which had initially flowed out from the eternal Divine *Fiat* <note>Latin: "let it be done"; the creative command of God</note>, but inverted; and instead of pure light, it was pure darkness and horror. Yet, by Divine unrestricted power, it could be made again from this place—which only accidentally became a curse through its ruler, Lucifer—into whatever was pleasing to His eternal Divine counsel.

§. 5. Now the text says in Genesis 1:1 that in the beginning the Elohim <note>original Hebrew: אלהים; the name of God used in the creation narrative</note> created the heavens and the earth; that was the aforementioned Chaos, namely, the spheres or circles of Lucifer and all his created host, which through the righteous vengeance of holy Divine judgment was reduced into this dark mixture in which the heavens and the earth were hidden,

<margin>See in the 1st Cap.</margin>
namely, see the three Principles: Sun <term>☉</term>, Jupiter <term>♃</term>, and Mercury <term>☿</term>; that is, *Shamajim*, which is Fire <term>△</term> as well as Water <term>∇</term>, namely the mystical Mercury <term>☿</term>: *toto prior* <note>Latin: "prior to the whole"</note> or the being created solely by GOD the Almighty, which is also three, yet made manifest in four.

<margin>42.</margin>
In the second verse the text says: "The earth was without form and void, and the Spirit of GOD moved upon the face of the waters." What is called Heaven, that is, *Shamajim*, in the first verse, is here in the second called *Mayim* <note>original Hebrew: מים; water</note>, and here the *Esh* <note>original Hebrew: אשׁ; fire</note>, that is, Fire <term>△</term>, is omitted because it had not yet become visible or manifest through Divine separation. This Chaos, which was like a melted dead body in whose slime lies hidden the entire essence of the individual, according to all its terrestrial principles (for every body has its terrestrial, vegetative, and spiritual principles, that is, the *vim spermaticam* <note>Latin: "generative power" or "seed-force"</note>), yet entirely invisible, and never to be recognized or felt without the right separation, because the entire body is melted as if into a slimy Water <term>∇</term>, which according to its external form is only one thing; thus is this Chaos also to be understood, which according to its external form was of a hideous shape; therefore the holy text says: *ve-ha-aretz hayetah tohu va-bohu* <note>original Hebrew: וְהָאָרֶץ הָיְתָה תֹהוּ וָבֹהוּ</note>, meaning without form and void.

that is, of a desolate, abominable form, which could not eternally endure before the most holy face of the Elohim <note>original: אלהים; Hebrew for "God," here interpreted by the author as "mighty Judges"</note>, that is, the mighty Judges.

§. 6. This gives us occasion to mention something of the reason why Moses, driven by the Holy Spirit, first names the eternal Godhead in the **Sacred Codex** <note>original Latin: *Sacro Codice*, referring to the Bible</note> with the glorious and awesome name אלהים, that is, **Elohim**, meaning the **mighty Judges;** (which the oldest and best Rabbis and Cabalists explain to be the **Name of Judgment** <note>original Latin: *Nomen Judicii*</note>). In the 4th verse of the 2nd chapter, however, he mentions for the first time the **Name of Grace** <note>original Latin: *Nominis Gratiæ*</note> יהוה <note>original: *Jehovah* or the Tetragrammaton</note>, **with the addition** of the name Elohim. Of this name יהוה the Almighty GOD Himself says in *Exodus 3:15*: **"This is my name forever, and this is my memorial unto all generations,"** etc. To understand this properly, we must return to the previous chapter and consider Figure Number 54. There we see how everything in the spirit-world stood in perfect glory and rest—until the self-will of Lucifer intervened—within the name of eternal grace and mercy, יהוה; thus, the Justice of GOD (of which we spoke above under numbers 3, 5, and 36, §. 2 of this chapter) was not yet revealed.

<margin>43.</margin>
But as soon as self-will, self-seeking, and the elevation of self manifested in Lucifer (which is essentially rebellion, malice, and disobedience), the Justice of the eternal God—that is, ELOHIM—was also revealed. Therefore, the Holy Spirit, through Moses, immediately uses these first words of Holy Scripture, even before heaven and earth had become visible or manifest out of the Chaos: **"In the beginning, the Elohim, that is, the mighty Judges—Father, Son, and Holy Spirit—created the heavens and the earth;"** that is, the Chaos, or the mass mixed together from Lucifer’s place with all his hosts.

<margin>44.</margin>
The Holy Spirit did not use this manner of speaking without special cause; namely, to encourage those who love God and truth to seek this **Mystery** <note>original Latin: *Mysterium*</note> in the Divine Light: why the living GOD, at the beginning of creation, first calls Himself by His most holy name of judgment, Elohim, before the name of eternal grace, יהוה, which one reads for the first time in Holy Scripture after the completion of creation. Namely, to show thereby how something must truly have happened that caused the living GOD to act in judgment, and how the Chaos, or the desolate and empty mass, did not stand in Divine Love, but in Wrath.

<margin>45.</margin>
For nothing can or may proceed from GOD but perfection and glory in magnificent light and radiance, as we have seen in the previous chapter regarding the creation of the spirit- or light-world. Since before anything was created from eternity, there was nothing but GOD, and thus nothing existed outside of GOD, it is then true and certain that we can in no way understand "creation out of nothing" through the Divine Light otherwise than that when Almighty GOD created, it happened out of and from His own essence: namely, from His glorious light and radiance.

<margin>46.</margin>
Therefore, the Chaos must necessarily have endured a destructive suffering before it fell from the Divine Light-radiance into this desolate, empty, and dark spiritual (that is, earthly) mass. This the Spirit of GOD allows us—and all who love eternal Divine truth—to recognize perfectly through His most holy name Elohim; and thus the God-loving soul will be fully satisfied regarding this **passage** <note>original Latin: *Locum*; Genesis 1:1</note>: **"In the beginning, the Elohim created the heavens and the earth."**

<margin>47.</margin>
7. §. We must also remind the reader that although this name Elohim is applied to men in Holy Scripture, it is in no other sense than that they exercise the office of judge in God's stead. It may then be translated into German or other languages as "judges" in general, or "mighty judges" specifically. But the translation as "gods" or "God," with which hypocrites flatter men, is fundamentally false and was spun by the Father of Lies as a blasphemy against the holy GOD, who is praised for eternity. For the word "God" <note>original German: *GOTT*</note> is equivalent to יהוה, which can or may never be eternally applied to any miserable mortal or immortal creature, whether in heaven or on earth; and cursed be he who does so for all eternity. This name Elohim is also sometimes applied to idols mockingly, following the malicious manner of speaking of men, as seen in *Exodus 22:20*; however, in the holy text, this refers more to the act of idolatry than the **essence** <note>original Latin: *Essential*</note> of the idol. No learned man, no matter how wise he thinks himself, will ever be able to prove from Holy Divine Scripture that the name Elohim was ever applied to the Devil. It says in *1 Samuel 28:13*: **"I saw Elohim ascending out of the earth,"** etc.

<margin>48.</margin>
That this was the true ethereal soul of Samuel, who had been an Elohim (that is, a judge) in Israel, is true and certain. We defy all "Antichrist-scholars" to prove to us in any way, without petty academic **distinctions** <note>original Latin: *Distinctiones*</note>, that the Devil is ever called Elohim in Holy Scripture, either as a God or as a judge.

<margin>49.</margin>
But it shall certainly be just as difficult for them as proving that Balaam was a sorcerer; which they indeed claim daily, yet it holds as much truth as comparing Venus <note>represented by the symbol ♀</note> to the Sun <note>represented by the symbol ☉</note>. If these people, before they boasted of their theology, had been like the priests of the Lord—Magi and Cabalists—then one might believe them. But instead, it is said of them: "we have been taught thus by men, by the highly learned, who have thus glossed and interpreted it." Therefore, if these people knew what man consists of—which the Scripture teaches perfectly clearly and distinctly—and if they understood the words of Christ, the Savior of all the world, which we cited above in **Chapter IV, §. 7** and, through God's grace, brought as much opportunity as was there...

That Samuel must still have been kept in such a place is clearly stated in the aforementioned history; but that he stood in a quiet, peaceful place in a state of longing and waiting for the appearance of God, and thus had no hellish suffering or torment of the impure spirits and souls around him, we must conclude from his holy life and conduct. Then all the men of his city shall stone him with stones, so that he dies: and you shall thus put away evil from among you: so that all Israel may hear it and fear.** This text speaks of gluttons and drunkards, whom parents should not overlook, much less those who turned toward avarice, took bribes, and perverted justice, etc., as the sons of Samuel did; thus Samuel had to endure this purification in this place because of earthly and fleshly love against the Spirit—namely, GOD. For according to the Law of God, he should have been the first to deliver them to punishment and death; but instead, out of softness, he placed his own love before the Law of God, which desire he had to let be fully washed away here in a place of longing; and this is the reason that at that time he still stood <term>בשאול</term> <note>original Hebrew: *be-Sheol*</note> that is, **in Sheol**, which is the place of the containment of souls, from whence he was disturbed by the spirit of the woman at Endor. Yet enough of this for now; there will be further opportunity to speak more extensively on this.

### The First Day's Work.

§. 8. The Holy Spirit continues further in the 3rd verse of *Genesis 1* to recount the first separation in this chaos: **Then GOD said, let there be a light, and there was a light** etc. This was the first separation, namely the revelation of the light in the darkness, so that now the *Principia* <note>Latin: "Principles" or "Fundamental elements"</note> from which Almighty GOD wished to create all things visible and invisible were manifest and recognizable; see <note>Figure Number 56 depicts the initial emergence of light as a central point of radiation within the swirling dark matter of chaos.</note>. Furthermore, the text says: **so it became evening, so it also became morning, the first day.** This day now was the first period of 24 hours, as is clearly evident from the contrast of the fourth day's work, on which the sun and moon were created, and thus the true night and the true day were first created through the *Motum Circularem Luminarium* <note>Latin: "Circular motion of the luminaries," i.e., the orbits of the sun and moon.</note>; for both this first day and that fourth day are given the name <term>יום</term> <note>original Hebrew: *Yom*</note> that is, **Day**, in the same sense. Thus, on this first day, by the power of the revealed light, it was bright in one hemisphere—that is, day—and because the coarser and darker elements remained below, it was simultaneously night in the other hemisphere. But because nothing created is or can exist without *Motu* or movement (as *Psalm 74:16* says: **Yours is the day, yours also is the night: you have prepared both, the light and the sun**), this revealed light and darkness made the very distinction between day and night which the luminaries made on the fourth day's work, as is clear and manifest through the words of distinction used by the Holy Spirit through Moses, such as <term>ערב</term> <note>original Hebrew: *Erev*; Welling glosses this with the Latin *vespera*</note> that is, **Evening**, and <term>בקר</term> <note>original Hebrew: *Boqer*; Welling glosses this with the Latin *mane*</note> that is, **Morning**. Verse 3 says: **Let there be a light, and there was a light** etc. This now was the first-mentioned revelation of the light and the darkness, and furthermore, all heavenly lights and bodies under the firmament were created from this light. Verse 4 also says: **And GOD saw that the light was good, and God made a distinction between the light and the darkness.** The Hebrew word is: <term>ויבדל</term> <note>original Hebrew: *Vajabhdel*</note> that is, **He divided** or distinguished. This distinction, according to the proper understanding of the Hebrew word, is such that when one distinguishes a thing, each of the separated parts has a special place without encompassing the other; see <note>Figure Number 57 illustrates the geometrical separation of light and dark into two distinct domains or spheres.</note>. In the 5th verse it stands: **And GOD called the light Day, and the darkness Night.** Which then shows us clearly and irrefutably that both the Day—that is, the light—as well as

# The Second Day’s Work.

<margin>Genesis 1:6.</margin>
§. 9. Now follows the second time or day’s work, in which the Almighty God created the heavens (<term>Schamajim<gloss>Hebrew for "heavens," often interpreted by Welling as a combination of fire and water</gloss></term>), i.e., the upper fiery or light-waters, i.e., the firmament, and separated them from the muddy, earthy, coarse salt-waters and sulfurous-waters <note>The symbols $\ominus$ (Salt) and $\triangle$ (Fire/Sulfur) are used here to describe the chemical nature of the primordial chaotic waters.</note>; for the 6th verse actually says that God made the expansion, or the stretching out, between the waters here below and the waters above in the height, as the 7th verse fully explains. Here, the actually so-called earth where we dwell is not yet mentioned, because it was still entirely mixed and hidden within the coarse, muddy salt-waters; for we proved quite irrefutably in the first chapter that all water <note>original: $\nabla$ (the symbol for water)</note> possesses a power within itself to create sand and stone. The Heaven, however, is properly mentioned in the 8th verse, and the expansion or stretching out is called Heaven; namely, both the circle which is actually the Heaven—specifically the firmament—as well as the expansion or fire <note>original: $\triangle$ (the symbol for fire)</note> between the upper waters and the waters here below. This includes everything that was separated from the water below and was of a spiritual water; thus, the mercury was separated from the jupiter <note>original: $\mercury$ à $\jupiter$</note> and oil. This distinction between the two—the upper fiery and the lower muddy saline waters—is not our coarse air that we breathe, but rather the great Ether <note>original: "Æthereum"</note> between the upper fiery water and the earthly waters here below. Within this expanse or expansion, the circles of all the stars are enclosed, which will be discussed later during their creation. In this expansion, all the powers of heaven are specified and formed together according to their own quality into each body of the upper lights; from there, through the operation of the Moon, they are poured into our watery air and further into this earthly circle. Concerning this, consult the previous chapters with all diligence.

And God called the dry land Earth, and the gathering together of the waters he called Seas, etc. Here, another separation has occurred within the muddy saline and sulfurous waters here below; namely, its sand- and stone-making power becomes manifest. In this separation, the Almighty God performed a quite astonishing miracle; for it was not (as the majority believe) a mere revelation of the great sea and the ground of the earth as a thick, dark, massive
<margin>Genesis 1:9.</margin>
lump. Rather, the fiery pool was also separated, which burns with fire and sulfur <note>original: $\triangle$ and $\triangle$</note>; this is the entire inner and central region (see Chapter I and Figure No. 1). Likewise, the great air-region F.G. and the spring-sources of the great abyss were separated, namely the great water-region E.F. All of these are not only dwellings, prisons, and storehouses for the devils, the damned, and the spirits and souls standing in suffering and torment, but they also serve to perform the works which the Almighty God, Father and Creator of all creatures, commanded them, as described in the first chapter of this work. Thus, the proud spirit <note>referring to Lucifer</note> who was so magnificent and powerful is now cast down, and against all his thoughts and hopes, is separated from his power and might, and firmly locked away, and bound with chains of darkness until the fire <note>original: $\triangle$</note> of judgment and vengeance shall be kindled over him and all the damned by the breath of the Lord. This fire will not cease until it has consumed all works of darkness and the offspring of damnation—that is, everything they have practiced in words, works, or thoughts—until the image of the devil is utterly destroyed. When this after-fire <note>original: "Nach $\triangle$"</note> finds nothing more to crush, destroy, or overturn, the fallen devil with all his followers shall appear again in his pure, original angelic form, having laid hold of the eternal redemption of Jesus Christ; and the eternal rest grounded in God shall manifest itself again in him and everywhere. Furthermore, it should be remembered that this fall of Lucifer occurred during the time of the reign of ORIFIEL <note>In esoteric tradition, Orifiel is the Archangel of Saturn, associated with the most distant and cold sphere.</note> (see Figure No. 53). The Latin language calls this spirit Saturn, and from this, the fable among the pagan poets may have arisen: that Jupiter cast Saturn out of heaven and gave him the earth to inhabit, etc.

§. 11. Thus, the fallen Lucifer and all his hosts were appointed each their own special place and region, in which they shall be kept until the determined times of judgment. Lucifer, as the original source of all corruption, is locked and sealed in the fiery pool that burns with fire and sulfur <note>original: $\triangle$ and $\triangle$</note>, namely in the central fire-region, with his most malicious, fierce, and rebellious spirits who served him most vehemently in his fall and rebellion; these are the ones who dwelt in the sphere of his throne

Now, however, they cannot or do not wish to unite into a single host, but each part must keep its dwelling or element, and cannot endure or bear the other element. And although the devils of the air can pass through all other elemental circles, yet it happens in the greatest wrath and suffering; thus they cannot (notwithstanding that they are of one and the same malice, mind, and rebelliousness against the Creator with all the others in the other elements) <margin>56.</margin> unite and remain together with all these others—namely as to substance <note>original: "sc. quoad substantiam"</note>, for as to quality <note>original: "quoad qualitatem"</note> they eternally remain one and the same—because their meeting happens in cruel, grim, raging, and suffering traversals of the elements. Their malice (that of the air-devils) is most grimly strengthened and inflamed by the other devils of each element, and thus they return much more maddened and cruel to their dwelling, until the day of their second judgment, when they shall be cast down into the great abyss, Revelation 20:1 & 3. <margin>57.</margin> In the times of the glorious <margin>See Chapter III, § 5, Letter A, etc.</margin> Kingdom of Peace and Joy of JESUS CHRIST, the length of which is presented to us under the number of 1,000 years—but whether it will actually be 1,000 of our current solar years, we leave to the judgment of those to whom the Spirit has kindled a brighter light in these matters. As much as we can perceive, however, it might be compared to a holy time, namely in the number that concludes all times, which is 7. Whether it will be 7,000 or more times 7,000 years is still hidden from us. Psalm 90:4 says: "For a thousand years in thy sight are but as yesterday when it is past," <margin>58.</margin> yes, even like a **watch in the night**, etc. 1,000 years were nearly the years of the life of the first soulful man, namely Adam; but when the firstborn of all creatures, from whom all creatures proceeded—namely Christ JESUS, the heavenly and spiritual Adam—comes to set up His kingdom, should this last only 1,000 common years? That is, should it not extend longer than the years of the life of one of the first sinful patriarchs? This does not seem to rhyme well with many places in Holy Scripture (especially where a **Mystical Number** <note>original: "Numerus Mysticus"</note> is used); yet we do not wish to preempt others herein to whom a brighter light has dawned from the Holy Spirit. This, however, we are convinced of in the spirit: that this present world was not created for the sake of 6 or <margin>59.</margin> 7,000 year-periods by Him who has neither beginning of times nor end of days, as many brave people have already erred in such interpretations of the Holy Books. # CHAPTER V.

<margin>64.</margin> Earthly beings have a body qualified from the element of Earth; those in the water from the element of Water; those in the air have a body qualified from the Air; but the fiery creatures have a body from the pure element of Fire. Now, the origin of the visible elements has been fully proven before: that prior to the fall of Lucifer, these were the Circles—that is, the Heavens <note>original: "Schamajim"</note>—the dwelling of him and all his innumerable hosts. They were entirely simple, but through the turning away of the Divine Light, they became Darkness <note>original: "חשך" (Choschech)</note>. We have just heard how these were further separated again. Now, the lover of truth should further know that we do not say here that these creatures have coarse and material bodies like the visible and tangible elements; for if so, we would be speaking against ourselves, and they would then not be invisible but visible creatures. Rather, they are (to use a <margin>65.</margin> <term>Chemical Term|In this context, "chemical" refers to alchemy, the precursor to modern chemistry, which dealt with the spiritual and physical purification of matter.</term>) the <term>Quintessence|The "Quinta Essentia" or fifth essence; the most refined, purest, and most spiritual part of a substance, believed by alchemists to be the underlying spirit of the elements.</term>, the very purest and most spiritual part of each element. Yet this too is to be understood by degree <note>original: "secundum magis ac minus"</note>, according to their goodness. For just as the center of malice in each element consists of the enclosed devils, who are the most evil of all, so also the purest part of each element consists of these beings or creatures. However, the closer they extend in their diameter toward the center of malice, the more evil and dangerous they <margin>66.</margin> are. The creatures of the element of Fire <note>original symbol: $\triangle$</note> are the purest; after them, those of the Air; <margin>67.</margin> then those in the Water <note>original symbol: $\nabla$</note>, and finally the earthly ones. The time of their creation occurred during the <margin>Time of the creation of the elemental creatures.</margin> separation of the elements, in the first, second, and third days' work: such that the fiery ones were created in the first day's work, the airy ones in the second, and the watery and earthly ones in the third day's work. Thus, those are greatly mistaken who have believed that the holy angels, with all their choirs, were created in the first day's work out of the separated light. Certainly, neither the first creation of the spirit-world nor this earthly world was known to them in any way; yet we gladly let everyone believe what suits their taste.

->§. 13.<-

Shortly above, we stated that these creatures are not of one single goodness, neither according to the elements in general <note>original: "in genere"</note> nor in particular <note>original: "in specie"</note>. According to the elements in general, the element of Fire <note>original symbol: $\triangle$</note> prevails over the Air <note>original symbol: $\unicode{x1F702}$</note>; the Air over the Water <note>original symbol: $\nabla$</note>; and the Water over the Earth <note>original symbol: $\unicode{x1F704}$</note>; as was heard just before. In particular, they are not of one goodness according to how they extend in each element toward the center of malice—the <margin>68.</margin> locked up and banished devils—as we can best prove through Figure Number 58: Where **B.** is the Throne of Satan, namely the hellish fiery region, the fiery pool that burns with fire and brimstone <note>original symbol for sulfur: $\text{\jupiter}$</note>, the center of wrath and the vengeance of GOD, where all the malice of the devils is gathered and the most cruel grimness and torment is locked away. **C.** is the Earthly Region. **D.** is the Water Region. **E.** is the Air Region. **X. W.** is the Fiery Region of the Heavens <note>original: "Schamajim"</note>. Now, it is indisputable that from the diameter **B. W.**, the point **C.** is closer to the Holy Light-world or Angelic world than point **G.** or **F.** of the subterranean Fire and Water; also **D.** is already closer than **C.** and thus more distant from the kingdom of hell; point **E.** is also closer to the light, and indeed the fiery region **X. W.** is the border of the Angelic world. It will now be easy to understand how these creatures differ in goodness in general. How they differ in particular, we wish to demonstrate and show by the earthly ones: there, **B.** is Satan's seat; **C.** is the Earth-circle, the realm of darkness. The creatures that are closest to the surface **C.**, and are shone upon by the rays of the Superior Light through the diameter **W. C.**, are still good and pure; those in the middle of the earth are of both good and evil inclination; but those who are closest to the subterranean water region, and thus to the great fiery whirlpool, are the worst of all, and are very often the couriers of the devils. <margin>69.</margin> For Satan, as the former absolute lord of all these regions, knows their essential substance, and knows how to masterfully use those closest to him through his cruel cunning and <margin>70.</margin> deceit to plunge the human creature, as his adversary, into destruction. It is the same with the element of Water; for just as the Water is a mirror of the light, and the Water is a mother or birthing-mother of the remaining elements—as we proved in the first chapter—so also its spirits or creatures are powerful, wise, and wonderful. <margin>71.</margin> The devil cannot use them as easily as those created from the dark element of the Earth. The Water is sanctified and is a Mystic Element <note>original: "Elementum Mysticum"</note>; and even though the devil practices his malice therein, he is nevertheless discovered very quickly through the constantly shining light, as if in a bright mirror; for he is the prince of darkness and shuns the light, and <margin>72.</margin> thus can practice the least malice in the Water. But with the spirits created in the Air, it is of an entirely different nature, and there is a mystery here that we believe will be revealed to few. For although the element of Air <note>original symbol: $\unicode{x1F702}$</note> is closest to the Light-Fire, and is thus far purer than the earthly water, yet, firstly, in this cruel and vast region, the governor of <margin>73.</margin> Lucifer, Beelzebub, has his dwelling with all his millions of devils. Secondly, all impressions occur in this element: all forms and ideas of human deeds, both of their good and evil speeches, as well as those of the upper stars and their powers, together with the hideous shapes of the devils and their grim raging and storming, are substantially pressed into this element. Now, no living creature can live or be maintained without taking a breath; because then, through the constant breathing in and letting out, all such forms and ideas are guided to the human through the senses to the fantasy, and from the form to the mind, according to the time and station. Thus, in the night-time, joy or <margin>74.</margin> sadness often overcomes a person, for which he cannot give the slightest cause; but upon the lighting of a fire...
