# Of the Fall of Lucifer, etc.

<margin>75.</margin> Or with the approach of a light, all this nocturnal fear vanishes, so that even the most fearful can then remain in such hideous and frightening places without further terror. This is also the reason why travelers are mostly seized with fear and terror, especially on dark nights, when they travel over a field or through a thicket where cruel battles and bloodshed have occurred; for the air in such places is itself full of substantial, yet spiritual forms of all those cruel deeds, which are presented to our minds through our breathing, as was just mentioned. It often happens to a traveler that in a lonely place, even in broad daylight, a shudder or horror overcomes him, for which no other true cause can be given than that a murder or other cruel deed must have been committed in that place, the idea and form of which were substantially impressed upon the Fire <note>The symbol $\triangle$ represents the element of Fire, which Welling considers the active spiritual principle that "records" events.</note> at that location.

<margin>76.</margin> The cause of all heavy and hideous dreams—especially for a soul that distances itself from the light of the Holy Spirit, or fails to commend itself to the Holy Divine Light upon going to sleep—is also to be sought here.

<margin>77.</margin> The Devil, as a Prince of Darkness, shuns the light and is not so cruel and grim around us in the bright light of day as he is in the dark of night; for when the sun departs and darkness approaches, the swarm of all devils also draws near, along with all the cruel deeds of themselves and their tools (evil men). From this arises the terrible, hideous fear of the night and the loathsome, evil dreams just mentioned.

<margin>78.</margin> That the devils rage more cruelly among the dark poles or world-vortices, and have their main seat in these dark and cold parts of the world, is beyond all doubt. For he is the Prince of Darkness, and the histories of those regions have sufficiently taught us how he has cunningly subjected not just individuals, but entire provinces and lands, to his cruel authority.

<margin>79.</margin> The air is the great book of conscience, wherein all people will one day find all their good and evil deeds substantially preserved; indeed, even the slightest word will not be forgotten here, for tone or sound is a substantial birth of the soul. If many good births have proceeded from his (the human’s) soul, he shall have reward and joy for it; if not, it will happen to him as it is written: that men must give an account even for every idle word, etc. And what is written in Revelation 14, verse 13 <note>original: "Apoc. XIV. v. 12" — Note: Welling cites verse 12, but the quote "their works follow them" is found in verse 13.</note>: "For their works follow them." They shall find this perfectly realized here.

<margin>80.</margin> For truly the air is the great mother of witnesses, in which everything done and worked by men is received and preserved, as was just cited.

<margin>81.</margin> Yes, through the Fire [$\triangle$] our imagination works quite supernaturally; for example, if one mind stands in equal harmony with another completely absent one, both will become partakers of a certain joy or sadness in the very same span of time or moment <note>original: "ipsissimo temporis spatio f. momento"</note>. The reason <note>original: "Ratio"</note> is because the Fire [$\triangle$] has substantially received the form or idea <note>original: "Ideam"</note> that caused this joy or sadness, and the sharp rays <note>original: "Radios"</note> of our imagination on both sides have greedily drawn it in and received and impressed it into our minds. Love and hate, indeed all other movements of the soul <note>original: "Motus animæ"</note>, are born from and in us

<margin>82.</margin> in this way. AND what is more, there is no generation—whether of rational or irrational beings, plants, animals, or minerals <note>original: "seu Rationalium seu Irrationalium, vegetabilium, Animalium vel Mineralium"</note>—that is not also substantially begotten in a spiritual manner in this great World-Spirit, the Air. From this Fire [$\triangle$] (which passes through the other elements quickly and easily), the Water [$\nabla$] is further fecundated and impregnated; hence, in the mountains and clefts, such strange births occur through petrifying waters <note>original: "aquas Petrificantes"</note>, consisting of skeletons <note>original: "Sceletis"</note> of strange animals and humans, as well as all sorts of other generations—not only in caves and clefts, but also in shale and other stones; concerning this, see Chapter I, §. 15, letter z.

<margin>83.</margin> Indeed, if we did not intend otherwise here than to treat one matter in its specific kind <note>original: "in specie"</note> most extensively according to all its circumstances, we would find enough opportunity in this material and in this element to complete a very large and extensive work <note>original: "Opus"</note>. For we have not yet even considered the specters <note>original: "Spectris"</note> or ghosts of the departed—both those who died naturally and those strangled in war or otherwise violently killed. This material, contrary to all School Theology <note>academic or orthodox church doctrine</note>, is so extensive that it would make up a very large work; for even those who still believe in these appearances according to the truth are nonetheless poorly informed regarding the circumstances.

<margin>84.</margin> For those souls who have their station or dwelling in the air are truly in a very bad state for the reasons just recounted, unless they departed in rest and peace of conscience and are found among those of whom Wisdom says: **"And no torment shall touch them."** Wisdom [of Solomon] Chapter 3, verse 1 <note>original: "Sap. Cap. III. v. 1"</note>. These souls appear easily and are also easily brought to appearance because the element of Fire [$\triangle$] can pass through all others so easily and quickly. Conversely, they are also easily freed and helped to rest through the devout prayer and intercession of the faithful.

<margin>85.</margin> After the soul has departed in its sharp imagination (turned away from God) in greed, envy, revenge, or whatever its desire may have been, it will then mostly appear in such circumstances, yet generally without any speech.

<margin>86.</margin> But he who is practiced and experienced in true Magic, Kabbalah, and Theology <note>original: "vera Magia, Cabala und Theologia"</note> will be able to address them and also receive a correct answer.

<margin>87.</margin> It also often happens that a soul lets itself be seen constantly in one place, not just for one, but for many days, months, and years. Of these, one should know that there are two causes that keep them in such places: first, the damned greed in which their imagination stood at the time of departure, having buried their money and treasure in that place out of envy, whereby they...

### CHAPTER V.

Then it must sit in great suffering and guard that same [treasure], in the company of many rebellious and impure spirits, in heat and cold. For here, it is not the immortal spirit immediately breathed in by God that suffers, but rather the soul which man has received <term>per traducem</term> <note>original: "per traducem"; a theological term referring to Traducianism, the belief that the soul is transmitted from parents to children through natural generation, rather than being a direct, new creation from God.</note>, according to the foul state of its inclinations during life, from which it may not be released, 
<margin>88.</margin> unless its appointed times of purification have passed. And if, during this time, such money or treasure is not found, the soul then falls entirely into the power of the malicious earth-spirits, from whom it may not be snatched away, except by a Magus, Cabalist, or Theologian; every other undertaking in this matter is <term>Necromantic</term> <note>Associated with the forbidden practice of conjuring the spirits of the dead for selfish or dark purposes.</note>, devilish, and occurs through cruel adjurations and the blasphemous abuse of the most holy name of God. 
<margin>89.</margin> Such devilish writings and books—among which the so-called 
<margin>90.</margin> <term>Clavicula Salomonis</term> <note>The "Key of Solomon," a famous historical grimoire or book of magic.</note> is not the least—are to be obtained both secretly and publicly. It is true that in these writings the true art, <term>Magia</term> and <term>Cabala</term>, is contained, but it is defiled with the most sinful and blasphemous abuse of the most holy Divine Name, so that a true lover of God cannot look upon them without trembling. Therefore, every God-seeking soul 
<margin>91.</margin> is warned neither to read nor use such writings, unless it possesses a small light of Magic and Cabala and is grounded in <term>vera Theologia</term> <note>original: "vera Theologia"; true theology.</note>; for then, it will be able to separate the good from the evil with great benefit and make use of the good.

->§. 14. Briefly above, we said that there are two main causes why a ghost<-

or soul (a. We are not speaking here of the indestructible spirit of life in man, which he receives immediately from God, but of the soul, which he has received <term>per traducem</term>) appears so long in a place and lets itself be seen, one of which has already been mentioned. The 
<margin>92.</margin> other is: that such a soul committed a cruel, evil deed during its life which remained secret and hidden from men, and thus the judgment in this world did not pass over it; therefore, in suffering after death, it must do penance at that place in quite cruel anguish and martyrdom, according to the state of the deed it committed, and it is most cruelly tortured for such a deed by 
<margin>93.</margin> the impure spirits. If it was a secret murder, and the murdered person also departed in impurity, then the state of such souls is even more cruel, as it is most terrifyingly tortured by the slain one alongside the impure spirits. It cannot even hope for the beginning of its purification times 
<margin>94.</margin> until the murdered soul has first gone to rest through all the degrees of its purification; only then does the time of its own purification actually begin. Here, the prayers and intercessions of the believers still wandering in life can achieve much; however, those intercessions rattled off from the pulpit or before the altar for a few coins achieve not the slightest thing. A <term>Magus Cabaliste</term> and <term>verus Theologus</term> <note>A practitioner of spiritual magic and a true theologian.</note> can accomplish much here. But alas! how few are they, O Lord, who seek Thee in spirit and in truth? This very extensive matter has now been touched upon briefly as far as the occasion here allows, to provide a small foretaste; more might be said of it in its proper place, should it be fitting. Only this shall be mentioned a little further, namely: that those who often violently curse such spirits and ghosts, wishing them ill and condemning them to the abyss of hell, act most wrongly and sin greatly. By doing so, they make the evil condition of such poor souls hovering in great pain even worse; indeed, often through such unchristian behavior, they put their parents, husbands, wives, children, and other close blood-relatives and friends into even worse suffering than they already feel. It would be a thousand times better if a devout prayer were offered for such an erring soul to snatch it from its torment and suffering, rather than plunging it into worse through banishment and cursing.

<margin>95. §. 15.</margin> We have thus dealt—as much as was necessary for what follows—with the state of the spirits of darkness and the forms and ideas of all works of light and darkness, as well as human deeds and speeches, and even their thoughts, which are impressed upon the Fire <note>Welling uses the symbol $\triangle$ for Fire.</note>. We say even thoughts: and it is true that the thoughts of men are thus revealed in the Fire through a spiritual birth, either through secret or public speech, or other physical or soulful movements of the body. Otherwise, it would be possible for neither the Magician nor the Necromancer to represent and reveal such things in <term>Speculis</term>, <term>Crystallen</term>, <term>Perillen</term>, etc. <note>Mirrors, crystals, and beryl lenses used for scrying or vision-work.</note>. Thus, those who believe that the Devil does not know the thoughts of men are greatly mistaken. We return, then, to the creatures of the Fire, of which we began to speak above in § 13, under number 72, page 56. These creatures, because they consist of no commixture or composition (just like the earthly creatures and those created from the 
<margin>96.</margin> water), but are the <term>simplicissima Essentia Elementi</term> <note>original: "simplicissima Essentia Elementi"; the simplest essence of the element.</note>, the power of these spirits is like the Fire, their element, very mighty and terrible. Their knowledge and recognition of past, present, and future things is 
<margin>97.</margin> immeasurable, yet at the same time very confused, false, and deceptive. Because they consist <term>ex simplicissima Essentia Elementari</term> <note>original: "ex simplicissima Essentia Elementari"; from the simplest elementary essence.</note>, they are capable of all impressions; therefore, alongside the knowledge of the truth, they are also blinded with lies and falsehood by the impure spirits, 
<margin>98.</margin> to which are added all the other causes we have just recounted in order. Moreover, they are likewise distinguished in goodness; the closer they are to the Light, the purer, more perfect, and better they are; but the closer to the Darkness, the more harmful, evil, and seductive. Those in the middle, between Light 
<margin>99.</margin> and Darkness, are both good and evil. And although they are generally well-disposed and inclined toward humans, one must nevertheless be exceedingly careful in having their company, and it belongs only...

...to the true **Magi**, **Cabalists**, and **Theologians**, who are perfectly practiced in the **Language of Nature**, that is, in the *Signatures of Things* <note>original: "Signatura Rerum"; the belief that the physical appearance of objects reveals their inner spiritual essence or medicinal use.</note> and the *Characters of the Spirits* <note>original: "Characteribus Spirituum"; the symbolic scripts or seals associated with spiritual entities.</note>. He knows how to "play the fiddle" for a man according to how the man is inclined to "dance," until the man falls from one seduction into another and completely departs from his Creator. The villain knows how to keep this up until he entirely drives away the elemental spirit from the man, whose essence and form he knows how to masterfully imitate, until finally complete sorcery and total apostasy are birthed through *abjuration* <note>original: "per abjurationem"; a formal renunciation or turning away from faith.</note>. <margin>102.</margin> This is then the last scene in this opera; therefore, let everyone well test and examine himself before he involves himself in such conversation or takes any part in it, for the Devil is an altogether filthy beast, from whom one may not easily withdraw oneself unstained. §. 16. Now it is time to also consider the fourth element and its creatures. In the first chapter of this work, we have thoroughly and irrefutably proven that Fire, Water, and the Sun—that is, Salt, Sulfur, and Mercury <note>The text uses symbols: $\Delta$ (Fire), $\nabla$ (Water), $\odot$ (Sun), $\ominus$ (Salt), $\text{\jupiter}$ (Tin/Sulfur in this system), and $\text{\mercury}$ (Mercury). Welling argues these are essentially a single "watery light."</note>—are one and the same in their essential being: namely, a watery light, or a light-saturated water. Now, these creatures of which we now wish to speak <margin>103.</margin> are generated from this element just defined; therefore, they are also entirely pure, holy, wise, and powerful. They cannot be blinded or seduced in the least by the devils, who are spirits of darkness. They have a very great and special love for the creatures of the Element of Water, as the element which was first chosen out of their own. The mortal human body may not easily endure their frequent visitation; nevertheless, the Magus and Cabalist may receive much and great wisdom from them through the mediation of the Water-creatures, in whom these Light-creatures incessantly mirror themselves. Yet, to go without their wisdom is best.

§. 17. This is now what we have to say of these creatures or spirit-men, and what has been revealed to us. Whoever denies it speaks against the eternal truth, namely against God Himself. In Exodus 20, verses 4 and 5, it stands written: <margin>104.</margin>
> You shall not make for yourself any idol, nor any likeness of anything that is in heaven above, or that is on the earth beneath, or that is in the water under the earth; you shall not bow down to them nor serve them, etc.

Furthermore, it stands in Revelation 5, verse 13:
> And every creature which is in heaven, and on the earth, and under the earth, and in the sea, and all that are in them, etc. Every creature that is in heaven, etc.

Under this are included the creatures of Fire and Air <note>$\Delta$ and $\mathbb{A}$.</note>. We are well aware that the schools understand this verse quite differently, nor do we wish to take the trouble to quarrel with them over it; it is enough that we are in agreement with those who love the truth, for the honor and praise of the Almighty Creator.

§. 18. Now it also remains to speak a little of their figure, and how one might enjoy their conversation (which, however, is to be avoided in every way, because God the Lord will not tolerate such things). Concerning their figure, know that every rational creature bears the image of Adam (b. this statement will give many cause to cry out, but if we wished to explain ourselves fully here, everyone should be able to be satisfied; however, it is not our intention to explain ourselves perfectly and extensively on each and every point, but rather we wish to leave something for others to reflect upon), namely the *human form* <note>original: "Figuram humanam".</note>. And because they are the *Simplest Essence of the Elements* <note>original: "Simplicissima Essentia Elementorum".</note>, they are also—contrary to the foolish opinion of the Count of Gabalis <note>Referring to the 1670 book "Comte de Gabalis" by Henri de Montfaucon de Villars, which popularized the idea of elementals but was seen by serious occultists as satirical or misleading.</note>—immortal, after the manner of all spirits. <margin>105.</margin> Moreover, the opinion of many carnal-minded people is almost foolish and ridiculous, who attribute a *distinction of sex* <note>original: "distinctionem sexus".</note> to these semi-complete spirit-men. Rather, it is certain and true that, after the manner of all spirits, they were created in their perfect number all at once, except for those of whom we shall speak below. But that they mixed with humans in Genesis 6, verses 2 and 4—which God the Almighty punished so severely—is not against us, but for us, and has an entirely different meaning, as will be explained in the following sections from 20 to 23. For it does not say there that the daughters of God went in unto the sons of <margin>106.</margin> men, but the holy text says: **That the sons of God saw the daughters of men, that they were fair.** And further: **When the sons of God came in unto the daughters of men, etc.** There we see, indeed, that the text mentions only the sons, as the male gender alone; but below in the following paragraphs up to section 23, this matter is handled in detail, also concerning the *female sex* <note>original: "sexum fœmininum".</note>. Furthermore, we are certain that this union with the daughters of men was accomplished only by the spirit-men <margin>107.</margin> of the Element of Earth; for the others of Fire, Air, and Water <note>$\Delta, \mathbb{A}$, and $\nabla$.</note> cannot possibly mix with human nature. All of this would be necessary to carry out perfectly in its own extensive work, but it cannot be done here in our project, lest the work grow excessively large. He who loves the truth and is guided by God will understand us perfectly and extensively enough, especially since he has taken good heed of everything previously recounted, and how man was formed from the dust of an earth. # CHAPTER V.

<margin>108.</margin> Regarding communication with such spirits, it is not as easily achieved in our times and ages <note>original: "Seculis"</note> as foolish conjurers and sorcerers imagine. Setting all such devilries aside, know that anyone who wishes to undertake such a thing must, in addition to a true inward fear of God, also possess perfect knowledge of the true Magical worship. He must be most skilled in true Philosophy <note>original: "in vera Philosophia peritissimus"</note> so that he knows perfectly what the true and most simple Essence <note>original: "vera & simplicissima Essentia"</note> of each element is. This is necessary so that he may know what is contrary to each type of these spirit-people in their element and what is pleasing to them, so that he may suppress what is repulsive within and about himself and exalt <note>original: "exaltire"</note> what is pleasing. We experience and witness this daily even with wild animals: each has its own particular scent or attraction by which it may be incited, lured, and finally caught, whereas it might be entirely driven away by that which is contrary to it. He must be a Magical Cabalist <note>original: "Magus Cabalist"</note> and a true Theosopher <note>original: "verus Theosophus"</note>, so that the characterization of the spirits <note>original: "Characteristicatio Spirituum"; the specific symbolic marks or spiritual signatures that identify different entities</note> is fully known to him, <margin>109.</margin> lest the Devil deceive him. And when he possesses these qualities, he will also be a true Astrologer, which is perfectly required in this secret art. He must accustom himself to a holy solitude and know how to exalt his imagination to the sharpest degree, so that he may, as it were, encounter these spirit-people through the rays of his imagination <note>original: "per Radios Imaginationis suæ"</note> and thereby draw them to himself as if by a powerful magnet. For even true faith is nothing other <margin>110.</margin> than the pure rays of our imagination sunk into the Divine Light. For wherever our imagination is sharply directed, there it also works. In this mystery lies the ground and the basis of the holy art of Magic and Cabala <note>original: "Magiæ & Cabalæ"</note> within secret Theology, of which there is much and great things to be said here. It would also be necessary to clearly report what the Magus must use both internally and externally to have the communion <note>original: "Conversation"</note> of these creatures, and how he must use it; however, since no Philosopher or Theosopher has ever dealt with this clearly and according to the literal word, it is not permitted for us to do so either, even if we knew it. And this shall be spoken clearly and truthfully enough concerning the most simple creatures of each element in human form <note>original: "de Creaturis Simplicissimis uniuscujusque Elementi in Figura humana"</note>. <margin>111.</margin> Now, whoever reads this might perhaps believe of us that we were speaking here against ourselves as if in a dream, since we—according to the content of the text in Genesis Chapter VI, verses 1, 2, 3, and 4—posit the union <note>original: "Congressum"</note> of certain spiritual creatures with the daughters of men. To counter this, and to place ourselves beyond all suspicion of error, we wish to explain ourselves clearly on this matter.

->§. 19.<-

We have heard that on the third day of work (see Paragraph 110 above in this chapter) the material, coarse water was separated from the earth, and thus these two elements also became visibly distinct. Then the Almighty God in the 11th verse commanded that the earth should bring forth all vegetation, each having its own seed within it, which is reported to have been accomplished in the 12th verse. That the earth is not without water <note>The symbol $\nabla$ represents Water.</note>, and the water not without earth, we do not need to prove here again, but everyone will find this perfectly detailed in the <margin>112.</margin> first chapter. We only need to remind the reader here that in the earth—just as in the water which permeates the earth everywhere—sticks the Essential Form of all Seeds of all plants, animals, and minerals <note>original: "Forma Essentialis Seminum omnium vegetabilium Animalium & Mineralium"</note>, as an essential extract of all elements. We do not say this of the coarse, thick, dark earth, which is only a cover or external vessel, <margin>113.</margin> but in the pure spirit-corporeal Virgin Earth <note>original: "Terra virginea"; in alchemy, this refers to the primordial, uncorrupted matter from which all things grow.</note>. Furthermore, it is perfectly known to all true Philosophers that every species under heaven that multiplies in its kind must also have a body variously mixed or composed <note>original: "Corpus variè commixtum f. compositum"</note>. No most simple body <note>original: "Simplicissimum Corpus"</note> or pure and most simple essence <note>original: "pura & simplicissima Essentia"</note> of an element—such as the creatures of Fire <note>The symbol $\triangle$ represents Fire.</note>, Light <note>The symbol $\triangle$ (upward triangle) often represents Fire or Light in this context.</note>, and Water <note>The symbol $\nabla$ represents Water.</note> are—can multiply either in its own kind or in other species, for they are a purely spiritual body <note>original: "pure Spirituale Corpus"</note>, a quasi-angelic body <note>original: "quasi Angelicum Corpus"</note>. Therefore, one should reasonably not seek the union <note>original: "Congreß"</note> of those just mentioned—Fire, Light, and <margin>114.</margin> Water. For whatever is to be fit for generation must have a spermatic body <note>original: "Corpus Spermaticum"</note> that is subject to "Gas" or "Blast" <note>Paracelsian terms for the vital spirit or volatile force in nature.</note>; for it is also known well enough to all physicists that the animal sperm is nothing other than the boiling of the purest blood or of the second digestion <note>original: "ebullitio sanguinis purissimi f. secundæ Concoctionis"</note>. Therefore, we must necessarily look for another species that has all the aforementioned requirements that are fit for that generation and operation of which the text in Genesis VI speaks. We have to seek these nowhere else but in the Element of Earth. These stand in the middle between those just described and the Microcosm <note>Man</note>; they are of both sexes, and because they consist of the secondary quality, that is, the element of Earth <note><note>A diagram shows the alchemical symbol for Earth: a downward-pointing triangle enclosed within a square box.</note></note>, and as the following explains, they cannot be immortal like the other creatures already spoken of above. Briefly <margin>115.</margin> above we said that in the earth sticks the spermatic power of all things formally and actually <note>original: "vis spermatica Rerum omnium formaliter & actualiter"</note> (but in the water ideally and potentially <note>original: "Idealiter & potentialiter"</note>; therefore, although contained in their essence, idea, and figure, they still lack the formal matter and consequently the act). Specifically, this is in the spirit-corporeal Virgin Earth; therefore, it is to be concluded unshakably that these spirit-corporeal creatures of the element of Earth, as the extract of the spirit-corporeal Virgin Earth, must be so qualified. For the Holy Scripture must be and remain eternally true against the Devil and all his raging host and all their followers. The Scripture says in Genesis VI, verse 4: **In those same days there were giants on earth, and also afterward, when the sons of God went in unto the daughters of men, and they bore children to them: the same are the mighty ones who were famous men of old,** etc. Now, every reasonable person will easily understand from what is mentioned above that these were no angels. <margin>116.</margin> Also, no creature can eternally prove that ever an angel, or the angels...

# Of the Fall of Lucifer, etc.

the name <term>Sons of God</term> <note>original Hebrew: "Bne ha-Elohim"</note> or <term>Sons of God</term> has been given in Holy Scripture <note>original: "in Sacris"</note>; therefore these Sons of God—of whom Moses speaks here so clearly and distinctly, stating that they performed intercourse <note>original Latin: "concubitum"</note> with the daughters of men—must truly have been of another species. The Holy Text does not say <margin>117.</margin> the <term>Daughters of God</term> <note>original Hebrew: "Benoth ha-Elohim"</note> entered in; thus the Scripture mentions only one gender, namely the masculine, and for particular reasons does not mention a similar act of the feminine gender at all. Now, if the Holy Scripture in such <margin>118.</margin> an important matter mentions only one gender, we must also hold fast to this and believe unshakeably that the many histories after the times of the Deluge <note>The Great Flood of Noah.</note>—concerning both some famous lineages in particular, as well as great powerful nations in general—are to be interpreted in no other way than from these principles. And although they may be described differently by rational people to avoid scandal <note>original Latin: "propter scandalum"</note>, it nevertheless remains according to these conclusions. Very many such histories could be cited, <margin>119.</margin> but it would only give further occasion to quarrelsome people and fools to vent their raging spirits against these writings of ours. Moreover, it is sufficiently known to everyone—even those <margin>120.</margin> who have only peeked into Philosophy with one eye—that a spiritual body <note>original Latin: "Corpus Spirituale"</note> can easily and perfectly operate within a material body <note>original Latin: "Corpus materiale"</note>, but a coarse material body cannot act so well, or in the same way, upon a spiritual body. What has been claimed here, however, are not necessary articles <margin>121.</margin> to be preached to the female sex without distinction, for they might otherwise draw excellent principles or consequences for their physics from this; therefore it is said only to wise and rational men.

§. 20. Now we must further prove what we already touched upon above in §. 18: namely, that these elementary spiritual-corporeal creatures of <note>A series of four alchemical symbols represents the elements: a triangle pointing up (Fire), a triangle pointing up with a horizontal bar (Air), a triangle pointing down (Water), and a triangle pointing down with a horizontal bar (Earth).</note> Fire, Air, Water, and Earth, and of the purest Earth, are also immortal and imperishable. That all humans are indeed mortal, yet also eternal and imperishable, is something all true believers are thoroughly convinced of through the Word of Life. Now Moses says in Genesis 6 that they fathered with the daughters of men the <term>Nephilim</term> <note>original Hebrew: "Nephilim"</note>, that is, the powerful ones or giants, who also existed thereafter, as in the times of Joshua and David. Now, who doubts that these were true humans? For the Holy Scripture also calls them such, namely giants, or great and powerful men, as in the books of Joshua, Samuel, etc. Now we have proven that these spiritual-corporeal creatures are of the most simple essence of the elements <note>original Latin: "Simplicissimæ Essentiæ Elementorum"</note> and thus of an entirely spiritual nature, in the likeness of angels; they can therefore not be subject to any death or passing away, for everything that is mortal must have a body subject to the change of heat and cold—as opposites—by which putrefaction or decay is generated. No simple spiritual body is subject to such passion; now these elemental beings are such simple substances, and therefore also immortal. Secondly, they also fathered great and mighty giants, namely humans, who were indeed mortal according to their elemental body, but according to their essential body, soul, and spirit, were immortal and remain eternal.

§. 21. Furthermore, we must remind ourselves that the name "Human" is given absolutely to no creature, whether in heaven or on earth, or in the water and under the earth, except to the unique Adam and his seed; also that no creature was created so perfectly as this one, which consists of three <note>original Latin: "ex tribus"</note>, namely Body, Soul, and Spirit, according to the three witnesses in heaven—the Father, the Word, and the Holy Spirit—and the three witnesses on earth—the Spirit, the Water, and the Blood (1 John 5:7-8). Of the angels it is said in Hebrews 1:7: *Who makes his angels spirits, and his ministers a flame of fire.* <margin>Note.</margin> Thus the angels are the most simple spirits from the divine light <note>original Latin: "simplicissimi Spiritus à lumine divino"</note>, and so also are these elemental creatures, like the angels, the most simple spirits from the four elements <note>original Latin: "simplicissimi Spiritus ex 4. Elementis"</note>. These creatures, as Moses clearly and distinctly says, fathered humans with the daughters of men—an individual existing from body, soul, and spirit <note>original Latin: "Individuum ex Corpore anima & Spiritu existens"</note>. From this, the fathers had only an essential, spiritual, elemental body; the fruit, however, had a material body, a soul (that is, an ethereal or elemental spirit held in common with the fathers), and a spirit (that is, the immediate breathing out or breathing in of God), which is a prerogative that the human alone possesses above all other creatures. Now, you gentlemen, likewise deeply learned physicists: what counsel is there here? And how is one to help oneself out of this darkness? And how is it to be proven, according to common teaching, that the immortal spirit of the human is propagated by transmission <note>original Latin: "per traducem"; a theological theory that the soul is generated by the parents along with the body.</note>? It seems to us that what has just been said proves enough: that the immortal spirit of the human is not handed over to the woman by transmission in the sinful will of the man, but comes into the fruit from the immediate breathing forth of the almighty God <note>original Latin: "ex immediato efflatu omnipotentis Dei"</note> when the woman feels the first life in her womb, as will be seen in what follows.

§. 22. Finally, we must also speak of the different types and properties of these spiritual-corporeal creatures of the element of Earth. We say spiritual-corporeal creatures: for although they have a human form <note>original Latin: "figuram humanam"</note>, they are nevertheless not of essential human substance, and the name "Human" belongs purely and alone to the image of the living God in Christ, as the one heavenly human. Thus, there are those specifically among the other elemental creatures from the element <note>An alchemical symbol consisting of a triangle pointing down with a horizontal bar represents the element of Earth.</note> of Earth, of various types and properties, according to the nature and properties of the earthly regions and the virginal spermatic earth <note>original Latin: "Terræ spermaticæ virgineæ"; a term for the pure, uncorrupted primordial matter of the earth.</note> from which they were born and fathered. For as we have thoroughly proven above, the likeness of every being created under heaven is hidden in this virgin earth, as are the influences of the spirits of the planets; thus they are small and large, weak and powerful, wise and foolish, according to the essential spermatic force of that earth.

The coarser and most evil kind of them are those / whom one commonly calls Kobolds <note>A type of subterranean spirit or goblin in German folklore, often seen as troublesome house-spirits.</note> / they are for the most part full of diabolical malice / for just as the evil spirit knows how to make masterly use of them / and exercises much of his cunning and malice through them / yet there are many among the blind common folk / who use such Kobolds for their own gain and their neighbor's harm / but they are not much better / than if they were using a true little devil. Thus those err / who take the same for the aforementioned pure substance of <term>🜂</term> <note>Fire</note>. Now man alone is the image of the living GOD / therefore the Holy GOD could not tolerate this disorderly and destructive mixture / for He is a GOD of order. Furthermore / from this disorderly mixture were procreated powerful ones / who exercised their power and tyranny; famous people / who gave occasion for the falling away from GOD / and after their death for idolatry / in the worship of their statues and images / the devil also mixed himself in / and through his cunning and malice plunged men into cruel ruin / and taught them all their malice. In summary, the devil thereby instigated such confusion and malice / that he finally would have obtained the general downfall of the human race / if GOD the LORD through the Flood had not [destroyed] all flesh under the heaven / and with them the knowledge / of this malice introduced against God's will,

§. 24. We have previously spoken at some length regarding the invisible creatures of the third day’s work; therefore, it is time that we also turn to the visible ones. The text in Genesis 1:11 and 12 describes them thus: **"Then God said: Let the earth bring forth grass, herbs that bear seed, and fruitful trees that yield fruit according to their kind, in which their seed is upon the earth; and it was so."** And further in the 12th verse, etc. Here again opens a particularly wonderful theater of the works of God, as was specifically asserted through the 16th paragraph of the first chapter, where the properties of the Virginal Earth (and how it contains all the seeds or powers of Heaven within itself) were discussed. In this day's work, we now see this effect; for when one considers the quite indescribable number of all the vegetables <note>In this context, "vegetables" refers to all plant life (vegetabilia).</note> of this day’s work—of which an innumerable multitude grow forth from a piece of earth of quite small length and breadth, and all together have only one kind of sap for their nourishment—one must surely conclude that what was said in the aforementioned paragraph is true: namely, that these seeds previously had their spiritual birth (which was proven in detail in the previous chapter and is unnecessary to repeat). This spiritual birth was sunk from above into the water-making <note>original symbol: ▽ (Water)</note> sand and stone, from where these seeds were further driven into this coarser earth in a somewhat more fixed form by the subterranean fire. There, they were softened in the moist soil by the powers of the created light, i.e., *Schamajim* <note>Hebrew: "Heavens." Welling uses this to represent the celestial/ethereal influence or the "fire-water" of the sky.</note>, and brought into a gas or ferment, driven upward by the subterranean fire and nourished by the upper [fire], until each seed—according to its quality specified by the stars—brought forth a herb, shrub, or tree in its own species. Now, when every plant has accomplished through the change of seasons that for which it was created—so that it has risen, turned green, bloomed, and brought forth fruit to the praise of GOD and for the use and delight of man in this troublesome life—in which fruits or flowers their own seed is contained (carried into them by the viscous nutritional sap) for their further propagation, it then rests again for its time, so that it may further become fit for the same effect once more. By this, however, no one should imagine that henceforth the earth would not be able to bring forth something of plants out of itself without sown seed; for we see daily that in places which have not been cultivated for many years, it brings forth entire forests and the like, which are signs that Heaven still showers us daily with its powers.

§. 25. The earth had brought forth all these terrestrial plants together when it was not yet cursed because of the Fall of Man (a. the following will speak of this in detail); therefore, at that time, even the very least little herb was many thousand times more healing and true than the *Aurum Potabile* <note>original: ☉ Potabile, Latin for "Drinkable Gold," a legendary alchemical medicine thought to cure all diseases and grant longevity.</note> now decried throughout the world but seen by no one. They remained in this perfection until the Fall of Man and the curse resulting therefrom. And although from that time until the Deluge and the banishment of all flesh under heaven, a ray of the first Divine blessing still remained in them all—and thus, as a whole and each one in particular, they were still incomparably more powerful, true, and healing than the above-mentioned philosophical wonder, the *Aurum Potabile*—it yet appears clear as sunlight that at the time of the banishment, such as the Flood, this ray was so enclosed and surrounded with a coarser and thicker husk that the second world, built by Noah, could no longer live solely from the plants (b. this speech is to be very well considered, for something more lies behind it than many clever thoughts might imagine) because of the many coarse impurities now dwelling [within], and because their lovely Salt <note>original symbol: ⊖</note>, Gold <note>original symbol: ☉</note>, or the powers of the *Schamajim*—i.e., the often-mentioned red and white Fire <note>original symbol: △</note> of Nature (from which the highest poured-out life and nutritional sap is hardened and turned into a coarse Salt <note>original symbol: ⊖</note> called *Alkali Salt*) [had changed]. Opportunity will arise to speak further of this later; and we proceed now to the fourth day’s work.

## The Fourth Day's Work.

§. 26. In this were accomplished the wondrous births of all the heavenly lights from the light first created, together with their indwelling creatures. Light and darkness were the beginning of creation, as the Holy Text says in few words, and of which we have dealt extensively everywhere in the preceding. These two—light and darkness—are each divided again: the light into fire and air, and the darkness into water and earth, as has been sufficiently proven before. The text says in Genesis 1:14, 15, 16: **"Then God said: Let there be lights in the expansion of the heavens to make a distinction be-"**

### CHAPTER V.

...divide the day and the night: and let them be for signs and appointed times, and for days and years. Let them also be for lights in the firmament of heaven, to shine upon the earth, and it was so.

For **GOD** made the two great lights: the greater light to rule the <margin>(b.)</margin> day, and the smaller light (b.) to rule the night, and likewise the stars. The first day's work was the separation of light from darkness, and these created entities distinguished the day and the night until the fourth day, at which point **Sun, Moon**, and all the stars were separated from that light. The remaining **Heavens** <note>original: "Schamajim," the Hebrew word for the heavens or celestial waters.</note> were placed into the **Sphere** <note>original: "Sphæram"</note> above the stars; for by now all the **circles** <note>The "circles" refer to the orbits or planetary spheres of the Ptolemaic and alchemical cosmos.</note> of the stars were created. Each star had received its own circle, and each **sphere** was specified according to its **Body** <note>original: "Corpore"</note>, which was to have its air or motion within it. From this **sphere**, the **body** of the star is no longer able to depart or enter into the **sphere** of another star. For just as the **bodies** of the stars are entirely distinct, so too are they distinct according to their **circles**, because each **circle** or **sphere** has been specified according to the **body** that must complete its course within it. These **circles** can no more mix with one another than can plain water and oil; thus, one **circle** is pressed or encompassed by another. However, all <margin>7.</margin> these **circles** are encompassed by the **Heavens** <note>original: "Schamajim"</note>, and these in turn by the Angelic Circles and Choirs; but the final encompassment is the almighty will of the living **GOD**. Although this could be clearly demonstrated through various analogies—such as an egg <note>The "Orphic Egg" or "Cosmic Egg" was a common analogy where the yolk represented the earth, the white the atmosphere, and the shell the firmament.</note> or different **liquids** <note>original: "Liquores"</note>—we have omitted such things to avoid being overly long-winded, believing that anyone whose brain is not deranged will have understood us sufficiently without these analogies.

§. 27. Until now, the first-created light had not only distinguished the first three days and nights, but also—through its powerful operation and irradiation—had not only brought forth all **Vegetation** <note>original: "Vegetabilia"</note> under the heavens, but also thoroughly impregnated the entire **Earthly Circle** and **Globe** with all the primordial seeds of the **Ideal World**, including all **plants**, **animals**, and **minerals**. However, so that the ground might bring forth all **vegetation** <margin>Gen. II. v. 6.</margin> by Divine command, the Almighty drew up a **salt-sulfurous**, lovely vapor from the earth, which was fit to receive that which comes from the **Heavens** <note>original: "Schamajim"</note>. We have spoken of this so extensively previously, but here, for certain reasons, we shall call it the universal **Salt-Essence** <note>original: "Sal Wesen"</note>. Now, as this vapor became watery, as it were, through the darkness (that is, through the cool night), and was pressed down onto the earth by the following third day or light, the earth—everywhere through the cooperation of the subterranean fire—brought forth on this third day all species of **vegetation** in innumerable quantities, thus fulfilling the will of the living **GOD**. For the text says in Genesis 2:5: For **GOD** the **LORD** had not yet caused it to **rain** upon the earth, and there was no man to till the ground, etc. For rain is an effect of the great heavenly lights, which were first created on this fourth day; and verses 4, 5, and 6 of Genesis 2 are to be considered with all diligence.

§. 28. The lover of Divine mysteries will not take it amiss that we digress somewhat in this matter to consider for a moment what wonderful powers these first earthly **plants** <note>original: "Vegetabilia"</note> must have possessed—even though they were not to be compared in the least with the fruits of the **Garden of Eden**. For they were brought forth in their first power, in full Divine blessing, and without any curse. Truly, one must conclude that a dish of turnips at that time had more strength and effect than whole buckets full of that strangely contrived **Drinkable Gold** <note>original: "Auri Potabilis," a legendary alchemical life-extender.</note>. These last words will surely rouse many practiced tongues against us, but that shall not trouble us in the least. For firstly, it is said that every man is the interpreter of his own words; secondly, we are certain that if we wished to explain ourselves clearly on this, every rational person would agree with our meaning. And although we do not intend to explain ourselves entirely, yet for the love of truth, we will say only this little: Namely, that we do not believe in any such **Drinkable Gold** <note>The symbol $\odot$ represents the Sun or Gold.</note> as is taught and described by most so-called **Philosophers** and their following of **dabblers** <note>original: "Sudelköche," literally "greasy cooks" or "slop-cooks," a term of derision for amateur or fraudulent alchemists.</note>, which until this hour has been seen by no one other than their fellow guild-members (that is, **liars** and **frauds**). However, we do believe with the true Sages in the clear little drink of water prepared from the living **Sun** <note>symbol: $\odot$</note> and **Moon** <note>symbol: $\mathbb{C}$</note>—that is, from the red and white **fire** <note>symbol: $\triangle$</note> of nature, the **Solar** and **Lunar** seeds—dissolved in the great unknown waters of the general **World-Ocean**. This we believe to be the true **Drinkable Gold** or **Universal Medicine**. But from where and how it is to be obtained unadulterated is a question we shall leave unanswered for now, as it has already been spoken of very clearly in a certain place above. However, some impetuous people might reproach us and say: "If this drink is so well known to you (as you imply by your last words), how does it happen that it is never found with you, since you are in need of it as much as other men?" This reproach from such dull simpletons would not be entirely unreasonable, and so we must answer it a little: Firstly, we did not say that the way or the preparation to obtain this drink was perfectly known to us (and even if it were said, it would truly be no lie); that we do not have such **Drinkable Gold of the Wise** <note>original: "Aurum Potabile Sophorum"</note> ourselves has higher causes, and therefore it is not necessary to lay such reasons before the nose of every quarrelsome fool. Whoever [reads] these writings from the beginning until here...

# On the Fall of Lucifer, etc.

...whoever has read well up to this point, and possesses an unwavering mind, will be perfectly satisfied in all things. For it is said: *to know is one thing, to be able is another* <note>original Latin: "aliud scire, aliud posse"</note>, or perhaps, knowing a thing is one thing, and possessing it is another; let us therefore return to the works of the fourth day.

§. 29. Sun, Moon, and all stars were created on this day out of the light, that is, out of the *Heavens* <term>Schamajim: The Hebrew word for the heavens, which Welling and other alchemists interpreted as a combination of 'Esh' (fire) and 'Mayim' (water).</term>, as the text clearly indicates with the words: *Let there be lights in the expanse of the heavens,* etc. Now, because the will of **GOD** created all these heavenly bodies ideally within this light or *Schamajim*, the <term>יהי</term> <note>Hebrew: "Yehi," meaning "Let there be."</note> or *Fiat* <note>Latin: "Let it be done."</note> brought them forth formally and visibly, so that every celestial body would have its own specific physical form, as much in light and density as in all other qualities (to understand this well, one must have kept the first chapter of this work well in mind). For just as the Sun is an extract of *Fire* <term>אש (Esh): fire</term> out of the *Heavens*, so the Moon is an extract of the *Waters* <term>מים (Mayim): water</term> out of the *Heavens*, and so it is arranged with all the other stars. Namely, that each has its greater or lesser inclination, or whatever their mixture of *Fire* or *Water* may be—about which one can more easily form a concept or idea in the intellect than speak of definitively—enough will have been said here to a rational person. Just as the Sun, Moon, and all other celestial bodies are specified in their essence, so too are their radiations or beamings toward this earthly globe equal and uniform; see Chapter I, studied with all diligence, where everything is explained in detail and need not be repeated here.

We return then to the rest that remains to be said concerning these creatures, which will surely stir up a whole swarm of foolish jesters against us. Namely, whether these bodies, collectively and individually according to common delusion, are merely empty and hollow lumps that were set on high simply for nightly decoration? Almost all those are filled with this delusion who are bound to judge **GOD** the Almighty and His creatures according to certain dogmatic principles; these are also the ones who will most vehemently attack us. That the Sun, Moon, and countless stars are specifically ruled by creatures who are of the simplest essence or spirits born of such bodies can be proven neither through tradition nor by those words of Holy Scripture (which would satisfy any adversary); nevertheless, through the contemplation of the omnipotence and glory of the living **GOD**, and several passages of Holy Scripture, it is proven so clearly and distinctly that a rational and true lover of wisdom must be perfectly satisfied. Above in §. 12 and further on, we spoke extensively of the creatures of the so-called four elements, and sufficiently proved that these four great essences—Fire, Air, Water, and Earth—are filled with certain spirit-beings or elementary creatures; how much less then would the Almighty **GOD** have left all these innumerable bodies (of which there are an indescribable multitude of unbelievable spirits, more than on this earthly globe) void of creatures who praise His great majesty and glory? The 148th Psalm says in verses 1, 2, 3, and 4:

> Praise the **LORD** from the heavens; praise him in the heights.
> Praise him, all his angels: praise him, all his hosts.
> Praise him, Sun and Moon: praise him, all you shining stars.
> Praise him, you highest heavens, and you waters that are above the heavens.

In the 147th Psalm, verse 4, it is said: *He counts the number of the stars; He calls them all by their names.* In Isaiah, Chapter 40, verse 26, it stands: *Lift up your eyes on high, and see who has created these things, who brings out their host by number: who calls them all by name, by the greatness of His might and because He is strong in power, not one of them is missing.* Why these passages of Holy Scripture are placed here, everyone will easily see; likewise, everyone knows how they are commonly accustomed to be interpreted, in which, in our opinion, there is a great error. The 3rd verse of the 148th Psalm says: *Praise him, Sun and Moon: Praise him, all you shining stars.* Who then is to praise the Most High here? According to the common delusion, the lifeless bodies of the Sun, Moon, and stars? Through their orderly, certain course and position? Oh, foolish thoughts regarding the majesty and glory of the living **GOD**! Rather, by these are understood the indwelling spiritual creatures and inhabitants of the Sun, Moon, and all the stars; it is they who laud and praise the name of the **LORD**. And according to this system, we consider every star, as well as the Sun and Moon, to be a world, insofar as our earthly globe with its airy sphere up to the sphere of the Moon <note>indicated by the symbol ☽</note> may be called a world; and the inhabitants of these worlds are qualified according to the essential property of that same world or globe upon which they were created. Each of these worlds and its inhabitants also has its ruler and king, who are mighty spirits, yet are subject to those high spirits in the Angelic Circles or regents of the spirit-world <margin>(c.)</margin> (see Figure No. 53), which are further subject to the seven great spirits before the throne, and receive their influence from them, which these seven great spirits receive for us mediately from **GOD**, as will be explained in the recently mentioned diagram. Just as the properties of these mighty spirits and their dominions in these star-worlds are natured, exactly so are their effects inclined toward our earthly globe, which is the treasury and collection-box of all nature. For example: when the Sphere of the Sun <note>indicated by the symbol ☉</note> beams its rays and powers entirely alone, without any hindrance from any other celestial body, against our earthly globe, then we have nothing but hot, dry times, hot pestilential diseases, war, murder, robbery, fire, and bloodshed. Which effects become much more powerful when another Martian star <note>indicated by the symbol ♂</note> from the higher circles (from the fixed stars) sends its...

# CHAPTER V.

...rays are united; and the same is to be judged of all the others. For all these star-worlds are qualified in certain orders according to the planetary worlds—or to speak more truly, the star-worlds of the firmament receive their powers from the twelve choirs of the **Angelic World**. From there, these powers are sent to the planetary worlds (as a narrower number) and then come further down to us. However, since all of this was handled in detail in the first, second, and following chapters, no further repetition is necessary here.

§. 30. Now, there would still be much to say concerning true <term>Astrology</term> <note>Welling distinguishes between common fortune-telling and a "true" astrology based on the divine signatures of the stars.</note> according to this <term>system</term> and all other properties belonging to it. However, because this requires a special <term>work</term> <note>original Latin: "Opus"</note>, nothing more shall be said of it here, except that whoever wishes to follow these <term>principles</term> and what is further discussed regarding **Diagram No. 5**, will be capable of supernatural things in these sciences. With this, we then conclude this day's work.

# The Fifth Day's Work.

§. 31. The creatures of this fifth day's work were everything that lives in the seas, lakes, rivers, and other waters, as well as in the air; as can be clearly seen in <term>Genesis 1:20–23</term>. The fluidic substances, such as all <term>vegetation</term> <note>original Latin: "Vegetabilia"</note>, were the first to be separated or brought forth; after these came everything of a <term>Salinic</term> <note>Saline or salt-based substance, which Welling associates with the element of water and the life within it.</note> substance, namely all that lives in the seas and all other waters, as well as in the air. The **Sulphuric** substances, however, such as all the animals of the earth, only appear on the sixth and final day.

All these creatures—fish and birds—were, like the **vegetation**, before the Fall of Man and the curse that followed it, magnificent, pure, and perfect in their substance. They were healthy and neither predatory nor inclined to cause harm. But after the Fall and the curse, everything within them was inverted; their first pure substance was wrapped and locked away in a coarser essence, so that little of that vital sap necessary for the maintenance of human life can be obtained from them. Here we should perhaps speak a little further on the various properties of both fish and birds, those living in fresh as well as salt water, and consequently of all birds. However, everything previously discussed will satisfy everyone. We only wish to say this: nothing seems more strange to us than that rational people make so much quarreling and talk regarding the winter quarters or whereabouts of certain birds, such as swallows and storks, etc. Yet their origin and creation is crystal clear in **Genesis 1:20**. Thus, it is truly and irrefutably concluded that throughout the winter of their absence, they must have their stay in deep lakes, warm marshes, and similar places, where they remain. And so we also close this fifth day's work. <note>Welling here defends the common pre-modern belief that birds like swallows did not migrate to Africa but hibernated in the mud at the bottom of frozen lakes.</note>

# The Sixth and Final Day's Work.

§. 32. Now we come to the conclusion of Creation in the Sixth Day's Work, in which the mysteries of **GOD** for this present world were completed—which are a perfect prefiguration of the world to come. The text says in <term>Genesis 1:24 & 25</term> that the Lord **GOD** on this day created from the earth every creature of the earth; namely those that can find their stay neither in the air nor in the waters. The text names them differently and divides them into three <term>main species</term>: namely Cattle, Creeping Things, and Beasts of the Earth. For the Eternal Word or <term>יהוה</term> <note>The Tetragrammaton: Yahweh or Jehovah.</note> spoke: *Totza ha-aretz* <note>Hebrew: "Let the earth bring forth."</note>, that is, let the earth bring forth, let go out from itself *Nephesh Chayah* <note>Hebrew: "Living souls" or living creatures.</note> *L'minah* <note>Hebrew: "After their kinds."</note>: *Behemah* <note>Hebrew: "Cattle."</note>, namely great and small, for the holy use and benefit of man after his fall; *v'Remes* <note>Hebrew: "And creeping things."</note>, everything that crawls in and on the earth; and *v'Chayto-aretz* <note>Hebrew: "And beasts of the earth."</note>, namely all kinds of large and small, wild and predatory animals (as they became after the Fall). The text says: *Totza ha-aretz*, "They go out from the earth," or "Let the earth let them go out from itself." By this is indicated their substance and essence. Just as in the **vegetation** before the Fall, curse, and corruption, the <term>Mercurial Essence</term> <note>original: "Ens ☿ ale." The symbol ☿ represents Mercury.</note> predominated, and in the fish the <term>Saline Essence</term> <note>original: "Ens Salinum."</note> predominated, so the most prominent substance in all cattle, creeping things, and beasts was the <term>Jovial</term> <note>original: "♃ liche." The symbol ♃ represents the planet Jupiter, associated in alchemy with growth and life-force.</note> essence. These aforementioned earthly creatures came forth last according to the wisdom of God, so that they might immediately find their nourishment and residence, of which is further spoken in the 30th verse of this chapter, where it is written: *But to every beast of the earth, and to every bird of the heavens, and to everything that creeps on the earth, in which there is a living soul, I have given every green herb for food,* etc. Here it is irrefutably proven what was mentioned briefly above: that in this <term>Stage of Creation</term> <note>original Latin: "Termino Creationis"</note> there was no harmful, predatory, or poisonous creature, for they had nothing but all kinds of green herbs for their nourishment. They had not yet tasted blood; the earth was not yet cursed for man's sake, and thus everything stood in perfect Divine blessing and love. For adversity, wrath, and harshness—or contraction—were not yet manifest, and thus no corruption was to be perceived.

§. 33. In the preceding, the creation of this our visible world is recounted. See Figure X.B.

# Of the Fall of Lucifer, etc.

Diagram No. 14, as well as the Holy Text, should be compared with what preceded. We have stated how this visible world—light and darkness, furthermore all celestial lights or stars, all <term>vegetation</term> <note>original Latin: "Vegetabilia"</note>, all kinds of fish and birds, and finally all cattle, creeping things, and beasts of the earth—were first created. There was also, says the text in Genesis 2:5, no man who <term>tilled the earth</term> <note>The author interprets "tilling the earth" not just as farming, but as the presence of a rational soul to govern and appreciate the physical realm.</note>; that is to say, no rational creature was yet there who might have been a lord of all these magnificent creations, and who, in contemplation of them, would have praised the majesty of the Creator. Therefore, the Lord GOD spoke in Genesis 1:26: "Let us make man," etc.

Here we come upon those mysteries of which a great multitude of people often speak much, but which are everywhere rarely understood, because such <term>mystical matters</term> <note>original Latin: "Mystica"</note> are usually explained according to people's own prejudices—that is, by their established dogmas. Hence, they are so poorly interpreted, and no one understands the other. However, we shall leave them to their opinions generated by the <term>glossators</term> <note>Commentators who write "glosses" or marginal explanations on a text.</note>, on the condition that they likewise leave us unburdened in our principles (the foundation of which is the Holy Text). In this creation of Man, the Lord GOD maintained the very same <term>process</term> as He had held in the creation of this visible world: namely, the Lord GOD did not create the living creatures until, through His will, their dwelling and nourishment had been brought forth. Thus, on this sixth day, according to Genesis 2:8, the Lord GOD had planted a garden in Eden toward the East, and therein He set the man whom He had made.

Verse 9: And the Lord God had made all kinds of trees to grow out of that same earth, pleasant to behold and good for food: also the tree of life in the midst of the garden, and likewise the tree of knowledge of good and evil.

v. 10: And a river went out of Eden to water the garden, and from there it divided itself so that it became four main rivers.

v. 11: The name of the first is Pishon: that is the one which flows around the whole land of Havilah, where there is gold.

v. 12: And indeed the gold of that land is precious; there is also bdellium and the onyx stone.

v. 13: The name of the second river is Gihon: that is the one which flows around the whole land of Cush.

v. 14: The name of the third river is Hiddekel <note>Commonly identified as the Tigris.</note>: that is the one which runs toward the east of Assyria. But the fourth river is the Phrat <note>The Euphrates.</note>.

v. 15: Thus the Lord God took the man and put him into the garden of Eden, to till it and to keep it.

In these words of the text, wonder upon wonder is found. Firstly, it stands in the 8th verse that GOD made the Garden of Eden and placed the man therein, who was of male and female gender <note>Welling refers here to the <term>Garden of Eden</term> using the Hebrew: גַן-עֵדֶן. His mention of "male and female" suggests the Cabalistic view of the original Adam as an androgynous spiritual being.</note>. Secondly, that out of the earth of this same garden grew all kinds of pleasant trees. Thirdly, that a river went out of this garden, which afterwards divided into four main rivers, each of which flowed through and around a certain region. Fourthly, that the Lord God placed the man in this garden to till it and to keep it. Fifthly, that the Lord God gave a command to the man not to eat from a certain tree of this garden (called in Gen. 2:9 the tree of the knowledge of good and evil) under penalty of temporal and eternal death; but of all other growths of this garden, he might eat quite freely and unhindered.

Up to this point, we will pause with the text and first go through this: that all of this which the text says here happened on the sixth day is as clear as the sun from Gen. 1:26 and Gen. 2:8. We will therefore make a beginning with the Garden of Eden (<note>Hebrew: גַן-עֵדֶן, Gan-Eden</note>) toward the East (<note>Hebrew: מִקֶּדֶם, mi-qedem</note>). Concerning this garden, both Jews and Christians create many fables and strange, inconsistent things. To set all those opinions down here would be unnecessary and tedious work for us, as none of them serve our purpose. Nor do we wish, according to the <term>Cabala</term>, to "anatomize" the words <term>Gan</term> or <term>Qedem</term>, because it would lead us far too away from our intention; we wish simply to remain with the understanding of the Scripture.

The text says: The Lord God planted the garden in Eden toward the East. Now, it is abundantly known to all who are acquainted with the original language <note>Hebrew.</note> that **Eden** signifies a place of all perfect delight. But where was this place? The text says <note>original Hebrew: מִקֶּדֶם</note> "toward the East." Where was this East? According to the rising of the sun? Or, as the Rabbis would have it, in the direction toward which Adam's face was created? Or in the land of Canaan? Or in some other lovely region elsewhere? We answer that neither the one nor the other is correct. For in Gen. 2:10 it is written: a river went out of Eden and divided into four main rivers. Where then are this main source and the main rivers that sprang from it hidden? From the beginning, this has caused no small trouble to the majority of men, especially those whose wisdom rests upon certain man-made dogmas. One river must soon be the Ganges, the other the Nile, and so forth. Especially because Holy Scripture mentions certain lands which these main rivers are supposed to flow around and water. Here it truly applies: "make it rhyme or let it be." One flows toward the South and originates in the North; the other originates in the South and flows toward the North, etc. Is that not a strange source from which these forced "main rivers" are supposed to flow or originate?

### CHAPTER V.

...it makes us wonder, seeing as they have taken such pains regarding so many famous rivers, why they have forgotten the *Rio della Plata* in America? Not to mention other famous rivers, such as our European **Danube** and **Rhine**, which carry such magnificent fruits as gold and wine (for which there are nonetheless more lovers than for Divine Truth) almost in overabundance. If one further asks how it comes to pass that today the sources of these principal rivers are <term>diametrically</term> <note>original Latin: "è Diamet."</note> opposed to one another and so terribly far apart, the answer given is that the Deluge caused this, along with other such miserable bumbling, which is more worthy of laughter than of being taken seriously. However, in order not to delay ourselves any longer with so many confused opinions, we wish to return to the Text as the eternal truth.

§. 34. And so we say that the Lord GOD first created for Man, as His image (following the example of the creation of this earthly world), his dwelling and nourishment; the dwelling was the **Garden of Eden toward the East**, and his nourishment grew from the very earth of **Eden**. Now, whoever does not wish to be a <term>sophist</term> <note>A person who uses clever but false arguments.</note>, or whose eyes are not darkened by school-dust, will see more clearly than the sun that Genesis 2:9 speaks of an entirely different earth than Genesis 1:11. Furthermore, the fruits of this earth of **Eden**—including the Tree of Life and the Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil—show that the Region or Garden of **Eden** had nothing to do with this animalistic world. We place this Region, in Diagram No. 54 at W. X., as being in the true East above the starry heaven; these are the upper waters <term>Fire-Water</term> <note>original Hebrew: "Æsch Majim" or "Schamajim." Welling uses this to describe the spiritual substance of the heavens, a compound of fire and water.</note>, the curtain or covering before the Throne of the Glory of GOD and the Angelic World, so that the Holy Fire of His righteousness might not consume this earthly world (now lying under the curse) until the day of His appointed Holy Judgment, when this covering or curtain shall be drawn away, so that all the world may see the Glory of GOD for the universal judgment. This is the Region of all delight, of rest, and of peace (also called Paradise) and therefore called **Eden** in the original text, which according to the true <term>Cabal</term> <note>The Kabbalah; Jewish mystical tradition.</note> may also be called the Garden or the Region of Wisdom. Just as the earth of this garden was, so also were its fruits, namely fruits of Life and Wisdom. From this earth was the man **Adam** (Genesis 2:7, the wonder and image of GOD) created; that now this earth called there <term>Adamah</term> <note>original Hebrew: אֲדָמָה (red earth/soil)</note>, from which the <term>Adam</term> <note>original Hebrew: אָדָם</note> was created, is not the same as the earth <term>Ha-Aretz</term> <note>original Hebrew: הָאָרֶץ (the physical earth/land)</note> in Genesis 1:1 and 1:11, is clearly to be seen from the text and from what has been treated above. Now the text says that the Lord GOD created **Adam** as <term>dust from the earth</term> <note>original Hebrew: עָפָר מִן־הָאֲדָמָה (Aphar min ha-Adamah)</note>; we have spoken clearly enough of this dust of the earth from **Eden** in Chapter I and elsewhere. Just as this earth (which was from the Heavens) was constituted, so also was the body of Adam magnificent, <term>diaphanous</term> <note>Translucent or light-permeable.</note>, and shining, because it was made from the spiritual **Sun** and **Moon**, or the red and white **Sulphur** of the fiery **Heavens** <note>original Latin/symbols: ☉ and ☽, or red and white 🜍 of the fiery 🜂, i.e., Schamajim.</note>. Therefore, he was also able to perfectly fulfill the Divine command in Genesis 1:26: "That they may rule over the fish of the sea, and over the birds of the heavens, likewise over the cattle, yes, over the whole earth, and over every creeping thing that creeps upon the earth." And it is therefore to be concluded from the glory of this first man or **Adam**, that he could pass through and rule from **Eden** or from his Region W.X. (see Figure No. 54) down to the earthly globe of all circles and regions according to his own will; in which glory he and all his descendants would have remained perfectly, provided he had abstained from the forbidden fruit. This man **Adam** was created <term>male and female</term> <note>original Hebrew: זָכָר וּנְקֵבָה (Zachar u-Nekevah)</note>, as the Scripture says; that is, of male and female gender, not as two distinct bodies, but One in his <term>essence</term> <note>original Latin: "Essentia"</note>, yet two in <term>potential</term> <note>original Latin: "Potentia"</note>. For he was the earth **Adamah**, the red and white **Sulphur** of the spiritual **Sun** and **Moon**, the male and female seed, the **dust from the earth** from the **Heavens**, and thus had the power (just as he was Paradisiacal) to multiply himself **Magically** (if we may use this term so hated by the schools). This could not have been otherwise, for this essential masculinity and femininity would otherwise have fallen apart. Thus, he was like the angels with his diaphanous, magnificent body, passing through everywhere, and could be held back or hindered by no worldly circle, because he was the <term>essence of all essences</term> <note>original Latin: "omnium Essentiarum Essentia"</note>. But <term>according to the breath of life</term> <note>original Hebrew: נִשְׁמַת חַיִּים (Nishmat Chayyim)</note>, or the immortal spirit immediately breathed in by GOD, this Adam or man was above all angels. But before we go further here, we must speak a little more of the Garden of **Eden**.

§. 35. The text says in Genesis 2:10 that a river (the name of which the Scripture makes no mention) went out from **Eden**, which not only watered this Garden of **Eden**, but from there divided itself into four main streams: Pison, Gihon, Hiddekel, and Phrat, and that these streams flowed around certain lands or regions. These words are now the machinery from the storehouse of the schools, with which these principles are intended to be overturned; however, because the truth is spiritual, it fears this coarse materiality not in the least. We therefore proceed without dread and say first: that the fundamental source of the river which rises in **Eden** and waters this garden is the glory and majesty of GOD Himself in the **Infinite** <note>original Hebrew: אֵין סוֹף (Ein Sof)</note> or the eternal, unfathomable depth, which reveals itself in the temple of the fullness of His divinity in **YHWH** <note>original Hebrew: יהוה</note>, the *Fiat* or the Word, in the spiritual **Adam** or heavenly man, the eternally begotten Son of GOD, Christ JESUS in a. (see Diagram No. 53), to all heavenly and entirely blessed creatures, and pours itself through a. b. to
