# Of the Fall of Lucifer etc.

to the seven great spirits, <note>original: "Apoc. I. v. 4."</note> *Revelation 1:4*, who are before the Throne of His Glory, between whom walks the Alpha and Omega, the Beginning and the End. From these great spirits, according to their revolution, this Source of Life pours out from **b.** into **d.** into the Angelic World or Circles—namely from **d.** into **e.**, from there into **f.**, up to **g. h. i. k. l. m. n. o. p.** and back again into **d.**, and thus through all the powers of the Angelic World. (See the expression of these powers in the Hebrew letters on Diagram No. 53 <note>Welling refers here to a complex "Schema" or mystical diagram intended to map the spiritual spheres.</note>, from **d. e. f. g. h. i. k. l. m. n. o. p.** into **d.** as if into **Eden**.) It is there that the primal source rises and becomes the river of which the Text says that it waters this garden. From there, it divides into the four principal streams of Fire, Air, Water, and Earth, within the **Machine B**. In this **Machine**, the outflow of life and the sustenance of all creatures—through the stars, as well as the Sun and Moon—pours unceasingly into our Earth-sphere as its Center (upon which the eternal Will and Purpose, as well as the Mysteries of Divine Majesty, have been revealed before all other circles, both of this earthly and the spiritual world). There it works, as has been said extensively and clearly enough above in the first and following chapters, and requires no repetition here.

However, it will be objected against us that the Text nevertheless says these named principal streams from the river of **Eden** flow around certain lands: such as the **Pison** <note>original: "Pischon"</note> flowing around the land of **Havilah** <note>original: "Hevila"</note> (which in their dreams must be the beautiful, gold-rich <term>Sun-land</term> <note>original: "☉ reiche"</note> of India, or some other such territory); the **Gihon** <note>original: "Giehon"</note> around the entire land of **Cush** <note>original: "Chus"</note> (which must be their land of the Moors); the **Hiddekel** around **Assyria**; and the **Phrat** (the Euphrates) going on in this manner—I know not how idly or insignificantly. One must wonder at such interpretations, which often rhyme together like bacon bits crumbled into cold buttermilk. It is also a wonder that there are so many blind people who, like the blind Jews, cling so stubbornly to the outer letter. Upon this letter, through their human wit, they have laid the foundations of their wisdom, yet they do not wish to seek out the *Mysteria* or Divine Mysteries from within the wrapping of the letter. But *what is that to us?* <note>original: "quid ad nos"</note> We wish to answer their objection according to our principles established in the Truth.

At first, we might well interpret the Hebrew names of these streams according to the **Cabal** <note>The Cabala or Kabbalah, a system of Jewish mysticism used by Welling to find hidden meanings in the Bible.</note>, but because this is understood by few and respected by even fewer, we shall pass over it as an unnecessary long-windedness and speak of the matter only briefly. Firstly, then, it is named פישון (**Pischon**), and it is said that it flows around the land of חוי’לה (**Chavilah**), rich in precious gold. There also is found <term>Bdellium</term> <note>A semi-transparent oleo-gum resin.</note> and the precious stone **Onyx** <note>original: "Onychel"</note>; in Hebrew, ברלח <note>original: "Bar-lach" (likely a variant for Bedolach)</note> and שהם (**Shoham**). This is the stream or water of growth which moves all life—namely, the so-called **Fire-Element**. This carries with it the precious Gold <note>original symbol: ☉</note> and the Red Jupiter <note>original symbol: ♃; in this context, Jupiter likely refers to a specific alchemical stage or metallic quality.</note>, from which are generated the ברלח and שהם. (Regarding this gold-shining stone, the **Onyx**, whose outermost part, ברלח, is a golden luster, but whose innermost part is שהם or **Schamajim** <note>The Hebrew word for "Heavens."</note>—locked within the anxious, outer Fire-Wheel—much more could be said, if we did not fear being cried out against as a "gold-cooker" <note>A derogatory term for a fraudulent or literal-minded alchemist.</note>; therefore, it shall not be touched upon further.)

The second stream is called גיחון (**Giehon**); this has gone forth from the belly of the **Sphere of Fire** <note>original symbol: △</note> and surrounds the entire Earth-sphere, the black, dark, and corrupted soil. The third stream is the חדקל (**Chiddekel**), which is the now-cursed, thorny, and prickly earth. The fourth is called פרת (**Phrat**); these are all the waters in their entirety, which are impregnated and filled by heavenly influences, and which refresh the burnt **Cush** once again. This, then, is what we wish to answer to the above objection. This could indeed have been done much more extensively and incomparably more clearly if we had wished to go a little further into the true **Cabala**, where we would have found much more to say regarding the mystery of the numbers of these streams. However, this is not our intention at present; rather, we wish to leave something for others to reflect upon further, and so it shall remain at this.

§. 36. Now we return again to the end of the preceding §. 34, where we heard what a glorious creature the man **Adam** was: in what magnificent light and splendor, in the perfect willing of the Will of **GOD**, and thus in true holiness and righteousness, in indescribable gentle stillness, peace, and joy. In this state, he would have remained eternally, provided he had not lusted against the prohibition of his Creator, who had formed him so magnificently. When **GOD** the **LORD** created him so gloriously according to His image and likeness, and made him a ruler of this entire **Universe** <note>original: "Universi"</note> (from which **Lucifer** had fallen), and gave him **Eden** as his throne, He commanded him that he should not lust after—much less eat from—the Tree of the Knowledge of Good and Evil, under the unavoidable penalty of temporal and eternal death. On the other hand, he might freely enjoy everything else—even the Tree of Life in the midst of the garden—and rule over all creatures and creations beneath his throne. Above in this chapter, we spoke extensively of the fall of **Lucifer** and its cause, as well as where this seat of **Lucifer** was changed into this present world, and how the man **Adam** was created in **Lucifer’s** stead to rule and fill this world.

Now, when **Lucifer** saw this magnificent creature, **Adam**, as a king of this mighty **Machine** <note>original: "Machinæ"</note> from which he himself had been cast out on account of his pride, his wrath and envy were ignited against this man, desiring to pull him down from this great glory into his own ruin and expulsion. Therefore, he dispatched his deputy, the lord and prince of the air, **Beelzebub** (for **Lucifer** himself lies locked with the chains and bonds of darkness in the fiery abyss until the day of final judgment), to incite the man **Adam** against **GOD’s** will and command. He took the opportunity to approach the man because he...

### CHAPTER V.

Satan showed him alone—or rather, pointed out to him—all the kingdoms of this world, presenting to him how useless all this was if he were to remain so alone. On the other hand, if he would seek to multiply himself into countless millions, only then would he truly be able to rule over this world. He claimed Adam could accomplish this by his own power, as such a mighty king of his own property (this great Universe), according to his own will. Thus, he enticed the human being to lust after his own Great Glory within himself, in opposition to his Creator, and generated disobedience against the eternal GOD within his imagination. He strove after a species <note>original: *Specie*</note> through which he might release his power and multiplication to occupy this world by his own authority, and thus set himself against his GOD and King. Meanwhile, Satan suggested to him that as an immortal spirit, breathed out directly from GOD, he was eternally immortal and could suffer no transience or death.

Through this, sin was actually, though spiritually, conceived in Adam. However, so that the Lord GOD might destroy the Devil's envy and malice and preserve the human being from a multiplication that ran contrary to his essence and was loathsome in God's eyes—and thus from total eternal ruin—the Lord GOD said in Genesis 2:18: **It is not good that the man should be alone; I will make him a helper to be around him (namely, for his company and multiplication).** But so that the Lord GOD might show the human being that he had erred in his imagination, and that such a helper for him was not yet to be found in the animal world (which was subject to man), GOD led all living creatures of this earthly sphere to Adam in Genesis 2:19, so that he might name each according to its essential species. Thus, Adam might learn to recognize that in this entire Universe, or visible structure of the world, there was as yet no helper present for his multiplication. He realized he had imagined something terrifying against God's most holy will and purpose, which was that Adam should multiply according to the nature of his essence and his proper dwelling in Eden. As the text in Genesis 2:20 further says: **But for Adam, there was found no helper to be around him.**

Now, so that Adam—through the inflamed imagination of the animal world—might not fall entirely against his essence into a desire for his eternal ruin, the Lord GOD revealed his spiritual crime, as well as his actual sin and disobedience, through the "dividing" of Adam (who was previously two and one). As follows from the text in Genesis 2:21, it is said: therefore the Lord GOD cast a deep sleep <note>original: תרדמה, *tardemah*</note> upon Adam, so that he slept; and He took one of his ribs and closed up the flesh in its place. Verse 22: And from the rib which the Lord GOD had taken from Adam, He built a woman, and brought her to Adam. Verse 23: **Then Adam said: This is now bone of my bones, and flesh of my flesh; she shall be called Woman <note>The original German uses "Männin," a feminine form of "man," to mirror the Hebrew wordplay between *Ish* (man) and *Ishshah* (woman).</note>, because she was taken from Man.** Verse 25: **And they were both naked, Adam and his wife, and were not ashamed.**

Here now, the 21st verse is a text so full of mysteries that they are unfathomable. First, it is said that the Lord GOD cast a deep sleep upon Adam. That sleep is an effect or consequence of labor, effort, grief, anxiety, cares, and earthly nourishment of this earthly body will require no proof to any reasonable person; likewise, that Adam in his blessed and glorious state, in the heavenly, rest-filled Eden, had no need of sleep, nor had ever felt it. It is therefore an irrefutable conclusion that Adam must have already committed something in the spirit which placed him out of his first perfection into a middle state, in which he was capable of such a sleep, which is the likeness of death. (It is not unjustly claimed that this deep sleep was an image of the darkening of the soul through sin; but we leave all such matters to the theologians for their work, and we remain with the mystery wrapped in the letters of the Scripture.) The text calls this sleep *Tardemah* <note>original: תרדמה</note>, the root <note>original: *Radix*</note> of which is *Tadam* <note>original: תדם</note>. This root indicates Cabalistically <note>original: *Cabalistice*</note> that this man at that time ideally possessed *Dam* <note>original: דם, "blood"</note> and *Ruach* <note>original: רוח, "spirit" or "breath"</note>, that is, a vegetative soul <note>original: *animam vegetativam*</note>, in which the Paradisiacal Adam—the inner immortal man, with his immortal spirit breathed in directly by GOD—was enclosed.

So that God's counsel and will would not be undone by Satan’s cunning in this Adam, who was the center <note>original: *Centro*</note> of the upper and lower worlds, and so that man would not choose a helper according to the devil's prompting, GOD took pity on Adam, who lay in the very deep sleep or darkening of sin, and separated Fire <note>original: אש, *Esch*</note> and Water <note>original: מים, *Majim*</note> within him (regarding this speech, the first chapter must be well understood). The text says that while Adam lay in the very deep sleep, the Lord GOD took one of his ribs and closed up flesh in its place. What Salt <note>original symbol: 🜔</note> and its origin are, is unnecessary to repeat, as this was proven extensively and thoroughly enough in the first three chapters. However, it must be well kept in mind here, as we wish to speak of the fixed Salt <note>original symbol: 🜔</note> and of man's bones. Its origin, like all other things, is from the Heavens <note>original: *Schamajim*</note>. Man's flesh is indeed also a Salt <note>original symbol: 🜔</note>, but not equal to that from which his bones are formed; the latter is of a far greater constancy. This can be proven through the deceased as well as many other experiments: that Salt <note>original symbol: 🜔</note> is the most indestructible essence. One may look this up in the chapters above; likewise, that the actual essence <note>original: *Esse*</note> of Salt <note>original symbol: 🜔</note> is the Heavens <note>original: *Schamajim*</note>, that is, Fire-Water <note>original: *Aesch-Majim*</note>, a watery fire <note>original symbols: 🜂/🜄</note> or fiery water, in which the red and white Earth-essence <note>original symbol: ♁</note> is the true Figure <note>original: תמונה, *tammunah*</note>. From this, Adam <note>original: אדם</note>, the man in Eden, was formed in the true Rising <note>See the diagram in the Schema No. 53</note> by the Lord GOD, according to His image and likeness. # Of the Fall of Lucifer, etc.

...Adam [אדם] from the earth [אדמה] <note>Adam's name is traditionally linked to *Adamah*, meaning red earth or ground.</note>, was, before his separation through the cunning of Satan (being blinded by the man’s own great glory), and had again imagined GOD into this animal world—over which he was meant to rule according to the words of Psalm 8:6: *You have made him Lord over the works of your hands: you have put all things under his feet*. He had so imagined this that he was already, in spirit, blood [דם] and breath [רוח] <note>Hebrew *Dam* (blood) and *Ruach* (spirit/breath). The author suggests Adam's spiritual fall preceded his physical transformation.</note>, according to the image of this dark world in which all animal life resides in the blood. 

Therefore, the Lord GOD took the essence of blood [דם] from Adam (which was one rib or foundation-pillar of this divine, miraculous building of Man) and built from it a woman. He closed the other rib or foundation-pillar of Adam with flesh—that is, with an extract of the so-called elements of this earthly world, into which Adam had imagined and sunk himself through the envy and cunning of the Devil. And GOD led the woman, whom He had thus made from Adam, to the man; he immediately recognized her as an essential part of his own essence or "earth from Eden." Therefore he said: *This is now bone of my bones, and flesh of my flesh: she shall be called Woman <note>original: *Männin*. This is a specific term from the Luther Bible, a feminized version of *Mann* (Man), intended to mirror the Hebrew wordplay between *Ish* (man) and *Ishah* (woman).</note>, because she was taken out of Man.* Genesis 2:23. 

Here we are led a little way from our purpose to say: whoever has understood us well regarding blood and life, as well as the separation of Adam, will fundamentally comprehend why all Israelites were forbidden to eat any blood, and why the blood of all creatures had to be completely poured out. Some of it had to be brought into the Most Holy Place by the High Priest for atonement, and the rest poured out at the altar of burnt offering. Likewise, one may understand what is said in Genesis 2:24: *And they shall be one flesh.* In truth, if our intention were to speak of the mysteries of divine service—both of the Old and New Covenants—we would find here the material for both the ceremonial worship and the mystery of the spiritual union between Christ and His Bride. It would become a very great work indeed to truly show what external, true worship (without all human inventions) must consist of; however, the work would become too large. Lovers of wisdom will, in the meantime, find enough in this guide to contemplate further; thus, we return to our purpose.

§. 37. Now Adam had a helper from the counsel of GOD, and thus the Devil’s plan was destroyed. As they were both formed in Eden (for they were now in a middle-state, as actual sin was not yet manifest in them), GOD blessed them in Genesis 1:28 and set them to rule over everything, as may be read further there. The Lord GOD also permitted them to eat from the plants of this earth (v. 29), because their flesh was made substantial from these earthly elements; they were free to eat from all the trees of the Garden of Eden, except for one (Genesis 2:17). 

However, so that the human being (who was already corrupted through imagination) might recognize and see for himself whether he could stand before his Creator in Eden with a perfect heart in this self-sought, mixed state, GOD had forbidden him—under the inevitable punishment of temporal and eternal death—from eating of the very pleasant-looking and tempting Tree of the Knowledge of Good and Evil. And so they both walked, Adam and his wife, naked, and were not ashamed (Genesis 2:25), because conscience had not yet been awakened in them through actual disobedience and apostasy. 

Therefore, Satan now tried his art on the woman, since his first strike against the whole Adam had not succeeded according to his plan. Satan knew through his knowledge (as the former ruler of this earthly or animal world) that the animal essence of the woman was much more watery and not as fiery and constant as the man’s, and therefore easier to move. He could radiate into her all forms of desire, even to lust after herself; therefore, he approached the woman in the essence of the serpent. For Genesis 3:1 states: *And the serpent was more subtle than all the beasts of the field which the Lord GOD had made.* It spoke to the woman: *Has GOD indeed said, you shall not eat of every tree of the garden?* 

Now first comes a very difficult question: what kind of animal was the serpent? Also, was it the Devil himself, or did the Devil only speak through it, using it merely as a tool? Truly, there are more opinions on this matter than there are religions, factions, and sects; to tell the truth, this text is one of the most important and does not easily allow itself to be explained through wisdom founded on human dogmas. The text mentions Satan not even by a jot [iota], but speaks of an animal called the serpent, more cunning than all the beasts of the field. This animal, above all beasts of the field (because it seduced the human being), was cursed by the Lord GOD to go upon its belly and eat earth all the days of its life (Genesis 3:14). From this it clearly follows that this animal—because it was now to go upon its belly—must have been created differently before and must have had feet. It must have been a magnificent and beautiful animal, which the woman doted upon. That it could enter Eden is shown by the text as clear as day and needs no explanation; it is therefore well worth the question: what kind of creature was it then? From the literal letter of the Holy Scripture, this is not easily seen; what the Rabbis and others dream about it is far too tasteless to be cited here, and other opinions might not be much better. Therefore, the truth must be taken from the circumstances of this history. The text says in Genesis 3:1 that the serpent spoke to the woman: *Has GOD indeed said...* etc. These words show as clear as day that a lengthy conversation (or dispute) with the woman must have preceded the words quoted here.

### CHAPTER V.

Therefore, it is truly to be concluded that the serpent must have been a very beautiful animal, very close to the human figure, because the woman engaged with it in such a way. Indeed, this serpent must have been entirely human (though without the immortal Adamic body and spirit) as a creature identified with this animal world, and very beautifully and perfectly formed. Adam and his wife were later forced to take on its flesh and blood as their punishment. The serpent, however, was deprived of its speech, hands, and feet as its punishment, and was transfigured into such a worm (now called the serpent). The woman was thus captivated by its beauty; and because she (the woman) saw a figure resembling herself, she believed it to be of her own species, and so she was led astray by Satan. For the text must again prove that Satan spoke through this serpent and deceived Eve, where it stands in the previously cited verse: *and the serpent was wiser* (<term>shrewd</term> <note>original Hebrew: ערום, *Arum*. The author argues this word implies intellectual experience rather than mere animal instinct.</note>). This word does not simply mean an animal-like deviousness or cunning such as a monkey, fox, or similar animals possess; rather, the actual and essential meaning of the Hebrew word refers to one who is very practiced and craftily experienced in things that have happened before. This cannot truly be said of any animal creature, but indicates that it was a rational creature endowed with an immortal spirit. Here, aside from the angelic creatures, none possessed an immortal spirit except Satan with all his legions, and after them came Man. That this <term>shrewdness</term> is not spoken of a human creature is given by the interpretation—namely, one very practiced in things—for Man was still too young for this; therefore, it could be none other than the crafty and devious Satan, as the text in Genesis 3:15 further confirms: *Moreover, I will put enmity*, etc. We shall let the matter rest at this point without further elaborateness, although much more could be brought forward for a greater and clearer explanation, which is not done now for certain reasons.

As for the further conversation of the serpent with the woman, and how and by what means it outwitted her: Satan attacked again at the point where he had left off with Adam. Specifically, he presented to her her own perfect beauty and glory, telling her it was in her power to multiply herself into countless millions. Along with this increase of her power and glory, he also presented to her the pleasure she would feel in the fulfillment of her desire in the multiplication of her own kind (for it was said above that before the Fall, his multiplication was not yet revealed to Adam, which may be looked up there again). At the same time, he suggested that they would thereby become rulers of this **Universe** <note>original Latin: *Universi*</note> without needing God’s wisdom and providence, if they would also eat of the forbidden fruit. He claimed it was nothing but an **essence** <note>original Latin: *Essenz*. In this context, a concentrated spiritual or physical substance containing the core power of a thing.</note> of all knowledge, recognition, and the fulfillment of one's will and desire, so that they would become equal to GOD in all things. He told her that GOD had forbidden them to eat it so that they would be hindered in this, lest they become equal to Him in glory, power, authority, and the fulfillment of will; and that was why He had said they would die the death. Satan also showed her how she should lead Adam to this same recognition and wisdom, which she had now perceived beforehand, by enticing him to enjoy this fruit; for if Adam did not have the same recognition as she, she could by no means attain this perfection.

When the woman’s **imagination** <note>original Latin: *Imagination*. In 18th-century mystical thought, this refers to the soul's creative power to form internal images that shape external reality.</note> was thus filled, and she saw that the fruit of this tree was in itself so perfectly beautiful and lovely to look upon, and moreover made one so wise and glorious, the lust and desire within her were ignited through the **imagination** in this animal world. This became so heated that the fulfillment of her will could no longer be restrained (because her **imagination**, through the ideas of this world, sought to use them for her own benefit). Therefore, she took of the fruit and ate, and enticed Adam to enjoy it with her. Because they were both of one **essence** in one flesh, the **rays** <note>original Latin: *Radii*</note> of the woman’s **imagination** ignited Adam's still-enclosed desire for his wife. Thus his desire was stirred to seek an object in which it might sink itself and find rest, which he could now find nowhere else but in this, his wife. Once the desire had been made so restless through this enticement, it burned into an inextinguishable will for fulfillment; therefore, Adam ate of the forbidden fruit without any resistance. Thus, together with his wife, he became a partaker of the knowledge of good and evil, but in this, he also partook of death and damnation. For the animal **imagination** had now completely introduced itself into the animal formation and revealed to them what they now saw and recognized through the **essence** of knowledge (which they enjoyed in the forbidden fruit). Consequently, they were ashamed of the nakedness of this animal body and hid themselves.

Because the decreed punishment stands before GOD **in the very act** <note>original Latin: *in ipso actu*</note> of the sin committed by the creature, so too **in the very act** of Adam and his wife sinning so violently against GOD in their fall, the punishment of each occurred, as can be read one after another in Genesis 3. First, the serpent, because it served Satan's cunning, was deprived of its previous excellent beauty and turned into the loathsome earth-worm (still called the serpent today), which still harbors in its venom the **idea** <note>original Latin: *Ideam*</note> of hellish soul-poison. And just as its bite brings temporal death, so the hellish poison brings sin and eternal death. Secondly, the woman’s pride, through which she thought to be so great a ruler, was cast down, and her desire was made entirely subject to the will of her husband; and instead of the imagined pleasure in her multiplication, she was burdened with great pain and hardship. Thirdly, Adam was punished because he allowed himself to be enticed by the woman’s desire and...

It details how Satan used the "imagination" to tempt Eve with promises of divine autonomy and how Adam was subsequently drawn into the sin through their shared essence.</summary>

-> **On the Fall of Lucifer, etc.** <-

...allowed it to be ignited, and at the same time, had imagined themselves into the forbidden, haughty knowledge. Consequently, they were burdened with toil, labor, and sorrow. To ensure that such burdens would rest incessantly upon his neck, the field was cursed by God the Lord. Thus, instead of previously bringing forth nothing but perfect, healthy, beautiful, and glorious plants and fruits, it was now to constantly bear nothing but thorns and thistles—that is, unhealthy, harmful, and perishable fruits and plants. Even if such things might still appear to be healthy and nourishing, man would nevertheless, through their consumption and use, promote his death rather than his life. For God the Almighty, through the curse, turned all the first-created simplicity and purity inward within every creature; that is, He concentrated the light-essence within them and enclosed it with dark tangibility. At the same time, He awakened in every creature of this universe a **gas**, **fermentation**, or **ferment** <note>Welling uses these terms to describe a restless internal energy or "spirit of life" that drives change and decay in matter.</note> through the awakened spiritual and material opposition of light and darkness, heat and cold, moisture and dryness. This ferment is now constantly active and at work throughout all of nature, until it resolves all mixed bodies back into their first material beginnings. This process is what we call passing away, death, and decay, through which everything earthly and damnable in this world (which fell into darkness through the curse) is completely destroyed. This happens so that every creature might once again come forth in its first light-essence—the state in which it stood before the curse—and especially so that in man, his inner, hidden, first man (as Adam was created in Eden) might rise again. Thus, man would not have to suffer eternal death alongside the fallen spirits in the dark world.

However, so that man—who now knew both death and life—might not willfully turn back from the death in which he was entangled and which had been revealed in him, and return to eternal life to complete what the serpent had used to trick the woman (namely, to be like God), God the Lord spoke in **Genesis 3:22–23**: *We must see to it that he does not stretch out his hand and also take from the tree of life, and eat and live forever.* Therefore, God the Lord cast him out of the Garden of Eden to till the earth from which he was taken.

Here the text clearly shows that Adam was driven away from the "earth" out of which his inner, immortal body had been created, and was placed upon another "earth" from which his perishable flesh was taken. This was done so that man might recognize that he could not stand before God in his perishable flesh. Therefore, God the Lord took away the hypocritical covering of fig leaves under which man sought to hide himself, and clothed him with skins. This was to show them that he could only stand before God with the coat of righteousness of the spotless Lamb, who would be slaughtered and sacrificed for him and for the whole world on the Cross. For this reason, the thoughts of those who say that such coats of skins were lambskins are quite well-founded. 

But so that man, in his expulsion from the face of God, might not utterly despair, God the Lord announced to him in **Genesis 3:15** the eternal redemption through the seed of the woman, Jesus Christ. This was so that he might remain safe in this comfort—as if in an invincible fortress—against the wiles of the Devil and be liberated from eternal death. Thus, man was driven onto this soil from which his earthly flesh was taken, and he could not return to the Tree of Life to eat of it and live forever, because God the Lord had blocked the way there with the Cherubim and flaming swords. He now had to till this soil with sorrow and toil if he wished to live at all. Furthermore, man was not yet permitted to eat flesh, nor did he need it until the second curse—the Great Flood—because a ray of the first blessing still remained in these elements. Consequently, all vegetation possessed far greater power, nourishment, and health than anything that might be found now in this world descending from Noah—even if it were the **Potable Gold** <note>original: ☉ Potabile. In alchemy, "Potable Gold" was a legendary universal medicine believed to grant longevity and health.</note> itself, hatched from so many strange dreams, of which we have already spoken above.

Thus, the Six Days' Work, or the creation of this machine or world-structure with all the creatures contained within it, was completed, as the Diagram No. 52 clearly shows. This chapter could therefore be ended, were it not necessary to add the following section. Certainly, much could be brought forward regarding this Sixth Day's Work, but because most of it would be pure theology, we wish to leave such matters to those who believe that practicing theology is their own particular profession. Concerning the invisible elementary creatures, as well as the nature of man's immortal spirit, there would still be much to say regarding this Day's Work. However, regarding the former, we shall (for certain reasons) leave it at what has already been said above in sections 12 through 22. Concerning the immortal spirit directly breathed in by God, we have already stated our opinion clearly enough at the end of section 21, so that it is not necessary to speak further of it. 

Nevertheless, we must still object to the opposing party on this point: namely, if they believe the immortal spirit (or the soul, as they prefer) comes into man **by transmission** <note>original: "per traducem," referring to Traducianism, the belief that the soul is generated by the parents along with the body.</note> and that it is hidden in the sperm, then where do all those souls go that are spilled through disordered lust in the sperm? Also, could a murder be committed against any creature other than man by reason of its immortal spirit? For otherwise, all who kill irrational creatures would also be murderers. Now consider...

### CHAPTER V.

...namely <note>This connects to the "catchword" from the previous page, a common printing practice where the first word of the next page is printed at the bottom of the current one.</note> one can further see, without our further explanation, the consequence and the most absurd conclusions to be drawn from the opposing view. For it is truly a fact that the Almighty **GOD** has, from eternity, firmly decreed the number of humans—namely, how many there shall be from Adam until the very last human at the end of this earthly operation—so that there can be neither more nor fewer of them. Since this **thesis** <note>original: "Thesis"</note> is correct—as it is indeed indisputable—the following **absurdities** <note>original: "Absurda"</note> arise from the opponent's teachings: that the eternal decree of **GOD** might be hindered by human malice, and that the soul is propagated by **traduction** <note>original: "per Traducem"; a theological theory suggesting the soul is passed from parents to children through biological birth, like one candle lighting another.</note>, such that man becomes an accidental being dependent on the will of a man—whether he wills it or not—that a human being should come into existence through his **act** <note>original: "Actum"</note>. We could support our **thesis** through even clearer and, as it were, tangible **arguments** <note>original: "Argumenta"</note>, but we refrain from doing so, partly to avoid causing a **scandal** <note>original: "scandalum"</note>, and partly for another important reason. Thus, we leave it at our explanation above in § 21 and hasten to the end of this long chapter.

§. 38. Once this section is concluded, it will be necessary for us to answer several questions posed by the wise, as well as by the inquisitive and the foolish. First, it might be asked: What kind of trees were they actually, namely the **Tree of Life** and the **Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil**? Second, at what time did the Fall of Man occur? Third, what is the path to the Tree of Life in the Garden of Eden? Fourth, who are the **Cherubim** who guard the Garden of Eden, and what kind of swords do they have? These are truly questions that require more than mere imagination to answer. The Holy Text speaks of this in such a way that the matter cannot be easily resolved from the literal word alone, unless one seeks the true **mystical understanding** <note>original: "Mystischen Verstand"</note> within them. So the text says of these trees in **Genesis 2:9**: That **GOD** the **LORD** brought forth from the earth of the Garden of Eden all kinds of trees, (Note) also the Tree of Life in the midst of the garden, as well as the Tree of Knowledge of Good and Evil, etc. Here we see as clear as day that these two trees were not imaginary beings, but **real** trees (from the earth in Eden) and still are; also that both bore fruit. **Christ** the **LORD** also promises in **Revelation 2:7** that he will give those who overcome to eat from the **Tree of Life**, which is in the midst of the **Paradise** <note>original: "Paradieß"</note> of **GOD**. Since they are real trees, what then was the "life" in one and the "knowledge" that brought death in the other? Both are **essential** <note>original: "essentiale"</note> properties in these trees. 

We have already heard in detail above how **Lucifer** fell: namely, he lusted after his own beauty, power, and glory, and was not satisfied with all of this. Therefore, there arose within him a self-willed volition to be as wise and powerful as **GOD**—namely, to be a lord for himself, outside of and without **GOD**. But when **GOD** the **LORD** turned his pride, his self-willed desire for knowledge, and his glorious light into dark damnation, then the knowledge and recognition of good and evil was manifested in this entire creature. This was then **essentificated** <note>A term meaning to turn an abstract quality into a physical or spiritual substance.</note> by **GOD** the **LORD** in the aforementioned tree and further passed into its fruit. Man, through the devil's cunning incitement and against the prohibition of **GOD**, partook of this fruit and thus manifested within himself the Satanic desires, as well as death and damnation. Through this, he was plunged into his present corruption, which was discussed at length just above. So much for the Tree of Knowledge. 

Regarding the Tree of Life, the **essence of life** is substantially sunken into it, which maintains all rational creatures in imperishable, indestructible life. For **GOD** the **LORD** says in **Genesis 3:22**: "Now we must see to it that he does not stretch out his hand and take also from the **Tree of Life**, and eat and live forever." This was done so that eternal life would not be introduced into this flesh, which is condemned to corruption, and so that the knowledge of malice would not remain eternally. Therefore, man was driven out from there, and the path for him to return was blocked. It is therefore certain that this Tree of Life was also a true, substantial tree. Much could still be said about its **mystery** <note>original: "Mysterio"</note>, which would not be unpleasant to many, but this **subject matter** <note>original: "Materia"</note> would again stir up the entire spiritual **hierarchy** <note>Likely referring to the established religious authorities of the time.</note> against us, who would label us heretics and condemn us according to their dogmas. And although this would not cause us any great distress, we nevertheless wish to be brief here so that this work does not extend too far. 

**GOD** the **LORD** now saw two creatures lying in corruption: first **Lucifer** with all his millions of spirits, and then man, with all his descendants still enclosed within him. Neither of these creatures, because of their terrible fall and disobedience, could reconcile the justice of **GOD**. They would have had to remain eternally separated from **GOD** and in eternal death and darkness, had not the almighty **GOD** fulfilled and revealed his eternal counsel and purpose in **Christ JESUS**. Now, all who sink themselves in deepest humility—casting off all pride and self-will—into this means of reconciliation as the source of all life (see 'a' in **Figure Number 53**), which pours itself out through 'b', 'd', and 'c' into all regions or circles of light as well as this earthly world, stirring, moving, and keeping everything alive; and those who do so with anxious and fiery desire to be in the light once more from out of the darkness, as the original state of their being...

...namely, to live eternally in **GOD** / to draw near / whose spirit / soul and inner man **Adam, dust from the ground of Eden** <note>original Hebrew: אדם עפר מן אדמת העדן (Adam afar min admat ha-Eden); refers to the biblical creation of man from the earth.</note> will truly and substantially enjoy the true Bread of Heaven, **Christ Jesus** / born in the unfathomable and unchangeable love of **GOD** / and thus live eternally united with **GOD** in His glory; and just as the substantial enjoyment of this true Bread of Heaven leads into the divine eternal life / so also does the enjoyment of the Tree of Life / in the midst of **Eden** (see the chapter in the *Schem-Hamphorasch* <note>original: "vid. c. in Schem. num. 53." Welling refers to his own commentary on the Divine Name, often called the "Schem-Hamphorasch" in Cabalistic traditions.</note> number 53) lead out of corruption and death / into the first original state / to see henceforth an eternal life without natural death.

Concerning those in the blessed Divine life, it is to be understood what is said in **Revelation 22, verse 1** / namely: that they shall be continually watered and refreshed with the pure river of the water of life / clear as crystal / proceeding out of the throne of **God** and of the Lamb. Also, they shall eat and be strengthened by the fruits of the wood / (*that is*, the trees / which stand on both sides of this river /) of which the leaves serve for the health of the **Gentiles** (*that is*, all condemned peoples <note>Welling suggests even those outside the initial grace may find healing in the spiritual restoration.</note>); when these people then enjoy these leaves / *that is*, have pressed through to **Christ** by faith and desire / they will also eat of the fruits / and drink the aforementioned stream in the eternal blessed life. And so much for the Tree of Life / and of the Knowledge of Good and Evil / and we proceed to the other object of the question: When and at what time did the Fall of **Man** occur? In **Genesis 2, verses 1 and 2** / it is written / that after the completed creation of the Heavens and the Earth / and all their host / **GOD the LORD** rested on the Seventh day from all His work / and blessed and sanctified that same Seventh day. From these words one sees / that on and after this seventh day **GOD the LORD** created nothing more / but that which was created henceforth multiplied itself through His spoken blessing; Now everyone will have sufficiently seen from all that has been handled previously in this entire work / that the cursing of this earthly creation / for the sake of man's disobedience / occurred / so that man must nourish himself with sorrow upon this earth all his life; for before man fell / this entire universe still stood in the first Divine blessing / which may be looked up in **Genesis 1** / and it had brought forth all present things in perfection and purity / without any curse / death / and destruction; the current dark, tangible state, however / which gives birth to death / was caused by man's fall. Since the entire creation was completed in the Six Days / up to the present material or tangible state / it follows from this irrefutably / that man was not only created on the sixth day / but also fell on the same day / and was cast out of **Eden**. But he whom this explanation does not please / let him search in the heathen dust of the schools <note>A disparaging reference to traditional academic or scholastic theology.</note> / with which he may satisfy his whims / as we are too few to satisfy each and every one / since **GOD the LORD** Himself earns thanks from few or none.

The third question regarding the way to the Tree of Life in the **Garden of Eden?** would perhaps not be necessary to answer / because in the entire spiritual hierarchy the location of the Garden in **Eden** / is fixed here or there upon this our earth (but was destroyed and washed away by the Deluge <note>The Great Flood of Noah.</note> along with its waters /) so the way there must surely not be unknown to them / unless the new geography does not wish to agree well with the old / and has paved the way for them to the wrong place; yet we will leave them their Paradise / and still answer a little to this question. It was previously said at length / how through the bonds of the visible and tangible elements / as firm clamps / all original light-essence is wrapped and locked up / also / how after man was surrounded with this elemental body / he could not remain in **Eden** (because he had enjoyed nothing from the Tree of Life /) since this elemental body / could not have its kind of nourishment there / thus he was driven out to this place. Therefore, there is nothing to be said of the way to the Tree of Life in **Eden** / as long as this destructive elemental body exists / but as soon as the same (namely of those / who enjoy the true Bread of Heaven / and are thereby reunited with their original state / the Divine Light and eternal life of joy /) is laid down to corruption through death / the **Adam, dust from the ground of Eden** <note>original Hebrew: אדם עפר מן אדמת העדן</note> will be unsealed and freed again (as **Saint Paul in 1 Corinthians 15, verses 36 to 50** / teaches very gloriously / and sets forth this entire mystery publicly without any covering /) and this way will be revealed to them / and no longer barred / and so much for this question. Indeed, a very important matter could have been discussed with this question / namely, concerning the different states of the deceased / as such was also initially our intention / but since the work has already grown larger under the pen than we initially determined / these and similar highly important matters must remain set aside for now / whereby the opportunity is also taken away from the adversaries / to attack us on that account / and to leave the paper untrimmed.

<header>CHAPTER VI.</header>

Let us hasten, then, to the fourth question and the end of this chapter: It is asked, who then were the Cherubim, and what kind of swords did they possess? The Cherubim are mentioned in few places in the Scriptures, yet they are described in such a way that one may indisputably conclude they had a human form <note>original Latin: "figuram humanam"</note>, and also that they are powerful angels. These are stationed toward the East to guard the Tree of Life. How many of them there were is not clearly stated according to the literal letter of the Scripture; nevertheless, from the circumstances of the Mysteries of the Old Testament <note>original Latin: "Mysteriorum Vet. Test."</note>, it can be certainly concluded that there were two of these mighty angels, doubtless with their subordinate hosts, whose weapons are the terrifying flames of the wrath of **GOD**. These now guard the way in the East (as was spoken of this above), so that from this animalistic world—before the reunification with **GOD** through **Christ** and the laying aside of the corruptible and the curse—nothing may approach the Tree of Life. But whoever is not satisfied with this answer may keep his own opinion, for this was written not to take away any man’s opinion. Before we entirely end this chapter, we ought to speak in detail according to System Number 53 concerning the true Astrology, and how, according to these principles, one may judge thoroughly and infallibly the effects of all influences <note>original: "Influentien," referring to the celestial influences believed to affect earthly matter.</note> upon these earthly bodies <note>original Latin: "irdische Corpora"</note> (among which we include each and every heavenly body). This is moved through the characters d. e. f. g. h. i. k. l. m. n. o. p. <note>These letters refer to specific points on Welling’s complex mystical diagrams found elsewhere in the work.</note> from the magnificent characters of Saturn ♄, Jupiter ♃, Mars ♂, the Sun ☉, Venus ♀, Mercury ☿, and the Moon ☽ from *a.* (see Diagram No. 53 <note>original Latin: "vid. Schem. Num. 53."</note>). However, this and much else, being uncommon and unheard-of teachings, shall remain set aside until a convenient time, and thus we close this chapter.

# CHAPTER VI.

## Concerning the Sabbath, the completion and exaltation of all times, the eternal rest and gentle quiet joy in the eternal Divine Kingdom.

-> §. 1. <-

<margin>Genesis 1, 2, and 3.</margin> In the preceding chapter, following the guidance of the Holy Text, the six days' work of creation was discussed in detail. But since we found in them nothing but labor, hardship, sorrow, suffering, pain, distress, and death, we wish to turn from there to the eternal rest and gentle, quiet soul-contentment in **GOD**, as the source of our light and life. <margin>Genesis 2, verses 1, 2, 3.</margin> It is said: "Thus the heavens and the earth were finished, with all their host. For on the seventh day **GOD** had ended His work which He had made: thereafter He rested on the seventh day from all His work which He had made."

"And **GOD** blessed the seventh day and sanctified it, because that in it He had rested from all His work, which **GOD** created and made." When **GOD** the **LORD** had completed His work through the creation of man, but man did not persist in perfection, and for that reason was cast out from the face of **GOD** out of Eden on the sixth day onto this earth, cursed through his sin, **GOD** the **LORD** rested on the seventh day from everything He had made. He thus concluded and completed a great number in Seven, and this seventh part or day He blessed and sanctified for a rest of the body and the soul, <margin>Exodus 20, verse 11.</margin> as may be seen there, so that man, after his six days of toil, labor, and the sweat of his face, might again have a refreshment for body and soul. For because man did not remain in this seventh day of holy Divine rest in Eden, he has not substantially enjoyed the incomprehensible rest and Divine blessing of the seventh day with the blessed spirits in the Divine light. However, so that his desire to become a partaker of this inner rest and Sabbath might be kindled within him, **GOD** the **LORD** gave him this seventh day or part of a time—namely, of a week—not only to rest in it from his outward hardships and labor, but also to refresh his spirit and soul again through Divine contemplation, and thus constantly have a foretaste of the eternal rest and Sabbath in **GOD** through **Christ**.

-> §. 2. <-

Namely, just as man finds his particular hardship and trouble on each day,

### CHAPTER VI.

and more in other books of Holy Scripture. Whereby we shall leave it at that, for the sake of this determination, and turn ourselves to the seventh day, the great Sabbath, the final judgment and the completion of all times.

<margin>Revelation 16:17.</margin>
§. 5. In the days when the seventh angel shall pour out his bowl, and the voice shall resound from heaven: "It is done," namely that Babylon, and all that has set itself against God and Christ, is fallen and destroyed, then the glorious day of the Lord shall dawn, namely of JESUS Christ, as the same is clearly and distinctly described in Revelation 20: First, a mighty Prince of Angels, with his heavenly hosts, shall descend from heaven and seize and bind Beelzebub, the ancient serpent, the Devil and Satan, with all his legions, and shut him in the abyss with Lucifer for a certain time (which is here called mystically <note>original: "Mystice"</note> 1,000 years), so that he can no longer seduce the nations (that is, all who do not yet walk in and with Christ). Now when Satan is thus locked away, the thrones for judgment shall be set, and the twenty-four elders shall sit upon them to hold the first judgment, where all the martyrs and those who were killed and persecuted for the sake of the testimony of Jesus, or who otherwise departed in perfect Divine conduct, shall rise and celebrate this great Sabbath (the duration of which is mystically designated as 1,000 years) with Christ their Savior.

But the other dead shall not become alive again until after the completion of this Sabbath or Kingdom of Christ; among these dead, there is still a great multitude who do not lie in total rejection, but are kept in the vessels of their purification <note>Welling suggests a middle state of cleansing for those not yet ready for the first resurrection but not entirely damned.</note> until their appointed times, of which we have already spoken in the chapter above and, to avoid prolixity, shall not repeat here, except for what will be briefly mentioned at the final judgment. Now the question is, where then shall this Kingdom of Christ be, or where shall this Sabbath be celebrated? First, it must be considered that those blessed ones who shall first rise for it have actually passed through bodily death into corruption and, therefore, according to Saint Paul’s statement in 1 Corinthians 15, shall rise with glorified bodies; therefore, it is to be concluded that they shall rule and live with Christ the Lord in Eden (otherwise called Paradise). But just as the first Adam, before his fall, had the whole world to rule, and could pass through all these spheres and come upon this earthly globe, so also these glorified bodies shall do the same, and be able to visit those who are still on this earth among the peoples of a spiritual seed, strengthening them through comfort against the approaching wrath of Satan and the stirring up of his nations against the camp of the saints, so that they may be ready with joy for the 
<margin>Luke 21:24.</margin>
glorious future of Christ JESUS at the final judgment. When this Sabbath has ended and the time of the nations <note>original: "Heyden Zeit," referring to the "times of the Gentiles."</note> has been fulfilled, Satan shall be released from his prison for a little time, so that he may incite and seduce the nations (which in the text are called Gog and Magog, <term>גוג</term> [Gog] and <term>מגוג</term> [Magog], which words, according to the true <term>Cabala</term> <note>The Kabbalah; Welling often uses "true Cabala" to refer to an esoteric, Christianized interpretation of Hebrew mysticism.</note>, mean "the people from all peoples," but it is not fitting to explain this in detail here) against the camp of the saints and the beloved city; then, however, the great slaughter of all these nations shall occur (of which we read in Ezekiel 38), and the fire of God shall fall from heaven and consume all the rest; and when this has been executed and fulfilled, then the Devil who seduced them shall also be thrown into the pool that burns with sulfur and fire, where already on the last weekday of the world, at the beginning of the Sabbath, the Beast and the false prophet, as Satan's agents, were banished and locked away for a certain time, where they shall be tormented day and night for all eternity. Of this Beast and the false prophet, who they are and why they are cast into the fiery pool before others, there would be much to say; however, a particular consideration we have regarding this matter keeps us from it for now, and so we proceed further.

§. 6. After this also is completed, the judgment over all the damned and their works shall proceed, and Christ the Victor-Prince shall appear in His glory for the general judgment, and with Him all those who were with Him in His Kingdom,

It concludes with the final rebellion of Gog and Magog and their ultimate destruction by divine fire before the general judgment.</summary>

<meta>This page continues the description of the Great Sabbath and the transition from the millennial reign of Christ to the General Judgment and the eventual restoration of all things.</meta>

# On the Sabbath Rest.

Kingdoms, having ruled over the appointed time, and celebrated the great Sabbath of rest; for they have partaken in the first resurrection, and therefore cannot die again, or taste the second death. Before the face of this powerful Judge, the heaven and the earth shall retreat, <note>original: "i. e." [id est]</note> that is, they shall lose their current form, in which they will find no place; then all the dead shall be awakened and must come forth, where then each person's conscience will be their accuser and judge, and their works shall follow them and present themselves as witnesses. Out of this great multitude, all those who have atoned in their <term>receptacles>The 'Behältnissen' or vessels mentioned earlier, referring to a middle state of purification after death.</term> through faith in Jesus Christ (of which was spoken more extensively in the preceding chapter, and thus it is unnecessary to repeat everything here) shall be moved toward their Lord and Savior; and at this general judgment, the sea, death, and the grave shall give up their dead that are within them, so that each may be rewarded according to his works. Afterward, death and the grave, as two terrifying princes of darkness, shall be cast into the fiery pool with all the damned, to feel there the second death—in separation from the Light and the Divine Face—in inconceivable torment with the devil and all his host and allies, the beast and the false prophet, as well as death and the grave, until each one's appointed times; concerning this, see further in Chapter II, § 6 and Chapter III, § 9. And when all the damned have thus flowed down to the devil into the fiery pool, the Almighty will surround and enclose this fiery pool (as well as hell and damnation) with <term>elementary palpability</term> <note>original: "Elementarischen Greiflichkeit"; Welling uses this to describe the coarse, dense matter of the fallen world that acts as a spiritual prison.</note> as a thick, dark, and impure slag, so that no one may come out of this terrifying dungeon before his appointed <term>term</term> <note>original: "Termino"; a fixed legal or providential end-date.</note>. For just as the elect, according to their works, surpass one another in clarity and glory, and yet all shall be perfectly satisfied; even so shall the damned, according to the magnitude of their devilish works, have to endure more torment and suffering than others, which is to be signified somewhat by many <term>spheres</term> <note>original: "Sphæren"</note> contained within one another (a.); namely, those who are closest to the <term>center</term> <note>original: "Centro"</note> (as Lucifer's seat, where the point of all torment and of the damned is), they shall also feel the most cruel torment. But the sphere around this vortex of the fire of wrath shall not be quite so severe, and so it shall be with the others according to their distance and removal from this center of all wrath, until the outermost sphere beneath the elementary cover or outer slag, which is the bond or clamp of all devils and the damned.

<margin>(b.) Read with all diligence what was said above in Chapter V, § 12;</margin>
What has been said here must be well-retained for what follows. When the total separation of damnation is completed, and that which Saint Paul says in 1 Corinthians 15:23 and following has occurred—that each is made alive in his own order: first, Christ the head; afterward, those who belong to Christ when He comes, namely those who ruled with Him in His kingdom and celebrated the great Sabbath of rest; afterward, those who are acquitted at the general judgment—then Christ shall rule with His elect until He has put all His enemies under His feet, and abolished all rule and all authority and power, namely in the kingdom of hell, of which the very last enemy to be abolished will be death; this is Lucifer, the highest and supreme prince of all devils. And when the times of purification are thus fulfilled, the vengeance-fire of the living God shall totally destroy all darkness and the elementary palpability that arose from it, together with the image of the devil, and everything shall come again through Jesus Christ to its first state of creation, and its first possession and inheritance; then shall be the end of all times, and the Kingdom of all Glory shall begin, and endure eternally with God. And He shall deliver this Kingdom to the Father, then also the Son himself will be made subject to Him who put all things in subjection under Him, so that God may be all in all, and that which is written in Zechariah 14:9 may be fulfilled: "At that time, the Lord shall be only one, and His name only one."

<margin>Revelation 21:1 & following.</margin>
And then shall break forth the new heaven and the new earth, for the first earth, along with the seas, has then passed away through the completion of the judgment and is no more; then the tabernacle of God shall be perfectly with men, and death (which is the last in damnation) shall then be no more; there will also be no more sorrow, nor crying, nor pain: for the former things have passed away, and now the glorious City of God, the New Jerusalem, is revealed to all creatures, as it is extensively described in the cited chapter of the Revelation; its gates shall never be closed. In this glorious city is the throne of God and of the Lamb, from whence goes out the
<margin>Note Well.</margin>

### CHAPTER VI. On the Sabbath Rest.

A river of living water, clear as a crystal, flows upon the square of this city, and on both sides of this river stands the **Tree of Life** <note>original: "Holz des Lebens," literally the wood of life.</note>, which brings forth twelve kinds of fruit, so that all the blessed may be eternally watered and fed; and then there will be no more accursed thing <note>original: "Verbanntes." In the context of the Book of Revelation, this refers to the removal of the curse of sin and separation from God.</note>. That is: **Hell, Death, and Damnation** shall be entirely and utterly abolished, and everything shall once again walk in **Divine Light, Rest, and Holiness**; for as long as an accursed thing exists, there is also Hell and Damnation, but when this ceases, then also—according to the words of the text—there will be no more accursed thing, but all creatures shall see the **Face of God**, and His Name shall be upon their foreheads, and there shall be no night there, and they will have no need of a candle or the light of the sun. For God the Lord will enlighten them, and they shall reign for all eternity. To which may He—who is the Beginning and the End, the faithful witness, the Firstborn from the dead, and the Prince of the kings on earth—help us, and all who have placed their faith and hope therein: He who has loved us, and washed us from our sins with His blood, and has made us into kings

->and priests unto God, namely His Father;<-
->to the same be honor and power for all<-
->eternity, Amen.<-
